000000r 2               ; ***  ONBOARD SERIAL ADAPTER, 65C51  ***
000000r 2

000000r 2               
000000r 2               .if SR_SELECT = SR_2400
000000r 2               SERIAL_RATE     = 2400
000000r 2               .elseif SR_SELECT = SR_4800
000000r 2               SERIAL_RATE     = 4800
000000r 2               .elseif SR_SELECT = SR_9600
000000r 2               SERIAL_RATE     = 9600
000000r 2               .elseif SR_SELECT = SR_19200
000000r 2               SERIAL_RATE     = 19200
000000r 2               .else
000000r 2               SERIAL_RATE     = 115200
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 0
000000r 2                   .if ACIA_USE_VIA_TIMER = 0
000000r 2               SWT_INNER_LOOP_CYCLES = 5
000000r 2               BITS_PER_CHAR   = 10          ; 8 + start + stop.
000000r 2               SWT             = ((((CLK_CPS / SERIAL_RATE) + 1) * BITS_PER_CHAR) / SWT_INNER_LOOP_CYCLES) + 1
000000r 2                   .else
000000r 2               BITS_PER_CHAR   = 10          ; 8 + start + stop.
000000r 2               HWT_OVERHEAD    = 50
000000r 2               SWT             = (((CLK_CPS / SERIAL_RATE) + 1) * BITS_PER_CHAR) - HWT_OVERHEAD
000000r 2                   .endif
000000r 2               
000000r 2               SWT_SELECT_L    = SWT .MOD 256
000000r 2               SWT_SELECT_H    = SWT / 256
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; ***  END OF ONBOARD SERIAL ADAPTER  ***

000000r 2               ; write a byte in A to the IO PORT
000000r 2               .macro IO_PORT_WRITE    port, byte, imm
000000r 2               .ifnblank       imm
000000r 2                               lda     #imm
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2               .ifblank        byte
000000r 2                               sta     port
000000r 2               .else
000000r 2                               sta     IO_PORT_BYTE port, byte
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; read a byte into A from the IO PORT/Byte (PORT_N | BYTE_M)
000000r 2               .macro IO_PORT_READ     port, byte
000000r 2               .ifblank        byte
000000r 2                               lda     port
000000r 2               .else
000000r 2                               lda     IO_PORT_BYTE port, byte
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2               .endmacro

000000r 2               ; MOV

000000r 2                               sta     addr2
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro MOVA             addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               MOV     addr1, addr2
000000r 2               .endmacro

000000r 2               .macro MOVX             addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               ldx     addr1
000000r 2                               stx     addr2
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro MOVY             addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               ldy     addr1
000000r 2                               sty     addr2
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro MOV16            addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               MOV     addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               MOV     addr1 + 1, addr2 + 1
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro MOVA16           addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               MOV16   addr1, addr2
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro MOVX16           addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               MOVX    addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               MOVX    addr1 + 1, addr2 + 1
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro MOVY16           addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               MOVY    addr1, addr2

000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro MOVAX            addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               lda     addr1,X
000000r 2                               sta     addr2,X
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro MOVAY            addr1, addr2

000000r 2                               sta     addr2,Y
000000r 2               .endmacro

000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; X: # of bytes to move
000000r 2               ; Clobbers A
000000r 2               .macro BLKMOVX          addr1, addr2
000000r 2                               phy
000000r 2                               txa
000000r 2                               tay
000000r 2               :
000000r 2                               dey
000000r 2                               lda     addr1,Y
000000r 2                               sta     addr2,Y
000000r 2                               bne     :-
000000r 2                               ply
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Y: # of bytes to move
000000r 2               ; Clobbers A, Y
000000r 2               .macro BLKMOVY          addr1, addr2
000000r 2               :
000000r 2                               dey
000000r 2                               lda     addr1,Y
000000r 2                               sta     addr2,Y
000000r 2                               bne     :-
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  _M_ADDTO        addr
000000r 2                               adc     addr
000000r 2                               sta     addr
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; _M_INCC: inc and set C/V if rollover.  Clobbers .A, C
000000r 2               .macro  _M_INCC    addr
000000r 2                               sec
000000r 2                               lda     #0
000000r 2                               _M_ADDTO    {addr}
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  _M_INCC16       addr
000000r 2                               inc     addr
000000r 2                               bne     :+
000000r 2                               _M_INCC     addr + 1
000000r 2                               bra     :++
000000r 2               :
000000r 2                               lda     addr + 1
000000r 2                               ora     #1
000000r 2               :
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  _M_INCC32       addr
000000r 2                               inc     addr
000000r 2                               bne     :+
000000r 2                               inc     addr+1
000000r 2                               bne     :+
000000r 2                               _M_INCC16   addr+2
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  INC16           addr
000000r 2                               inc     addr
000000r 2                               bne     :+
000000r 2                               inc     addr + 1
000000r 2                               bra     :++
000000r 2               :
000000r 2                               lda     addr + 1
000000r 2                               ora     #1
000000r 2               :
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  INC32           addr
000000r 2                               inc     addr
000000r 2                               bne     :+
000000r 2                               inc     addr+1
000000r 2                               bne     :+
000000r 2                               INC16   addr+2
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  DEC16           addr
000000r 2                               lda     addr
000000r 2                               bne     :+
000000r 2                               dec     addr
000000r 2                               dec     addr+1
000000r 2                               bra     :+++
000000r 2               :
000000r 2                               dec     addr
000000r 2                               bne     :+
000000r 2                               lda     addr+1      ; LOB is zero, so use Z and N from HOB
000000r 2                               bra     :++
000000r 2               :
000000r 2                               lda     addr+1
000000r 2                               ora     #1          ; reset Z, if set, without affecting N
000000r 2               :
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro  DEC32           addr
000000r 2                               lda     addr
000000r 2                               bne     :+++
000000r 2                               cmp     addr+1
000000r 2                               bne     :++
000000r 2                               cmp     addr+2
000000r 2                               bne     :+
000000r 2                               dec     addr+3
000000r 2               :
000000r 2                               dec     addr+2
000000r 2               :
000000r 2                               dec     addr+1
000000r 2               :
000000r 2                               dec     addr
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; No-clobber (NC) macros to wrap another macro that overwrites one or more registers

000000r 2                               pla
000000r 2               .endmacro

000000r 2               .endmacro

000000r 2               ; SPI
000000r 2               .macro SPI_SEND_CMD b0, b1, b2, b3, b4, crc
000000r 2                               lda             #b0 | $40
000000r 2                               jsr             SPI_SEND
000000r 2                               lda             #b1
000000r 2                               jsr             SPI_TRANSCEIVE
000000r 2                               sta             ZP_TEMP_2
000000r 2                               lda             #b2
000000r 2                               jsr             SPI_TRANSCEIVE
000000r 2                               lda             #b3
000000r 2                               jsr             SPI_TRANSCEIVE
000000r 2                               lda             #b4
000000r 2                               jsr             SPI_TRANSCEIVE
000000r 2               .ifnblank       crc
000000r 2                               lda             #(crc << 1)+1
000000r 2               .else
000000r 2                               lda             #$FF
000000r 2               .endif
000000r 2                               jsr             SPI_TRANSCEIVE
000000r 2                               jsr             SPI_RECV
000000r 2                               sta             ZP_TEMP
000000r 2               .endmacro

000000r 2               .macro META_CALL    call, C1, C2, C3, C4, C5, C6, C7, C8, C9
000000r 2                   .ifblank C2
000000r 2                               LDA_CORA        {C1}
000000r 2                               jsr             call
000000r 2                               .exitmacro
000000r 2                   .else
000000r 2                               lda             C1
000000r 2                               jsr             call
000000r 2                   .endif
000000r 2                               META_CALL       {call}, {C2}, {C3}, {C4}, {C5}, {C6}, {C7}, {C8}, {C9}
000000r 2               .endmacro

000000r 2                               jmp             call
000000r 2                               .exitmacro
000000r 2                   .else
000000r 2                               lda             C1
000000r 2                               jsr             call
000000r 2                   .endif
000000r 2                               META_JMP        {call}, {C2}, {C3}, {C4}, {C5}, {C6}, {C7}, {C8}, {C9}
000000r 2               .endmacro

000000r 2               ; save a byte on branching, when small in necessary
000000r 2               .macro SKIPNEXT
000000r 2                               .byte   $22     ; Undocumented 2-byte NOP, 2 cycles; uses 1 byte to skip the next byte
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro SKIPNEXT2
000000r 2                               .byte   $DC     ; Undocumented 3-byte NOP, 4 cycles, reads absolute address IP+1, IP+2; uses 1 byte to skip two bytes
000000r 2               .endmacro

000000r 2               .macro BYTECOPY fm, to
000000r 2                               lda         fm
000000r 2                               sta         to
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro WORDCOPY fm, to
000000r 2                               BYTECOPY    {fm + 1}, {to + 1}
000000r 2                               BYTECOPY    {fm}, {to}
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro LOAD_ADDR    addr, to
000000r 2                               BYTECOPY    {#>addr}, {to + 1}
000000r 2                               BYTECOPY    {#<addr}, {to}
000000r 2               .endmacro

000000r 2               .macro MEMCP    addrFrom, addrTo, size
000000r 2                               PUSH_AY
000000r 2                               LOAD_ADDR   {addrFrom}, ZP_TEMP_VEC
000000r 2                               LOAD_ADDR   {addrTo}, ZP_TEMP_VEC2
000000r 2                               lda         #<size
000000r 2                               ldy         #>size
000000r 2                               jsr         MEM_COPY
000000r 2                               PULL_YA
000000r 2               .endmacro

000029r 2               SERIAL_INIT:
000029r 2  78                           sei
00002Ar 2  A9 1F                        lda             #$10 | SR_SELECT    ; 8-N-1
00002Cr 2  8D rr rr                     sta             ACIA_R_CTRL
00002Fr 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 1
00002Fr 2  A9 05                        lda             #ACIA_CMD_BIT_DTRL | ACIA_CMD_BIT_TLIE  ; No parity, no echo, tx & rx interrupts.
000031r 2               .else
000031r 2                               lda             #ACIA_CMD_BIT_DTRL | ACIA_CMD_BIT_TLID  ; No parity, no echo, rx interrupts.
000031r 2               .endif
000031r 2  8D rr rr                     sta             ACIA_R_CMD
000034r 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 1 .OR ACIA_USE_VIA_TIMER = 1
000034r 2  A9 00                        lda             #SER_SEND_STATUS_READY
000036r 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS
000038r 2               .endif
000038r 2  A2 06                        ldx             #IRQ_NUMBER_ONBOARD_SERIAL
00003Ar 2  A9 rr                        lda             #<SERIAL_IRQ_HANDLER
00003Cr 2  A0 rr                        ldy             #>SERIAL_IRQ_HANDLER
00003Er 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             IRQ_SET_VECTOR
000041r 2  58                           cli
000042r 2  60                           rts
000043r 2               
000043r 2               ; Input a character from the serial interface.
000043r 2               ; On return, carry flag indicates whether a key was pressed
000043r 2               ; If a key was pressed, the key value will be in the A register
000043r 2               ;
000043r 2               ; Modifies: flags, A
000043r 2               ; TODO: select the appropriate read stream for the current task
000043r 2               READ_CHAR:
000043r 2               SERIAL_READ:
000043r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             BUFFER_SIZE
000046r 2  D0 02                        bne             :+
000048r 2  18                           clc
000049r 2  60                           rts
00004Ar 2               :
00004Ar 2  DA                           phx
00004Br 2  A6 rr                        ldx             ZP_READ_PTR
00004Dr 2  BD rr rr                     lda             INPUT_BUFFER,X
000050r 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_READ_PTR
000052r 2  FA                           plx
000053r 2  20 rr rr                     PRINT_CHAR                                  ; echo
000056r 2  38                           sec
000057r 2  60                           rts
000058r 2               
000058r 2               ; Write decimal value of .A to output
000058r 2               WRITE_DEC:
000058r 2  C9 00                        cmp             #0
00005Ar 2  10 18                        bpl             WRITE_DEC_U
00005Cr 2  48                           pha
00005Dr 2  A9 2D 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #ASCII_MINUS
000061r 2  rr           
000062r 2  68                           pla
000063r 2  C9 80                        cmp             #$80                        ; special case for -128
000065r 2  D0 0A                        bne             :+
000067r 2  A9 31 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #ASCII_1
00006Br 2  rr           
00006Cr 2  A9 28 4C rr                  PRINT_BYTE_JMP  #$28
000070r 2  rr           
000071r 2               :
000071r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             NEGATE
000074r 2               
000074r 2               WRITE_DEC_U:
000074r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             MOD_10
000077r 2  E0 00                        cpx             #0
000079r 2  F0 1B                        beq             :++++
00007Br 2  5A                           phy
00007Cr 2  A8                           tay
00007Dr 2  8A                           txa
00007Er 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             MOD_10
000081r 2  E0 00                        cpx             #0
000083r 2  D0 06                        bne             :+
000085r 2  C9 00                        cmp             #0
000087r 2  D0 08                        bne             :++
000089r 2  80 09                        bra             :+++
00008Br 2               :
00008Br 2  48                           pha
00008Cr 2  8A                           txa
00008Dr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             WRITE_HEX
000090r 2  68                           pla
000091r 2               :
000091r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             WRITE_HEX
000094r 2               :
000094r 2  98                           tya
000095r 2  7A                           ply
000096r 2               :
000096r 2  4C rr rr                     jmp             WRITE_HEX
000099r 2               
000099r 2               WRITE_BYTE_MIN:
000099r 2  C9 10                        cmp             #$10
00009Br 2  90 0B                        bcc             WRITE_HEX
00009Dr 2               
00009Dr 2               WRITE_BYTE:
00009Dr 2  48                           pha                                         ; Save A for LSD.
00009Er 2  4A                           lsr
00009Fr 2  4A                           lsr
0000A0r 2  4A                           lsr
0000A1r 2  4A                           lsr                                         ; MSD to LSD position.
0000A2r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             WRITE_HEX                   ; Output hex digit.
0000A5r 2  68                           pla                                         ; Restore A.
0000A6r 2               
0000A6r 2               WRITE_HEX_MASK:
0000A6r 2  29 0F                        and             #$0F                        ; Mask LSD for hex print.
0000A8r 2               
0000A8r 2               WRITE_HEX:
0000A8r 2  DA                           phx
0000A9r 2  AA                           tax
0000AAr 2  BD rr rr                     lda             HEX_MAP,x
0000ADr 2  22                           SKIPNEXT
0000AEr 2               
0000AEr 2               ; Output a character (from the A register) to the serial interface.
0000AEr 2               ;
0000AEr 2               ; Modifies: flags
0000AEr 2               ; TODO: select appropriate output stream for the given task
0000AEr 2               WRITE_CHAR:
0000AEr 2               SERIAL_WRITE:
0000AEr 2  DA                           phx
0000AFr 2               
0000AFr 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 0
0000AFr 2                               phy
0000AFr 2               .endif
0000AFr 2               WRITE_DELAY:
0000AFr 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 1 .OR ACIA_USE_VIA_TIMER = 1
0000AFr 2  A6 rr                        ldx             ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS
0000B1r 2  E0 00                        cpx             #SER_SEND_STATUS_READY
0000B3r 2  F0 03                        beq             :+
0000B5r 2  CB                           wai                                         ; Leave this in, even if RDY has a pull-up
0000B6r 2  80 F7                        bra             WRITE_DELAY
0000B8r 2               :
0000B8r 2               .endif
0000B8r 2  8D rr rr                     IO_PORT_WRITE   ACIA_R_DATA
0000BBr 2               
0000BBr 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 1 .OR ACIA_USE_VIA_TIMER = 1
0000BBr 2  A2 01                        ldx             #SER_SEND_STATUS_BUSY
0000BDr 2  86 rr                        stx             ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS
0000BFr 2               .endif
0000BFr 2               
0000BFr 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 0
0000BFr 2                   .if ACIA_USE_VIA_TIMER = 1
0000BFr 2                               pha
0000BFr 2                               lda             #SWT_SELECT_L
0000BFr 2                               ldy             #SWT_SELECT_H
0000BFr 2                               jsr             VIA_START_T2
0000BFr 2                               pla
0000BFr 2                   .else
0000BFr 2                               ldx             #SWT_SELECT_L + 1
0000BFr 2                               ldy             #SWT_SELECT_H + 1
0000BFr 2               :
0000BFr 2                               dex
0000BFr 2                               bne             :-
0000BFr 2                               dey
0000BFr 2                               bne             :-
0000BFr 2                   .endif
0000BFr 2                               ply
0000BFr 2               .endif
0000BFr 2               
0000BFr 2  FA                           plx
0000C0r 2  60                           rts
0000C1r 2               
0000C1r 2               ; Convenience method to write CR/LF to output stream
0000C1r 2               WRITE_CRLF:
0000C1r 2  A9 0D 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR_JMP  #ASCII_CR, #ASCII_LF
0000C5r 2  rr A9 0A 4C  
0000C9r 2  rr rr        
0000CBr 2               
0000CBr 2               WRITE_PROMPT:
0000CBr 2  20 rr rr                     PRINT_CRLF
0000CEr 2  A9 54 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #ASCII_T
0000D2r 2  rr           
0000D3r 2  AD F0 FF 20                  PRINT_HEX_MASK  $FFF0
0000D7r 2  rr rr        
0000D9r 2  A9 20 20 rr                  PRINT_SPACE
0000DDr 2  rr           
0000DEr 2  A5 rr 20 rr                  PRINT_BYTE      RAM_BANK_REG
0000E2r 2  rr           
0000E3r 2  C9 F0                        cmp             #$F0
0000E5r 2  90 10                        bcc             @not_shared
0000E7r 2  A9 28 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #ASCII_LPAREN
0000EBr 2  rr           
0000ECr 2  AD F1 FF 20                  PRINT_HEX_MASK  $FFF1                       ; Shared RAM sub-bank
0000F0r 2  rr rr        
0000F2r 2  A9 29 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #ASCII_RPAREN
0000F6r 2  rr           
0000F7r 2               
0000F7r 2               @not_shared:
0000F7r 2  A9 3A 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #ASCII_COLON
0000FBr 2  rr           
0000FCr 2  A5 rr 20 rr                  PRINT_BYTE      ROM_BANK_REG
000100r 2  rr           
000101r 2  A9 3E 4C rr                  PRINT_CHAR_JMP  #ASCII_GT
000105r 2  rr           
000106r 2               
000106r 2               ; .A, .Y hold the addr of HString to write
000106r 2               ; Clobbers .A, .Y; Preserves .X
000106r 2               WRITE_HSTRING:
000106r 2  DA                           phx
000107r 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_HS_TEMP
000109r 2  84 rr                        sty             ZP_HS_TEMP + 1
00010Br 2  B2 rr                        lda             (ZP_HS_TEMP)                ; Length of HString
00010Dr 2  AA                           tax
00010Er 2  A0 00                        ldy             #0
000110r 2               @write_loop:
000110r 2  C8                           iny
000111r 2  B1 rr 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      {(ZP_HS_TEMP),Y}
000115r 2  rr           
000116r 2  CA                           dex
000117r 2  D0 F7                        bne             @write_loop
000119r 2  FA                           plx
00011Ar 2  60                           rts
00011Br 2               
00011Br 2               ; Initialize the circular input buffer
00011Br 2               ; Modifies: flags, A
00011Br 2               INIT_BUFFER:
00011Br 2  A5 rr 85 rr                  MOV             ZP_READ_PTR, ZP_WRITE_PTR
00011Fr 2  60                           rts
000120r 2               
000120r 2               ; Escape sequences
000120r 2               CLEAR_SCR:
000120r 2  A9 1B 20 rr                  PRINT_ESC_SEQ #ASCII_LBRACKET, #ASCII_2, #ASCII_J
000124r 2  rr A9 5B 20  
000128r 2  rr rr A9 32  
000134r 2  A9 1B 20 rr                  PRINT_ESC_SEQ_JMP #ASCII_LBRACKET, #ASCII_0, #ASCII_SEMI, #ASCII_0, #ASCII_f
000138r 2  rr A9 5B 20  
00013Cr 2  rr rr A9 30  
000152r 2               
000152r 2               ; Set up the SPI interface registers on the VIA
000152r 2               SPI_INIT:
000152r 2  48                           pha
000153r 2  A9 00 8D rr                  IO_PORT_WRITE   VIA_R_AUX_CTRL, , 0
000157r 2  rr           
000158r 2  8D rr rr                     IO_PORT_WRITE   VIA_R_INT_ENABLE
00015Br 2  A9 FF 8D rr                  IO_PORT_WRITE   VIA_R_PER_CTRL, , $FF
00015Fr 2  rr           
000160r 2  A9 7F 8D rr                  IO_PORT_WRITE   IOR_SPI_DDR,  , SPI_DDR_BITS
000164r 2  rr           
000165r 2  A9 02 8D rr                  IO_PORT_WRITE   IOR_SPI_DATA, , SPI_BIT_CSB   ; de-select all SPI devices
000169r 2  rr           
00016Ar 2  68                           pla
00016Br 2  60                           rts
00016Cr 2               
00016Cr 2               ; Macro to remove essentially duplicate code
00016Cr 2               .macro          SPI_SEND_SETUP  mode
00016Cr 2                               sta             ZP_SPI_DATA_OUT
00016Cr 2                               phy
00016Cr 2                               txa
00016Cr 2                               ora             #SPI_BIT_MOSI
00016Cr 2                               tay
00016Cr 2                               lda             ZP_SPI_DATA_OUT
00016Cr 2                               sei
00016Cr 2               .ifblank        mode
00016Cr 2                               asl             ZP_SPI_DATA_IN
00016Cr 2               .endif
00016Cr 2                               sec
00016Cr 2                               rol
00016Cr 2               .endmacro
00016Cr 2               
00016Cr 2               ; Write and Read SPI data
00016Cr 2               ; Uses two ZP registers for data_in and data_out
00016Cr 2               ; A: data to send
00016Cr 2               ; X: device ID to send to/receive from
00016Cr 2               ; Returns input data in A
00016Cr 2               ; Modifies A, ZP_SPI_DATA_IN, ZP_SPI_DATA_OUT
00016Cr 2               SPI_TRANSCEIVE:
00016Cr 2  85 rr 5A 8A                  SPI_SEND_SETUP
000170r 2  09 04 A8 A5  
000174r 2  rr 78 06 rr  
00017Ar 2  B0 05                        bcs             @spi_send_1
00017Cr 2               @spi_send_0:
00017Cr 2  8E rr rr                     stx             IOR_SPI_DATA
00017Fr 2  80 03                        bra             @spi_send
000181r 2               @spi_send_1:
000181r 2  8C rr rr                     sty             IOR_SPI_DATA
000184r 2               @spi_send:
000184r 2  EE rr rr                     inc             IOR_SPI_DATA        ; SPI_CLK = 1
000187r 2  2C rr rr                     bit             IOR_SPI_DATA        ; MISO (bit 7) => N flag
00018Ar 2  10 02                        bpl             @spi_recv
00018Cr 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_SPI_DATA_IN      ; incoming bit was a 1 (set LSb = 1)
00018Er 2               @spi_recv:
00018Er 2  0A                           asl
00018Fr 2  F0 0A                        beq             SPI_OPERATION_DONE
000191r 2  B0 04                        bcs             @had_1
000193r 2  06 rr                        asl             ZP_SPI_DATA_IN
000195r 2  80 E5                        bra             @spi_send_0
000197r 2               @had_1:
000197r 2  06 rr                        asl             ZP_SPI_DATA_IN
000199r 2  80 E6                        bra             @spi_send_1
00019Br 2               
00019Br 2               SPI_OPERATION_DONE:
00019Br 2  A9 02                        lda             #SPI_BIT_CSB        ; de-select all SPI devices
00019Dr 2  0C rr rr                     tsb             IOR_SPI_DATA
0001A0r 2  7A                           ply
0001A1r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_SPI_DATA_IN      ; load the input for return in A
0001A3r 2  58                           cli
0001A4r 2  60                           rts
0001A5r 2               
0001A5r 2               ; Write SPI data
0001A5r 2               ; Uses two ZP registers for data_in and data_out
0001A5r 2               ; A: data to send
0001A5r 2               ; X: device ID to send to
0001A5r 2               ; Modifies A, ZP_SPI_DATA_OUT
0001A5r 2               SPI_SEND:
0001A5r 2  85 rr 5A 8A                  SPI_SEND_SETUP  1
0001A9r 2  09 04 A8 A5  
0001ADr 2  rr 78 38 2A  
0001B1r 2               @send_loop:
0001B1r 2  B0 05                        bcs             @spi_send_1
0001B3r 2  8E rr rr                     stx             IOR_SPI_DATA
0001B6r 2  80 03                        bra             @spi_send
0001B8r 2               @spi_send_1:
0001B8r 2  8C rr rr                     sty             IOR_SPI_DATA
0001BBr 2               @spi_send:
0001BBr 2  EE rr rr                     inc             IOR_SPI_DATA        ; SPI_CLK = 1
0001BEr 2  0A                           asl
0001BFr 2  D0 F0                        bne             @send_loop
0001C1r 2  4C rr rr                     jmp             SPI_OPERATION_DONE
0001C4r 2               
0001C4r 2               ; Read from the SPI device
0001C4r 2               ; X: device to read from
0001C4r 2               ; Result returned in A
0001C4r 2               SPI_RECV:
0001C4r 2  5A                           phy
0001C5r 2  A0 08                        ldy             #8
0001C7r 2  8A                           txa
0001C8r 2  09 06                        ora             #SPI_BIT_MOSI | SPI_BIT_CSB
0001CAr 2  8D rr rr                     sta             IOR_SPI_DATA        ; Select the device to receive from
0001CDr 2  78                           sei
0001CEr 2               @recv_loop:
0001CEr 2  0A                           asl                                 ; Shift in 0 to LSb of result
0001CFr 2  EE rr rr                     inc             IOR_SPI_DATA
0001D2r 2  2C rr rr                     bit             IOR_SPI_DATA        ; MISO (bit 7) => N flag
0001D5r 2  10 01                        bpl             @spi_recv_2
0001D7r 2                                                                   ; Set LSb = 1
0001D7r 2  1A                           inc
0001D8r 2               @spi_recv_2:
0001D8r 2  88                           dey
0001D9r 2  D0 F3                        bne             @recv_loop
0001DBr 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_SPI_DATA_IN
0001DDr 2  4C rr rr                     jmp             SPI_OPERATION_DONE
0001E0r 2               
0001E0r 2               ; Delay for some number of cycles to ensure SPI device is ready to start working
0001E0r 2               SPI_INIT_DELAY:
0001E0r 2  48 DA 5A                     PUSH_AXY
0001E3r 2  8A                           txa                                         ; set SPI device
0001E4r 2  09 06                        ora             #SPI_BIT_CSB | SPI_BIT_MOSI ; de-select all devices
0001E6r 2  AA                           tax
0001E7r 2  09 01                        ora             #SPI_BIT_CLK
0001E9r 2  A0 50                        ldy             #SPI_INIT_DELAY_CYCLES
0001EBr 2               @loop:
0001EBr 2  8D rr rr                     sta             IOR_SPI_DATA
0001EEr 2  8E rr rr                     stx             IOR_SPI_DATA
0001F1r 2  88                           dey
0001F2r 2  D0 F7                        bne             @loop
0001F4r 2  7A FA 68                     PULL_YXA
0001F7r 2  60                           rts
0001F8r 2               
0001F8r 2               ; Return (in A) the number of unread bytes in the circular input buffer as an unsigned byte
0001F8r 2               ; Modifies: flags, A
0001F8r 2               BUFFER_SIZE:
0001F8r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WRITE_PTR
0001FAr 2  38                           sec
0001FBr 2  E5 rr                        sbc             ZP_READ_PTR
0001FDr 2  60                           rts
0001FEr 2               
0001FEr 2               ; Maskable interrupt request handler; by default, do nothing
0001FEr 2               IRQ_HANDLER:
0001FEr 2  40                           rti
0001FFr 2               
0001FFr 2               
0001FFr 2               SERIAL_IRQ_HANDLER:
0001FFr 2  48                           pha
000200r 2               
000200r 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 1
000200r 2  A9 10                        lda             #ACIA_STATUS_BIT_TDRE
000202r 2               .endif
000202r 2               
000202r 2  2C rr rr                     bit             ACIA_R_STATUS
000205r 2  10 19                        bpl             @int_done 	            ; bit 7 not set, so not ACIA IRQ
000207r 2               
000207r 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 1
000207r 2  F0 0B                        beq             @do_recv                ; if not Tx, then must be Rx
000209r 2  A9 00                        lda             #SER_SEND_STATUS_READY
00020Br 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS
00020Dr 2               
00020Dr 2               @check_recv:
00020Dr 2  A9 08                        lda             #ACIA_STATUS_BIT_RDRF   ; is read register full?
00020Fr 2  2C rr rr                     bit             ACIA_R_STATUS
000212r 2  F0 0C                        beq             @int_done
000214r 2               .endif
000214r 2               
000214r 2               @do_recv:
000214r 2  AD rr rr                     IO_PORT_READ    ACIA_R_DATA
000217r 2  DA                           phx
000218r 2  A6 rr                        ldx             ZP_WRITE_PTR
00021Ar 2  9D rr rr                     sta             INPUT_BUFFER,X
00021Dr 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_WRITE_PTR
00021Fr 2  FA                           plx
000220r 2               
000220r 2               @int_done:
000220r 2  68                           pla
000221r 2  40                           rti
000222r 2               
000222r 2               ;; *****************************************************************
000222r 2               .if 0
000222r 2               ; I2C
000222r 2               
000222r 2               I2C_SCL = $01
000222r 2               I2C_SDA = $02
000222r 2               I2C_CTRL_PORT = VIA_R_PORTA
000222r 2               I2C_DATA_PORT = VIA_R_DDRA
000222r 2               
000222r 2               .macro I2C_ON       val
000222r 2                           tay
000222r 2                           lda     #val
000222r 2                           ora     I2C_DATA_PORT
000222r 2                           sta     I2C_DATA_PORT
000222r 2                           tya
000222r 2               .endmacro
000222r 2               
000222r 2               .macro I2C_OFF      val
000222r 2                           tay
000222r 2                           lda     #~val
000222r 2                           and     I2C_DATA_PORT
000222r 2                           sta     I2C_DATA_PORT
000222r 2                           tya
000222r 2               .endmacro
000222r 2               
000222r 2               .macro SDA_LOW
000222r 2                           I2C_OFF I2C_SDA
000222r 2               .endmacro
000222r 2               
000222r 2               .macro SCL_LOW
000222r 2                           I2C_OFF I2C_SCL
000222r 2               .endmacro
000222r 2               
000222r 2               .macro SDA_HIGH
000222r 2                           I2C_ON  I2C_SDA
000222r 2               .endmacro
000222r 2               
000222r 2               .macro SCL_HIGH
000222r 2                           I2C_ON  I2C_SDA
000222r 2               .endmacro
000222r 2               
000222r 2               .macro SCL_PULSE
000222r 2                           inc     I2C_DATA_PORT
000222r 2                           dec     I2C_DATA_PORT
000222r 2               .endmacro
000222r 2               
000222r 2               ; A: Byte to send
000222r 2               ; Return (in A): 1 = SUCCESS, 0 = FAILURE
000222r 2               I2C_SEND:
000222r 2                           ldx     #$00
000222r 2                           stx     I2C_CTRL_PORT
000222r 2                           ldx     #$09
000222r 2               @loop:
000222r 2                           dex
000222r 2                           beq     @ack
000222r 2                           rol
000222r 2                           jsr     I2C_SEND_BIT
000222r 2                           bra    @loop
000222r 2               @ack:
000222r 2                           jsr     I2C_RECV_BIT    ; ack in A, 0 = success
000222r 2                           eor     #$01            ; return 1 on success, 0 on fail
000222r 2               @end:
000222r 2                           rts
000222r 2               
000222r 2               
000222r 2               I2C_RECV:   lda     #$00
000222r 2                           sta     I2C_CTRL_PORT
000222r 2                           pha
000222r 2                           ldx     #$09
000222r 2               @loop:      dex
000222r 2                           beq     @end
000222r 2                           jsr     rec_bit
000222r 2                           ror
000222r 2                           pla
000222r 2                           rol
000222r 2                           pha
000222r 2                           jmp     @loop
000222r 2               @end:
000222r 2                           pla
000222r 2                           rts
000222r 2               
000222r 2               ; A: Bit to send
000222r 2               I2C_SEND_BIT:
000222r 2                           bcc     @send_one
000222r 2                           SDA_LOW
000222r 2                           bra    @clock_out
000222r 2               @send_one:
000222r 2                           SDA_HIGH
000222r 2               
000222r 2               @clock_out:
000222r 2                           SCL_PULSE
000222r 2                           SDA_LOW
000222r 2                           rts
000222r 2               
000222r 2               I2C_RECV_BIT:
000222r 2                           SDA_HIGH
000222r 2                           SCL_HIGH
000222r 2                           lda     I2C_CTRL_PORT
000222r 2                           and     #I2C_SDA
000222r 2                           bne     @is_one
000222r 2                           lda     #$00
000222r 2                           jmp     @end
000222r 2               @is_one:
000222r 2                           lda     #$01
000222r 2               @end:
000222r 2                           SCL_LOW
000222r 2                           SDA_LOW
000222r 2                           rts
000222r 2               
000222r 2               
000222r 2               I2C_START:
000222r 2                           SDA_LOW
000222r 2                           SCL_LOW
000222r 2                           rts
000222r 2               
000222r 2               
000222r 2               I2C_STOP:
000222r 2                           SCL_HIGH
000222r 2                           SDA_HIGH
000222r 2                           rts
000222r 2               
000222r 2               
000222r 2               I2C_ACK:
000222r 2                           pha
000222r 2                           lda     #$00
000222r 2                           jsr     I2C_SEND_BIT
000222r 2                           pla
000222r 2                           rts
000222r 2               
000222r 2               I2C_NACK:
000222r 2                           pha
000222r 2                           lda     #$01
000222r 2                           jsr     I2C_SEND_BIT
000222r 2                           pla
000222r 2                           rts
000222r 2               .endif
000222r 2               
000222r 2               VIA_T2_INT_BIT = $20
000222r 2               VIA_T1_INT_BIT = $40
000222r 2               VIA_INT_ENABLE = $80
000222r 2               
000222r 2               
000222r 2               VIA_INIT:
000222r 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA <> 1 .AND ACIA_USE_VIA_TIMER = 1
000222r 2                           pha
000222r 2                           lda     #0
000222r 2                           sta     VIA_R_AUX_CTRL
000222r 2                           pla
000222r 2               .endif
000222r 2  60                       rts
000223r 2               
000223r 2               VIA_ENABLE_T1_INT:
000223r 2  A9 40                    lda     #VIA_T1_INT_BIT
000225r 2  09 80                    ora     #VIA_INT_ENABLE
000227r 2  8D rr rr                 sta     VIA_R_INT_ENABLE
00022Ar 2  60                       rts
00022Br 2               
00022Br 2               VIA_ENABLE_T2_INT:
00022Br 2  A9 20                    lda     #VIA_T2_INT_BIT

00022Fr 2  8D rr rr                 sta     VIA_R_INT_ENABLE
000232r 2  60                       rts
000233r 2               
000233r 2               VIA_DISABLE_T1_INT:
000233r 2  48                       pha
000234r 2  A9 40                    lda     #VIA_T1_INT_BIT
000236r 2  8D rr rr                 sta     VIA_R_INT_ENABLE
000239r 2  68                       pla
00023Ar 2  60                       rts
00023Br 2

000241r 2  68                       pla
000242r 2  60                       rts
000243r 2               
000243r 2               ; .A.Y = Timer value
000243r 2               VIA_START_T2:

000246r 2  8C rr rr                 sty     VIA_R_T2C_H

00024Cr 2

00024Cr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     VIA_DISABLE_T2_INT
00024Fr 2               
00024Fr 2               ; .A = Sub-component interrupt flag to test
00024Fr 2               VIA_IS_INT:
00024Fr 2  18                       clc

000256r 2               :
000256r 2  60                       rts
000257r 2               
000257r 2               ; OUT: C = 1 if T2 timer set the IRQ, 0 if not
000257r 2               VIA_IS_T1_INT:

000259r 2  80 F4                    bra     VIA_IS_INT
00025Br 2

00025Br 2  A9 20                    lda     #VIA_T2_INT_BIT
00025Dr 2  80 F0                    bra     VIA_IS_INT
00025Fr 2               
00025Fr 2               VIA_CLEAR_T1_INT:       ; READ LOB OF T1 Counter
00025Fr 2  AD rr rr                 lda     VIA_R_T1C_L
000262r 2  60                       rts
000263r 2               
000263r 2               VIA_CLEAR_T2_INT:       ; READ LOB OF T2 Counter
000263r 2  AD rr rr                 lda     VIA_R_T2C_L
000266r 2  60                       rts
000267r 2               
000267r 2               ; ****************************************************************************
000267r 2               
000267r 2               IRQ_VECTOR_INIT:
000267r 2  78                       sei
000268r 2  38                       sec
000269r 2  48 DA 5A                 PUSH_AXY
00026Cr 2  A2 0F                    ldx     #$F
00026Er 2  A9 rr                    lda     #<SERIAL_IRQ_HANDLER
000270r 2  A0 rr                    ldy     #>SERIAL_IRQ_HANDLER
000272r 2               
000272r 2               @loop:
000272r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     IRQ_SET_VEC1
000275r 2  CA                       dex
000276r 2  10 FA                    bpl     @loop
000278r 2

00027Ar 2  A9 rr                    lda     #<SW_IRQ_HANDLER
00027Cr 2  A0 rr                    ldy     #>SW_IRQ_HANDLER
00027Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     IRQ_SET_VEC1
000281r 2

0002A1r 2  90 01                    bcc     :+

0002B2r 2               ; check which sub-device is triggering the IRQ
0002B2r 2  48                       pha
0002B3r 2  A9 20                    lda     #VIA_T2_INT_BIT
0002B5r 2  2D rr rr                 and     VIA_R_INT_FLAGS
0002B8r 2  F0 0E                    beq     :+
0002BAr 2               .if ROCKWELL_ACIA = 1 .OR ACIA_USE_VIA_TIMER = 1
0002BAr 2  A9 00                    lda     #SER_SEND_STATUS_READY
0002BCr 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_SER_SEND_STATUS     ; signals serial send buffer is ready for another byte
0002BEr 2               .endif
0002BEr 2  AD rr rr                 lda     VIA_R_T2C_L             ; clear the interrupt
0002C1r 2  A9 20                    lda     #VIA_T2_INT_BIT         ; disable the T2 interrupt
0002C3r 2  8D rr rr                 sta     VIA_R_INT_ENABLE
0002C6r 2  80 0A                    bra     :++
0002C8r 2               
0002C8r 2               :
0002C8r 2  A9 20                    lda     #VIA_T2_INT_BIT
0002CAr 2  2D rr rr                 and     VIA_R_INT_FLAGS
0002CDr 2  F0 03                    beq     :+
0002CFr 2                                                           ; Do whatever T1 timer would do
0002CFr 2  AD rr rr                 lda     VIA_R_T1C_L             ; clear the interrupt
0002D2r 2               
0002D2r 2               :
0002D2r 2  68                       pla
0002D3r 2  40                       rti
0002D4r 2

0000FAr 2               .macro VECTORS
0000FAr 2                               .word   NMI_HANDLER     ; NMI vector
0000FAr 2                               .word   RESET_ENTRY     ; RESET vector
0000FAr 2                               .word   IRQ_HANDLER     ; IRQ vector
0000FAr 2                               ;       will actually be pulled from the Vector RAM, depending on lowest priority IRQ currently triggered,
0000FAr 2                               ;       or vector for IRQ# set in V[0..3] if none are triggered.  Can trigger S/W IRQs by setting V and then
0000FAr 2                               ;       calling BRK.  S/W IRQ# is $F, so setting V[0..3] to $F and v[4..7] to a different number, you can
0000FAr 2                               ;       have up to 16 unique S/W IRQs.  You can invoke a hardware device IRQ handler in the same way
0000FAr 2               .endmacro
0000FAr 2

000338r 2               ; .A, .Y hold ADDR of LOB to increment, .X holds the length of the value in bytes
000338r 2               ; Incremets an x-byte value at addr, addr + 1 and sets Z, N properly based on HOB
000338r 2               .macro _M_ADD_BYTE_X           byteVal
000338r 2                           sta             ZP_MATH_TEMP
000338r 2                           sty             ZP_MATH_TEMP + 1
000338r 2               .ifblank    byteVal
000338r 2                           pha
000338r 2               .else
000338r 2                           lda             #byteVal
000338r 2               .endif
000338r 2                           ldy             #0
000338r 2                           dex
000338r 2                           phx
000338r 2                           bra             ADD_X_LOOP
000338r 2               .endmacro
000338r 2               
000338r 2               ADD_X_LOOP:
000338r 2  71 rr                    adc             (ZP_MATH_TEMP),Y
00033Ar 2  91 rr                    sta             (ZP_MATH_TEMP),Y
00033Cr 2  CA                       dex
00033Dr 2  30 05                    bmi             :+
00033Fr 2  A2 00                    ldx             #0
000341r 2  C8                       iny
000342r 2  B0 F4                    bcs             ADD_X_LOOP
000344r 2               :
000344r 2  7A                       ply
000345r 2  B1 rr                    lda             (ZP_MATH_TEMP),Y  ; set Z and N from HOB
000347r 2  90 02                    bcc             :+
000349r 2  09 01                    ora             #1                ; reset Z since LOB is not zero, but preserve other flags
00034Br 2               :
00034Br 2  60                       rts
00034Cr 2               
00034Cr 2               ;  Adds a byte passed on the stack to a .X-byte long integer where the LOB address is in .A, .Y
00034Cr 2               ADD_STACK_BYTE_X:
00034Cr 2  85 rr 84 rr              _M_ADD_BYTE_X
000350r 2  48 A0 00 CA  
000354r 2  DA 80 E1     
000357r 2               
000357r 2               INC_X:
000357r 2  85 rr 84 rr              _M_ADD_BYTE_X   1
00035Br 2  A9 01 A0 00  
00035Fr 2  CA DA 80 D5  
000363r 2               .macro  _M_INCX_C  xval
000363r 2                           phx
000363r 2                           ldx             #xval
000363r 2               .endmacro
000363r 2               
000363r 2               .macro  _M_INCX  xval
000363r 2                           _M_INCX_C       {xval}
000363r 2                           bra             INCX_CONTINUE
000363r 2               .endmacro
000363r 2               
000363r 2               INC_8:

000367r 2  0D           
000368r 2

00036Cr 2  08           
00036Dr 2               
00036Dr 2               INC_24:
00036Dr 2  DA A2 03 80              _M_INCX         3
000371r 2  03           
000372r 2               
000372r 2               INC_32:
000372r 2  DA A2 04                 _M_INCX_C       4
000375r 2               
000375r 2               INCX_CONTINUE:
000375r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr             INC_X
000378r 2  08                       php                             ; save the flags
000379r 2  68                       pla                             ; move them to .A for restore later
00037Ar 2  FA                       plx                             ; retrieve saved .X (which modifies N, Z)
00037Br 2  48                       pha                             ; push old flags to stack
00037Cr 2  28                       plp                             ;    and restore them to the flags register
00037Dr 2  60                       rts

000392r 2               .feature c_comments
000392r 2               /*
000392r 2               MATH_PS_ACCEPT_BYTES    := 0

000392r 2               BasePlaceValues:

000392r 2               BalanceTerneryValues:

000392r 2               
000392r 2               ; Number parsing

000392r 2               MATH_PARSE_DEC_TO_WORD:
000392r 2                           ldy             #2
000392r 2               
000392r 2               MATH_PARSE_DEC:
000392r 2                           phx
000392r 2                           lda             #10
000392r 2               
000392r 2               ; .A = initial base, .Y = size in bytes
000392r 2               MATH_PARSE_INIT:

000392r 2                           sty             ZP_MATH_PNS

000392r 2                           rts

000392r 2               ; if state = INIT, can get % or digit

000392r 2                           bcc             @base_mult

000392r 1               .include "tasks.s"
000392r 2               .debuginfo
000392r 2               .segment "TASKS"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               TASK_0_VECTOR           = $E000
000000r 2               
000000r 2               TASK_BUSY_FLAG          = 1
000000r 2               TASK_PAUSED_FLAG        = 2
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; TASK STATUS REGISTER BITS
000000r 2               ;   0: 0 = Available, 1 = In Use
000000r 2               ;   1: 0 = Active, 1 = Paused
000000r 2               ;   2: 0 = Not Waiting, 1 = Awaiting I/O
000000r 2               ;   3: 0 = , 1 =
000000r 2               ;   4: 0 = , 1 =
000000r 2               ;   5: 0 = , 1 =
000000r 2               ;   6: 0 = , 1 =
000000r 2               ;   7: 0 = , 1 =
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro SELECT_TASK      task
000000r 2                           lda     T_REGISTER
000000r 2                           and     #$F0
000000r 2                           ora     #(task & $0F)
000000r 2                           sta     T_REGISTER
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .macro SELECT_SHARED_BANK bank
000000r 2                           lda     T_REGISTER
000000r 2                           and     #$0F
000000r 2                           ora     #(bank << 4)
000000r 2                           sta     T_REGISTER
000000r 2               .endmacro
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; Initialize the tasks, their stacks, etc.
000000r 2               TASKS_INIT:
000000r 2  78                       sei                                     ; Turn off interrupts
000001r 2  AD F0 FF                 lda     T_REGISTER
000004r 2  D0 1C                    bne     @cleanup                        ; Only support tasks init when on task 0
000006r 2  A2 0F                    ldx     #MAX_TASK_NUMBER
000008r 2               
000008r 2               @loop:
000008r 2  8E F0 FF                 stx     T_REGISTER                      ; Quick switch to task X
00000Br 2  64 rr                    stz     RAM_BANK_REG
00000Dr 2  64 rr                    stz     ROM_BANK_REG
00000Fr 2  64 rr                    stz     TASK_STATUS_REG
000011r 2  64 rr                    stz     TASK_PARENT
000013r 2  A9 FF                    lda     #$FF
000015r 2  85 rr                    sta     STACK_SAVE_REG
000017r 2  A5 rr 85 rr              MOV     ZP_READ_PTR, ZP_WRITE_PTR       ; Do INIT_BUFFER, without the stack
00001Br 2  CA                       dex
00001Cr 2  10 EA                    bpl     @loop                           ; Loop back as long as X >= 0
00001Er 2  A9 01                    lda     #TASK_BUSY_FLAG
000020r 2  85 rr                    sta     TASK_STATUS_REG                 ; Mark task zero as "busy"
000022r 2               
000022r 2                           ; setup interrupt handler and interrupt timer
000022r 2               
000022r 2                           ; Will fall through when X = $FF, leaving us in Task 0, as required
000022r 2               
000022r 2               @cleanup:
000022r 2  58                       cli                                     ; Turn interrupts back on
000023r 2  60                       rts
000024r 2               
000024r 2               ;  Task switch
000024r 2               ;  Task# to switch to in A
000024r 2               SWITCH_TO:
000024r 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_A_SAVE
000026r 2  68                       pla                                     ; need to change return addr from RTS style (IP - 1) to RTI style (IP)
000027r 2  1A                       inc
000028r 2  D0 07                    bne     :+                              ; page boundary?
00002Ar 2  86 rr                    stx     ZP_X_SAVE
00002Cr 2  FA                       plx
00002Dr 2  E8                       inx
00002Er 2  DA                       phx
00002Fr 2  A6 rr                    ldx     ZP_X_SAVE
000031r 2               :
000031r 2  48                       pha
000032r 2  08                       php
000033r 2  A5 rr                    lda     ZP_A_SAVE
000035r 2               
000035r 2               SWITCH_TO_NO_PHP:
000035r 2  48 DA 5A                 PUSH_AXY
000038r 2  BA                       tsx
000039r 2  86 rr                    stx     STACK_SAVE_REG
00003Br 2               
00003Br 2               SWITCH_TO_NSS:
00003Br 2  8D F0 FF                 sta     T_REGISTER
00003Er 2  A6 rr                    ldx     STACK_SAVE_REG                  ; Restore the stack pointer
000040r 2  9A                       txs                                     ; ...
000041r 2  7A FA 68                 PULL_YXA
000044r 2  40                       rti
000045r 2               
000045r 2               ; .A.Y: Address of task entrypoint
000045r 2               SPAWN_TASK:
000045r 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_TEMP_VEC
000047r 2  84 rr                    sty     ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
000049r 2               
000049r 2               ; Find a task that is idle and start it executing at the address in ZP_TEMP_VEC && ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
000049r 2               ; Return task # in A and C == 1
000049r 2               ;   OR error in A and C == 0 (if no task available)
000049r 2               TASK_START:
000049r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     RESERVE_TASK
00004Cr 2  B0 03                    bcs     @start_task
00004Er 2  A9 F1                    lda     #ERR_NO_TASKS_AVAILABLE
000050r 2  60                       rts
000051r 2               
000051r 2               @start_task:
000051r 2  CD F0 FF                 cmp     T_REGISTER
000054r 2  D0 01                    bne     :+                              ; task is current task, so just bail out
000056r 2  60                       rts
000057r 2               
000057r 2               :
000057r 2  AC F0 FF                 ldy     T_REGISTER
00005Ar 2  8D F0 FF                 sta     T_REGISTER
00005Dr 2  84 rr                    sty     TASK_PARENT
00005Fr 2  8C F0 FF                 sty     T_REGISTER
000062r 2  A6 rr                    ldx     ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
000064r 2  A4 rr                    ldy     ZP_TEMP_VEC
000066r 2  D0 01                    bne     :+                              ; skip HOB of addr if LOB <> 0
000068r 2  CA                       dex                                     ; update entrypoint to rts-style addr-1
000069r 2               
000069r 2               :
000069r 2  88                       dey                                     ; update LOB
00006Ar 2  97 rr                    smb1    TASK_STATUS_REG                 ; mark parent task state as PAUSED
00006Cr 2  8D F0 FF                 sta     T_REGISTER                      ; do the task switch
00006Fr 2  86 rr                    stx     ZP_X_SAVE                       ; new task ZP
000071r 2  A2 FF                    ldx     #$FF                            ; Reset the stack pointer
000073r 2  9A                       txs
000074r 2  A6 rr                    ldx     ZP_X_SAVE
000076r 2  98                       tya
000077r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     @task_start
00007Ar 2               
00007Ar 2               @task_complete:
00007Ar 2  64 rr                    stz     TASK_STATUS_REG
00007Cr 2  A5 rr                    lda     TASK_PARENT
00007Er 2  A2 FF                    ldx     #$FF
000080r 2  86 rr                    stx     TASK_PARENT                     ; ...and reset the resume-to register to #$FF (invalid)
000082r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     SWITCH_TO_NSS
000085r 2               
000085r 2               @task_start:
000085r 2  17 rr                    rmb1    TASK_STATUS_REG                 ; remove the PAUSED flag
000087r 2  DA                       phx                                     ; push the start address onto the stack
000088r 2  48                       pha                                     ; ...
000089r 2  60                       rts                                     ; start executing
00008Ar 2               
00008Ar 2               
00008Ar 2               ; Find an available task
00008Ar 2               ; Modifies: A, CNZ Flags
00008Ar 2               ; Returns C = 1 AND A = TaskNumber (when found)
00008Ar 2               ; Returns C = 0 AND A = $FF        (when not found)
00008Ar 2               RESERVE_TASK:
00008Ar 2  78                       sei                                     ; Disable interrupts
00008Br 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
00008Dr 2               
00008Dr 2               ; !! NO STACK MANIPULATIONS UNTIL SWITCHING BACK TO ORIGINAL TASK !!
00008Dr 2  AC F0 FF                 ldy     T_REGISTER
000090r 2  A2 0F                    ldx     #$F                             ; Start search with Task $F
000092r 2               
000092r 2               @task_busy:
000092r 2  8E F0 FF                 stx     T_REGISTER                      ; Quick task switch to task in .X
000095r 2  0F rr 07                 bbr0    TASK_STATUS_REG, @task_found    ; Is Bit 0 (TASK_BUSY_FLAG) reset/clear?
000098r 2  CA                       dex                                     ; Not found, so check next
000099r 2  D0 F7                    bne     @task_busy                      ; Until we reach the system task (0), loop
00009Br 2  18                       clc                                     ; Not found
00009Cr 2  CA                       dex                                     ; .X == $FF
00009Dr 2  80 05                    bra     @cleanup
00009Fr 2               
00009Fr 2               @task_found:
00009Fr 2  87 rr                    smb0    TASK_STATUS_REG
0000A1r 2  97 rr                    smb1    TASK_STATUS_REG
0000A3r 2  38                       sec                                     ; Found
0000A4r 2               
0000A4r 2               @cleanup:
0000A4r 2  8A                       txa                                     ; Return the task number in A (OR $FF if not found)
0000A5r 2  8C F0 FF                 sty     T_REGISTER                      ; Switch back to the original task
0000A8r 2               
0000A8r 2               ; Back on the original task, so restore the registers
0000A8r 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
0000AAr 2  58                       cli                                     ; Re-enable interrupts
0000ABr 2  60                       rts
0000ACr 2               
0000ACr 2               ; Find the next task that is paused
0000ACr 2               ; Return task # to switch to in A.  C == 0, none found; C == 1, found
0000ACr 2               NEXT_TASK:
0000ACr 2  48 DA 5A                 PUSH_AXY
0000AFr 2  AD F0 FF                 lda     T_REGISTER
0000B2r 2  29 0F                    and     #$0F
0000B4r 2  A8                       tay
0000B5r 2               
0000B5r 2               @test_next:
0000B5r 2  1A                       inc
0000B6r 2  29 0F                    and     #$0F                            ; masking since we could have carried
0000B8r 2  85 rr                    sta     ZP_TEMP
0000BAr 2  C4 rr                    cpy     ZP_TEMP                         ; are we back where we started?
0000BCr 2  F0 08                    beq     @not_found
0000BEr 2  8D F0 FF                 sta     T_REGISTER                      ; switch to the next task
0000C1r 2  1F rr F1                 bbr1    TASK_STATUS_REG, @test_next     ; Is bit 1 clear (TASK_PAUSED_FLAG)? if so, try next task
0000C4r 2  38                       sec
0000C5r 2  22                       SKIPNEXT
0000C6r 2               
0000C6r 2               @not_found:
0000C6r 2  18                       clc
0000C7r 2               
0000C7r 2               @done:
0000C7r 2  8C F0 FF                 sty     T_REGISTER
0000CAr 2  7A FA 68                 PULL_YXA
0000CDr 2  60                       rts
0000CEr 2               
0000CEr 2               ; Non-maskable interrupt handler (same as maskable interrupt handler for now)
0000CEr 2               NMI_HANDLER:
0000CEr 2  40                       rti
0000CFr 2               
0000CFr 2  48                       pha
0000D0r 2  A9 2A 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #ASCII_STAR
0000D4r 2  rr           
0000D5r 2  68                       pla
0000D6r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr     NEXT_TASK
0000D9r 2  B0 01                    bcs     @switch
0000DBr 2  40                       rti
0000DCr 2               
0000DCr 2               @switch:
0000DCr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp     SWITCH_TO_NO_PHP
0000DFr 2

000100r 2               
000100r 2               ; 0..3 = even indexes, 4..7 = odd
000100r 2               ; x000: one byte
000100r 2               ; xxx1: two byte
000100r 2               ; xxx0 (!x000): three byte
000100r 2               
000100r 2               ;x1xx = Indexed
000100r 2               ;   x10x = Indexed by X
000100r 2               ;   x11x (!1110) = Indexed by Y
000100r 2               ;1xxx = Indirect (!100x && !1110)
000100r 2               
000100r 2               ; INDEXED SUFFIXES
000100r 2               ; 010x == ,X
000100r 2               ; 110x == ,X)
000100r 2               ; 011x == ,Y
000100r 2               ; 1111 == ),Y
000100r 2               
000100r 2               AM_ACC   := 0     ;                     %0000
000100r 2               AM_REL   := 1     ; $rr => $aaaa        %0001
000100r 2               AM_ZPREL := 2     ; $zz,$rr => $aaaa    %0010
000100r 2               AM_ZP    := 3     ; $zz                 %0011
000100r 2               AM_ABSX  := 4     ; $aaaa,X             %0100
000100r 2               AM_ZPX   := 5     ; $zz,X               %0101
000100r 2               AM_ABSY  := 6     ; $aaaa,Y             %0110
000100r 2               AM_ZPY   := 7     ; $zz,Y               %0111
000100r 2               AM_IMP   := 8     ;                     %1000
000100r 2               AM_IMM   := 9     ; #$ii                %1001
000100r 2               AM_IND   := $A    ; ($aaaa)             %1010
000100r 2               AM_ZPIND := $B    ; ($zz)               %1011
000100r 2               AM_ABSIX := $C    ; ($aaaa,X)           %1100
000100r 2               AM_ZPIX  := $D    ; ($zz,X)             %1101
000100r 2               AM_ABS   := $E    ; $aaaa               %1110
000100r 2               AM_ZPIY  := $F    ; ($zz),Y             %1111
000100r 2
