000082r 2  20 rr rr         jsr         _disasm_zeropage
000085r 2  8A               txa
000086r 2  4A               lsr                                     ; shift LOb to C
000087r 2  B0 0D            bcs         @not_zprel                  ; AM_REL = 1, AM_ZPREL = 2, so skip ZP part if LOb is 1
000089r 2  A9 2C 20 rr      PRINT_CHAR  #ASCII_COMMA
00008Dr 2  rr           
00008Er 2  A9 20 20 rr      PRINT_SPACE
000092r 2  rr           
000093r 2  20 rr rr         jsr         _disasm_zeropage
000096r 2               
000096r 2               @not_zprel:
000096r 2  A9 rr A0 rr      _M_WRITE_HSTRING HS_RelPrefix
00009Ar 2  20 rr rr     
00009Dr 2  18               clc
00009Er 2  A4 rr            ldy         ZP_D_EXBYTES
0000A0r 2  64 rr            stz         ZP_TEMP
0000A2r 2  B9 rr rr         lda         ZP_D_INST, y
0000A5r 2  10 02            bpl         @skip_ff_set
0000A7r 2  C6 rr            dec         ZP_TEMP                     ; $FF => ZP_TEMP
0000A9r 2               
0000A9r 2               @skip_ff_set:
0000A9r 2  65 rr            adc         ZP_D_XAM
0000ABr 2  AA               tax
0000ACr 2  A5 rr            lda         ZP_D_XAM + 1
0000AEr 2  65 rr            adc         ZP_TEMP
0000B0r 2  20 rr rr         PRINT_BYTE
0000B3r 2  8A               txa
0000B4r 2  20 rr rr         PRINT_BYTE
0000B7r 2  80 31            bra         @end_of_print
0000B9r 2               
0000B9r 2               @not_relative:
0000B9r 2  A5 rr            lda         ZP_D_EXBYTES
0000BBr 2  C9 01            cmp         #1
0000BDr 2  F0 05            beq         @two_byte_inst              ; inst is two bytes (vs three)?
0000BFr 2  20 rr rr         jsr         _disasm_absolute
0000C2r 2  80 03            bra         @continue_multibyte
0000C4r 2               
0000C4r 2               @two_byte_inst:
0000C4r 2  20 rr rr         jsr         _disasm_zeropage
0000C7r 2               
0000C7r 2               @continue_multibyte:
0000C7r 2  E0 0E            cpx         #AM_ABS                     ; absolute?
0000C9r 2  F0 1F            beq         @end_of_print
0000CBr 2  E0 0F            cpx         #AM_ZPIY
0000CDr 2  D0 05            bne         @not_zpiy
0000CFr 2  A9 29 20 rr      PRINT_CHAR   #ASCII_RPAREN
0000D3r 2  rr           
0000D4r 2               
0000D4r 2               @not_zpiy:
0000D4r 2  2F rr 06         bbr2        ZP_D_MODE, @not_indexed     ; indexed?
0000D7r 2  8A               txa
0000D8r 2  4A               lsr
0000D9r 2  4A               lsr                                     ; shift LOb to C
0000DAr 2  20 rr rr         jsr         _disasm_commaXY
0000DDr 2               
0000DDr 2               @not_indexed:
0000DDr 2  E0 0A            cpx         #AM_IND
0000DFr 2  30 09            bmi         @end_of_print
0000E1r 2  E0 0E            cpx         #AM_ABS
0000E3r 2  B0 05            bcs         @end_of_print               ; AM_ABS or AM_ZPIY?  Skip trailing RPAREN
0000E5r 2  A9 29 20 rr      PRINT_CHAR  #ASCII_RPAREN
0000E9r 2  rr           
0000EAr 2               
0000EAr 2               @end_of_print:
0000EAr 2  C6 rr            dec         ZP_D_ICOUNT
0000ECr 2  F0 06            beq         @done
0000EEr 2  20 rr rr         PRINT_CRLF
0000F1r 2  4C rr rr         jmp         DISASM_LOOP
0000F4r 2               
0000F4r 2               @done:
0000F4r 2  60               rts
0000F5r 2               
0000F5r 2               ;---------------------------------------
0000F5r 2               ; Helper procedures
0000F5r 2               
0000F5r 2               _disasm_load_inst:
0000F5r 2  A0 00            ldy         #0
0000F7r 2  A6 rr            ldx         ZP_D_STATE
0000F9r 2  F0 22            beq         @done                       ; if not in DISASM mode, just print one byte
0000FBr 2  B2 rr            lda         (ZP_D_XAM)
0000FDr 2  4A               lsr                                     ; /2 and shift LOb to C
0000FEr 2  90 04            bcc         @shift_mode                 ; is even bytecode?
000100r 2  29 0F            and         #$0F
000102r 2  09 80            ora         #$80
000104r 2               
000104r 2               @shift_mode:
000104r 2  4A               lsr
000105r 2  AA               tax
000106r 2  BD rr rr         lda         MN_AMODE_EVEN, x
000109r 2  90 04            bcc         @no_shift
00010Br 2  4A 4A 4A 4A      SR_N        4
00010Fr 2               
00010Fr 2               @no_shift:
00010Fr 2  29 0F            and         #$0F
000111r 2  85 rr            sta         ZP_D_MODE
000113r 2  29 07            and         #7                          ; test if bits 0..2 are zeros (one-byte inst)
000115r 2  F0 06            beq         @done
000117r 2  C8               iny
000118r 2  29 01            and         #1
00011Ar 2  D0 01            bne         @done                       ; odd modes are 2-byte inst
00011Cr 2  C8               iny
00011Dr 2               
00011Dr 2               @done:
00011Dr 2  84 rr            sty         ZP_D_EXBYTES
00011Fr 2  A0 00            ldy         #0
000121r 2               
000121r 2               :
000121r 2  B2 rr            lda         (ZP_D_XAM)
000123r 2  99 rr rr         sta         ZP_D_INST, y
000126r 2  E6 rr            inc         ZP_D_XAM
000128r 2  D0 02            bne         :+
00012Ar 2  E6 rr            inc         ZP_D_XAM + 1
00012Cr 2               
00012Cr 2               :
00012Cr 2  C4 rr            cpy         ZP_D_EXBYTES
00012Er 2  F0 03            beq         :+
000130r 2  C8               iny
000131r 2  80 EE            bra         :--
000133r 2               
000133r 2               :
000133r 2  60               rts
000134r 2               
000134r 2               ;AM_ACC   := 0     ;                     %0000
000134r 2               ;AM_REL   := 1     ; $rr => $aaaa        %0001
000134r 2               ;AM_ZPREL := 2     ; $zz,$rr => $aaaa    %0010
000134r 2               ;AM_ZP    := 3     ; $zz                 %0011
000134r 2               ;AM_ABSX  := 4     ; $aaaa,X             %0100
000134r 2               ;AM_ZPX   := 5     ; $zz,X               %0101
000134r 2               ;AM_ABSY  := 6     ; $aaaa,Y             %0110
000134r 2               ;AM_ZPY   := 7     ; $zz,Y               %0111
000134r 2               ;AM_IMP   := 8     ;                     %1000
000134r 2               ;AM_IMM   := 9     ; #$ii                %1001
000134r 2               ;AM_IND   := $A    ; ($aaaa)             %1010
000134r 2               ;AM_ZPIND := $B    ; ($zz)               %1011
000134r 2               ;AM_ABSIX := $C    ; ($aaaa,X)           %1100
000134r 2               ;AM_ZPIX  := $D    ; ($zz,X)             %1101
000134r 2               ;AM_ABS   := $E    ; $aaaa               %1110
000134r 2               ;AM_ZPIY  := $F    ; ($zz),Y             %1111
000134r 2               
000134r 2               _disasm_print_inst_bytes:
000134r 2  A0 00            ldy         #0
000136r 2  80 01            bra         :++
000138r 2               
000138r 2               :
000138r 2  C8               iny
000139r 2               
000139r 2               :
000139r 2  A9 20 20 rr      PRINT_SPACE
00013Dr 2  rr           
00013Er 2  B9 rr rr 20      PRINT_BYTE  {ZP_D_INST, y}
000142r 2  rr rr        
000144r 2  C4 rr            cpy         ZP_D_EXBYTES
000146r 2  90 F0            bcc         :--
000148r 2  60               rts
000149r 2               
000149r 2               _disasm_print_padding:
000149r 2  A5 rr            lda         ZP_D_EXBYTES
00014Br 2  C9 01            cmp         #1
00014Dr 2  F0 04            beq         :+
00014Fr 2  49 02            eor         #2
000151r 2  F0 0F            beq         :+++
000153r 2               
000153r 2               :
000153r 2  AA               tax
000154r 2               
000154r 2               :
000154r 2  A9 20 20 rr      PRINT_SPACE
000158r 2  rr           
000159r 2  20 rr rr         PRINT_CHAR
00015Cr 2  20 rr rr         PRINT_CHAR
00015Fr 2  CA               dex
000160r 2  D0 F2            bne         :-
000162r 2               
000162r 2               :
000162r 2  60               rts
000163r 2               
000163r 2               ; Carry: 1 = Y, Carry: 0 = X
000163r 2               _disasm_commaXY:
000163r 2  A9 2C 20 rr      PRINT_CHAR  #ASCII_COMMA
000167r 2  rr           
000168r 2  A9 58            lda         #ASCII_X
00016Ar 2  90 01            bcc         :+
00016Cr 2  1A               inc
00016Dr 2               
00016Dr 2               :
00016Dr 2  4C rr rr         PRINT_CHAR_JMP
000170r 2               
000170r 2               _disasm_absolute:
000170r 2  C8               iny
000171r 2  20 rr rr         jsr         _disasm_zeropage
000174r 2  88               dey
000175r 2  80 0B            bra         _disasm_zp_byte
000177r 2               
000177r 2               _disasm_immediate:
000177r 2  A9 23 20 rr      PRINT_CHAR  #ASCII_HASH
00017Br 2  rr           
00017Cr 2                   ; fall through
00017Cr 2               
00017Cr 2               _disasm_zeropage:
00017Cr 2  C8               iny
00017Dr 2  A9 24 20 rr      PRINT_CHAR  #ASCII_DOLLAR
000181r 2  rr           
000182r 2               
000182r 2               _disasm_zp_byte:
000182r 2  B9 rr rr 4C      PRINT_BYTE_JMP  {ZP_D_INST, y}
000186r 2  rr rr        
000188r 2               
000188r 1               .include "hyforth/hyforth.s"
000188r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
000188r 2               ;
000188r 2               ;  Patrick Struthers - March 2026
000188r 2               ;      - THANKS to AGSB for the starting point here....
000188r 2               ;
000188r 2               ;  The HyForth project starts here with AGSB's core Forth engine;
000188r 2               ;  the engine will be moved to ROM and the heap will be copied to RAM
000188r 2               ;  on cold start.
000188r 2               ;
000188r 2               ;  The ulitmate purpose of working this port through is to develop a
000188r 2               ;  flexible and powerful operating system for the Hydra-16 of reasonable
000188r 2               ;  efficiency and minimal memory footprint.  HyForth will grow and
000188r 2               ;  shrink in RAM footprint according to context.
000188r 2               ;
000188r 2               ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------
000188r 2               ;
000188r 2               .debuginfo
000188r 2               .setcpu "65C02"
000188r 2               
000188r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000188r 2               ; macros for dictionary, creates code as follows:
000188r 2               ;
000188r 2               ;   h_name:
000188r 2               ;   .word  link_to_previous_entry
000188r 2               ;   .byte  strlen(name) + flags
000188r 2               ;   .byte  name
000188r 2               ;   name:
000188r 2               ;
000188r 2               ; label for primitives
000188r 2               ;
000188r 2               .macro makelabel arg1, arg2
000188r 2               .ident (.concat (arg1, arg2)):
000188r 2               .endmacro
000188r 2               ;
000188r 2               ; header for primitives
000188r 2               ; the entry point for dictionary is h_~name~
000188r 2               ; the entry point for code is ~name~
000188r 2               ;
000188r 2               .macro def_word name, label, flag
000188r 2               makelabel "h_", label
000188r 2               .ident(.sprintf("H%04X", hcount + 1)):
000188r 2                 .word .ident (.sprintf ("H%04X", hcount))
000188r 2               hcount .set hcount + 1
000188r 2                 .byte .strlen(name) + flag + 0 ; nice trick !
000188r 2                 .byte name
000188r 2               makelabel "", label
000188r 2               .endmacro
000188r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000188r 2               ;  macros for PGS stuff
000188r 2               ;
000188r 2               .macro WCRLF_np                ; no push of A
000188r 2                   PRINT_CRLF
000188r 2               .endmacro
000188r 2               
000188r 2               .macro WCRLF
000188r 2                   pha
000188r 2                   WCRLF_np
000188r 2                   pla
000188r 2               .endmacro
000188r 2               
000188r 2               .macro  WSEQ_np  strlbl
000188r 2                   phy
000188r 2                   ldy #0
000188r 2               :
000188r 2                   lda strlbl, y
000188r 2                   beq :+
000188r 2                   iny
000188r 2                   PRINT_CHAR
000188r 2                   bra :-
000188r 2               :
000188r 2                   WCRLF_np
000188r 2                   ply
000188r 2               .endmacro
000188r 2               
000188r 2               
000188r 2               .macro  WSEQ_raw  strlbl
000188r 2                   phy
000188r 2                   ldy #0
000188r 2               :
000188r 2                   lda strlbl, y
000188r 2                   beq :+
000188r 2                   iny
000188r 2                   PRINT_CHAR
000188r 2                   bra :-
000188r 2               :
000188r 2                   ply
000188r 2               .endmacro
000188r 2               
000188r 2               .macro WSEQ  strlbl
000188r 2                   pha
000188r 2                   WSEQ_np  strlbl
000188r 2                   pla
000188r 2               .endmacro
000188r 2               
000188r 2               ;
000188r 2               ;   ANSI screen stuff
000188r 2               ; <rr>;<cc>f move cursor to rr,cc
000188r 2               ; <cc>m for color/attributes
000188r 2               ; 2J for clear screen
000188r 2               ; H for 'home'
000188r 2               ; <cc>[ABCD] screen moves
000188r 2               ;
000188r 2               .macro ANSI b1,b2,b3,b4,b5,b6,b7
000188r 2                   pha
000188r 2                   PRINT_ANSI_ESC_SEQ b1, b2, b3, b4, b5, b6, b7
000188r 2                   pla
000188r 2               .endmacro
000188r 2               
000188r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000188r 2               ;  for error messages
000188r 2               ;
000188r 2               .macro ERR_entry errmsg
000188r 2                   .byte <errmsg
000188r 2                   .byte >errmsg
000188r 2                   emcount .set emcount + 1
000188r 2               .endmacro
000188r 2               
000188r 2               .macro WERR errptr
000188r 2               ;.macro WERR
000188r 2                   .local werrloop, werrend
000188r 2                   WCRLF_np
000188r 2                   ldy #0
000188r 2                   lda (errptr),y
000188r 2                   sta TEMP0
000188r 2                   iny
000188r 2                   lda (errptr),y
000188r 2                   sta TEMP0+1
000188r 2                   ldy #0
000188r 2               werrloop:
000188r 2                   lda (TEMP0),y
000188r 2                   beq werrend
000188r 2                   iny
000188r 2                   PRINT_CHAR
000188r 2                   bra werrloop
000188r 2               werrend:
000188r 2                   WCRLF_np
000188r 2                   rts
000188r 2               .endmacro
000188r 2               
000188r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000188r 2               ; variables for macros
000188r 2               
000188r 2               hcount .set 0
000188r 2               emcount .set 0             ; # of error messages set up
000188r 2               
000188r 2               H0000 = 0
000188r 2               
000188r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000188r 2               ;               CONFIGURATION OPTIONS
000188r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000188r 2               
000188r 2               ; number conversion
000188r 2               numbers := 1      ; include DEC/BIN/HEX conversion
000188r 2               
000188r 2               SINGLE := 1     ; single digits hard coded?
000188r 2               
000188r 2               DEBUG := 1        ; enable inclusion of debug code
000188r 2               
000188r 2               HYWORDS := 1      ; add in additional hardcoded words / logic
000188r 2               
000188r 2               ANSIOK := 1         ; add ANSI screen stuff
000188r 2               
000188r 2               YSOUND := 1        ; add sound support
000188r 2               
000188r 2               ;TXT2STACK := 1     ; use old TXTGET instead of new
000188r 2               
000188r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000188r 2               ;              for PGS hyforth stuff
000188r 2               ;
000188r 2               ; error codes
000188r 2               ;
000188r 2               ERR_RPTR := $01    ; return stack pointer error
000188r 2               ERR_SPTR := $02    ; data stack pointer error
000188r 2               ERR_DIV0 := $03    ; divide by zero
000188r 2               ERR_MEM := $04     ; memory not-avail
000188r 2               ERR_UKW := $05     ; unknown word
000188r 2               ERR_SEC := $06     ; security error, ie dangerous address write
000188r 2               ERR_SYS := $07     ; return from system call error
000188r 2                 ; warnings
000188r 2               WRN_SEC := $86     ; security
000188r 2               WRN_MEM := $84     ; memory alloc
000188r 2               ;
000188r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000188r 2               ;                 CORE ENGINE CONSTANTS
000188r 2               ;
000188r 2               CELL = 2         ; cell size, two bytes, 16-bit
000188r 2               FLAG_IMM = 1<<7  ; immediate flag
000188r 2               FLAG_COM = 1<<6  ; compiled flag
000188r 2               MAXSTR = 100
000188r 2               
000188r 2               ; terminal input buffer, forward
000188r 2               ; getline, token, skip, scan, depends on page boundary
000188r 2               ; INBUF = $0400  (see segment STACKS below)
000188r 2               ; moves forwards
000188r 2               INBUF_end = $FD
000188r 2               
000188r 2               ; data stacks
000188r 2               ; moves backwards, push decreases before copy
000188r 2               DSEND = $7E
000188r 2               
000188r 2               ; return stack
000188r 2               ; moves backwards, push decreases before copy
000188r 2               RTEND = $FE
000188r 2               
000188r 2               ; malloc stack
000188r 2               ; moves backwards
000188r 2               MEMEND = $01FE
000188r 2               MEM_SZ = $04
000188r 2               
000188r 2               ;
000188r 2               ; spi
000188r 2               ;F9E8 SPI_INIT_DELAY
000188r 2               ;F9CC SPI_RECV
000188r 2               ;F9AD SPI_SEND
000188r 2               ;F9A3 SPI_OPERATION_DONE
000188r 2               ;F974 SPI_TRANSCEIVE
000188r 2               
000188r 2               ALTBUF = $6000
000188r 2               ALTBUF_end = $6FFF
000188r 2               MEMTOP = $8000            ; malloc allocates DOWN from MEMTOP
000188r 2               
000188r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
000188r 2               ;       Look closely at hyforth.cfg and the output of ca65/ld65 after
000188r 2               ;  a build; the RAM and ROM code is carefully arranged when the binary
000188r 2               ;  is created, to make initialization easier and optimize RAM use.
000188r 2               ;  The main program engine starts at $A000 in ROM and is only about 500 bytes
000188r 2               ;  long.  Additional functions, then initialization and debug code,
000188r 2               ;  then the dictionary follow in the binary, and these stay in ROM.
000188r 2               ;
000188r 2               ;       The dictionary has a particular structure, with 'bye' and 'abort'
000188r 2               ;  at the beginning; there are some core words,
000188r 2               ;  then contents of 'primitives.s', then 'hywords.s', followed by
000188r 2               ;  the end of the dictionary with critical items such as 'fetch', 'store',
000188r 2               ;  'immediate', 'compile, 'semis', 'exit, and ancillary stuff.
000188r 2               ;
000188r 2               ;       When $A003 is run from WozMon, a jump instruction to 'main' is
000188r 2               ;  copied to $0600, followed by the dictionary, with 'exit' at the end.
000188r 2               ;  HyForth is start by running '600R' or 'A000R'.
000188r 2               ;
000188r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
000188r 2               ;                   ZERO PAGE USAGE
000188r 2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
000188r 2               .segment "ZEROPAGE"
00003Cr 2               .org $C8
0000C8  2               ZPSTART:
0000C8  2               ;
0000C8  2               ;                   HyForth setup stuff
0000C8  2               ;
0000C8  2               TEMP0:                        ;  DUMPREG         $C8
0000C8  2  xx xx           .res 2
0000CA  2               TEMP8:                        ;  Hstring macro   $CA
0000CA  2  xx xx           .res 2
0000CC  2               TEMP9:                        ;   Imm            $CC
0000CC  2  xx xx           .res 2
0000CE  2               MEMPTR:                       ;   malloc         $CE
0000CE  2  xx xx           .res 2
0000D0  2               MEMLAST:
0000D0  2  xx xx           .res 2                      ;  malloc         $D0
0000D2  2               TIB:
0000D2  2  xx xx           .res 2          ; pointer to input buffer     $D2
0000D4  2               TIBEND:
0000D4  2  xx xx           .res 2          ; pointer to end of TIB       $D4
0000D6  2               DFLAG:
0000D6  2  xx              .res 1          ; debug flag                  $D6
0000D7  2               ERRFLAG:
0000D7  2  xx              .res 1          ; error type, 0 = none        $D7
0000D8  2               ERRPTR:
0000D8  2  xx xx           .res 2          ; ptr to mitigation/message   $D8
0000DA  2               DIGBASE:
0000DA  2  xx              .res 1          ; base for number conversion  $DA
0000DB  2               RSEED:
0000DB  2  xx xx xx xx     .res 4          ; random # seed               $DB
0000DF  2               ALFLAG:            ; autoload flag               $DF
0000DF  2  xx              .res 1
0000E0  2               ;
0000E0  2               ;                   internal Forth
0000E0  2               ;
0000E0  2  00 00        STATUS:     .word $0   ; state at lsb, last size+flag at msb   $E0
0000E2  2  00 00        CURBUF:     .word $0   ; CURBUF next free byte in TIB          $E2
0000E4  2  00 00        LASTHEAP:   .word $0   ; last link cell                        $E4
0000E6  2  00 00        NEXTHEAP:   .word $0   ; next free cell in heap dictionary     $E6
0000E8  2               ;
0000E8  2               ;                   pointer registers
0000E8  2               ;
0000E8  2  00 00        DSPTR:      .word $0   ; data stack pointer                    $E8
0000EA  2  00 00        RTPTR:      .word $0   ; return stack pointer                  $EA
0000EC  2  00 00        INSTPTR:    .word $0   ; instruction pointer                   $EC
0000EE  2  00 00        WORKREG:    .word $0   ; working register                      $EE
0000F0  2               ;
0000F0  2               ;                    TEMP1 - 4
0000F0  2               ;
0000F0  2               mainoff:               ; used for COPYTORAM, MEMCPY
0000F0  2  00 00        TEMP1:    .word $0     ; first                                 $F0
0000F2  2               endsoff:               ; used for COPYTORAM, MEMCPY
0000F2  2  00 00        TEMP2:    .word $0     ; second                                $F2
0000F4  2               ramstart:              ; used for COPYTORAM, MEMCPY
0000F4  2  00 00        TEMP3:    .word $0     ; third  (two bytes)                    $F4
0000F6  2  00 00        TEMP4:    .word $0     ; fourth  (two bytes)                   $F6
0000F8  2               ;
0000F8  2               ;          NXTTOK, BACKHEAP, TEMP5 - 7
0000F8  2               ;
0000F8  2  00 00        NXTTOK:     .word $0   ; next token in tib (INBUF)             $F8
0000FA  2  00 00        BACKHEAP:   .word $0   ; hold 'here while compile              $FA
0000FC  2               supprint:              ; suppress printing in MEMCPY
0000FC  2               FFLAG:                 ; 'find flag' for wfind entry point
0000FC  2  xx           TEMP5:   .res 1        ;    WORDS, WFIND                       $FC
0000FD  2  xx           TEMP6:   .res 1        ;    WORDS, DIGCONT, TEXTGET            $FD
0000FE  2  xx xx        TEMP7:   .res 2        ;    AUTOLOAD                           $FE
000100  2               ;
000100  2               ; *** $DO-$FF total usage in ZP, including TEMP vars ***
000100  2               ;
000100  2               ;   HYDRA-16 serial/read buffers at $200 and $300 so skip those
000100  2               ;
000100  2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
000100  2               ;                   FORTH STACKS
000100  2               ;----------------------------------------------------------------------
000100  2               .segment "BUFFERS"
000100r 2               INBUF:
000100r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 256
000104r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000108r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000200r 2               DS:                          ; data stack (S)
000200r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 126
000204r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000208r 2  xx xx xx xx  
00027Er 2  xx xx              .res 2
000280r 2               RT:                          ; return stack (R)
000280r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 126
000284r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000288r 2  xx xx xx xx  
0002FEr 2  xx xx              .res 2
000300r 2               MEMSTK:                      ; memory manager
000300r 2  xx xx xx xx        .res 510
000304r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000308r 2  xx xx xx xx  
0004FEr 2  xx xx              .res 2

00001Cr 2

00001Cr 2  A9 rr            lda #>h_exit               ; initialize HEAP pointers
00001Er 2  85 E5            sta LASTHEAP + 1
000020r 2  A9 rr            lda #<h_exit
000022r 2  85 E4            sta LASTHEAP
000024r 2               
000024r 2               ; next heap free cell
000024r 2  A9 rr            lda #>ends + 1
000026r 2  85 E7            sta NEXTHEAP + 1
000028r 2  64 E6            stz NEXTHEAP
00002Ar 2  64 D7            stz ERRFLAG                ; clear ERROR flag
00002Cr 2               
00002Cr 2               
00002Cr 2  A0 rr            ldy #>(MEMSTK+MEMEND)       ; initialize memory manager area
00002Er 2  84 CF            sty MEMPTR + 1
000030r 2  A0 rr            ldy #<(MEMSTK+MEMEND)
000032r 2  84 CE            sty MEMPTR
000034r 2  A0 00            ldy #<MEMTOP
000036r 2  84 D0            sty MEMLAST
000038r 2  A0 80            ldy #>MEMTOP
00003Ar 2  84 D1            sty MEMLAST+1
00003Cr 2               
00003Cr 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00003Cr 2               ; various reinitialization points
00003Cr 2               ;
00003Cr 2               reset:
00003Cr 2  A0 FD            ldy #INBUF_end
00003Er 2  84 D4            sty TIBEND

000042r 2  84 D2            sty TIB
000044r 2  A0 rr            ldy #>INBUF
000046r 2  84 D3            sty TIB+1
000048r 2  84 D5            sty TIBEND+1
00004Ar 2  84 E3            sty CURBUF+1
00004Cr 2  84 F9            sty NXTTOK+1
00004Er 2               
00004Er 2  A0 rr            ldy #>DS                     ; DS and RT are now half page each
000050r 2  84 E9            sty DSPTR + 1
000052r 2  A0 rr            ldy #>RT
000054r 2  84 EB            sty RTPTR + 1
000056r 2               
000056r 2  A9 01            lda #1                       ; DEBUG OFF by default

00005Cr 2               
00005Cr 2               abort:                            ; clear DS
00005Cr 2  A0 7E            ldy #<DSEND
00005Er 2  84 E8            sty DSPTR
000060r 2               
000060r 2               errrtn:                          ; return from error
000060r 2               quit:                             ; clear RT
000060r 2  A0 FE            ldy #<RTEND
000062r 2  84 EA            sty RTPTR
000064r 2               
000064r 2  20 rr rr         jsr wrterror                 ; print any error messages
000067r 2  A0 00            ldy #0          ; reset INBUF
000069r 2  A9 00            lda #0
00006Br 2  91 D2            sta (TIB),y     ; clear INBUF stuff
00006Dr 2  64 E2            stz CURBUF    ; clear cursor  (pointer into INBUF)
00006Fr 2  64 E0            stz STATUS    ; status is 'interpret' == \0
000071r 2               
000071r 2  2C               .byte $2c       ; mask next two bytes, nice trick !
000072r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000072r 2               ; the outer loop
000072r 2               
000072r 2               resolvept:
000072r 2  rr rr            .word okey
000074r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000074r 2               okey:               ; well shit, I hope this is easy....
000074r 2               resolve:           ; get a token
000074r 2  20 rr rr         jsr token      ; then just process the regular way
000077r 2               .ifdef DEBUG
000077r 2  A5 D6            lda DFLAG                 ; DEBUG
000079r 2  D0 08            bne RVPSKIP
00007Br 2  20 rr rr         WCRLF_np
00007Er 2  A9 50            lda #'P'
000080r 2  20 rr rr         PRINT_CHAR            ; DEBUG
000083r 2               .endif
000083r 2               
000083r 2               RVPSKIP:
000083r 2               
000083r 2               RESFIND:                ; load last or 'latest' word on heap
000083r 2  A5 E5            lda LASTHEAP + 1
000085r 2  85 F3            sta TEMP2 + 1
000087r 2  A5 E4            lda LASTHEAP
000089r 2  85 F2            sta TEMP2
00008Br 2               
00008Br 2               RESLOOP:              ; lsb linked list
00008Br 2  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
00008Dr 2  85 EE            sta WORKREG             ; so 'last' -> W
00008Fr 2  05 F3            ora TEMP2+1             ; only zero if both are zero
000091r 2  D0 07            bne RESEACH              ; PGS - did he forget this?
000093r 2               
000093r 2               WORDNOTFOUND:
000093r 2  A9 05            lda #ERR_UKW           ; UNKNOWN WORD error
000095r 2  85 D7            sta ERRFLAG
000097r 2  4C rr rr         jmp errrtn ; end of dictionary, no more words to search, abort
00009Ar 2               
00009Ar 2               RESEACH:                        ; msb linked list
00009Ar 2  A5 F3            lda TEMP2 + 1
00009Cr 2  85 EF            sta WORKREG + 1           ; update next link
00009Er 2               
00009Er 2  A2 EE            ldx #WORKREG
0000A0r 2  A0 F2            ldy #TEMP2
0000A2r 2  20 rr rr         jsr copyfrom                  ; W += 2, now pointing at size/flag byte from 'here'
0000A5r 2  A0 00            ldy #0              ; compare words
0000A7r 2  B1 EE            lda (WORKREG), y    ; save the flag, first byte is (size and flag)
0000A9r 2  85 E1            sta STATUS + 1
0000ABr 2                           ;; *** start of mod for bit check
0000ABr 2  29 3F            and #$3F            ; mask off flags
0000ADr 2  38               sec
0000AEr 2  F1 F8            sbc (NXTTOK), y    ; compare lengths
0000B0r 2  D0 D9            bne RESLOOP
0000B2r 2  C8               iny
0000B3r 2               ; compare chars
0000B3r 2               RESEQUAL:
0000B3r 2  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK), y
0000B5r 2  C9 20            cmp #ASCII_SPACE            ; space ends
0000B7r 2  F0 09            beq RESDONE
0000B9r 2  38               sec                 ; verify
0000BAr 2  F1 EE            sbc (WORKREG), y
0000BCr 2  0A               asl                 ; clean 7-bit ascii
0000BDr 2  D0 CC            bne RESLOOP
0000BFr 2  C8               iny                 ; get next char
0000C0r 2  D0 F1            bne RESEQUAL
0000C2r 2               
0000C2r 2               RESDONE:
0000C2r 2  98               tya                ; increment W by y, W will point at CFA?
0000C3r 2  20 rr rr         jsr addwx
0000C6r 2               
0000C6r 2               eval:
0000C6r 2               ; executing ? if status = 0
0000C6r 2  A5 E0            lda STATUS
0000C8r 2  F0 18            beq execute
0000CAr 2               ;
0000CAr 2               ; falls thru on compile, but...
0000CAr 2               ; immediate ? if status+1 < 0 (bit seven set)
0000CAr 2  A5 E1            lda STATUS + 1
0000CCr 2  30 14            bmi immediate
0000CEr 2               
0000CEr 2               compile:          ; otherwise compile
0000CEr 2               .ifdef DEBUG
0000CEr 2  A5 D6            lda DFLAG          ; DEBUG, print C if here
0000D0r 2  D0 08            bne CMPSKIP
0000D2r 2  20 rr rr         WCRLF_np
0000D5r 2  A9 43            lda #'C'
0000D7r 2  20 rr rr         PRINT_CHAR
0000DAr 2               CMPSKIP:
0000DAr 2               .endif
0000DAr 2  20 rr rr         jsr wcomma          ; copy W into NEXTHEAP ('here'), increment NEXTHEAP
0000DDr 2  B0 03            bcs immediate
0000DFr 2  4C rr rr         jmp resolve         ; if not 'immediate' go on to next token
0000E2r 2               ;
0000E2r 2               immediate:
0000E2r 2               execute:
0000E2r 2               
0000E2r 2               .ifdef DEBUG
0000E2r 2  A5 D6            lda DFLAG         ; DEBUG, print E if here
0000E4r 2  D0 08            bne EXESKIP
0000E6r 2  20 rr rr         WCRLF_np
0000E9r 2  A9 45            lda #'E'
0000EBr 2  20 rr rr         PRINT_CHAR
0000EEr 2               EXESKIP:
0000EEr 2               .endif
0000EEr 2  A9 rr            lda #>resolvept     ; set up INSTPTR to run,
0000F0r 2  85 ED            sta INSTPTR + 1     ; or return to interpreter.
0000F2r 2  A9 rr            lda #<resolvept
0000F4r 2  85 EC            sta INSTPTR
0000F6r 2  4C rr rr         jmp pick             ; almost done, 'next' and either ENTER or EXEC
0000F9r 2               
0000F9r 2               ;-----------------------START PROCESSING INPUT-----------------------
0000F9r 2               try:
0000F9r 2  B1 D2            lda (TIB), y                   ; index is in y
0000FBr 2  F0 04            beq getline    ; if \0  - get a line if pointing at 0
0000FDr 2  C8               iny
0000FEr 2  49 20            eor #ASCII_SPACE    ; return 0 in  A if a space
000100r 2  60               rts
000101r 2               
000101r 2               ;--------------------GET AN INPUT LINE ENDING WITH CR/LF ------------
000101r 2               getline:   ; drop rts of try, fall through to 'token'
000101r 2  68               pla
000102r 2  68               pla
000103r 2               ;
000103r 2               ;   DO AUTOLOAD HERE
000103r 2               ;      load a space, then copy next line to buffer
000103r 2               ;      calc y (length + 1) jump to GETLNEND
000103r 2               ;
000103r 2  A5 DF            lda ALFLAG
000105r 2  F0 06            beq GLNORMAL
000107r 2  20 rr rr         jsr ALOADTIB
00010Ar 2  4C rr rr         jmp GETLNSKIPCRLF
00010Dr 2               
00010Dr 2               GLNORMAL:
00010Dr 2  5A A0 00 B9      WSEQ_raw HYPROMPT    ; print prompt
000111r 2  rr rr F0 06  
000115r 2  C8 20 rr rr  
00011Cr 2               ;
00011Cr 2  A0 00            ldy #0   ; leave the first
00011Er 2               GETLOOP:
00011Er 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y  ; dummy store on first pass, overwritten
000120r 2  C8               iny
000121r 2  C4 D4            cpy TIBEND
000123r 2  F0 19            beq GETLNEND
000125r 2  C0 FF            cpy #$FF
000127r 2  D0 02            bne GETREADLOOP
000129r 2  A0 01            ldy #1
00012Br 2               GETREADLOOP:
00012Br 2  20 rr rr         jsr READ_CHAR
00012Er 2  90 FB            bcc GETREADLOOP
000130r 2  C9 0D            cmp #ASCII_CR
000132r 2  F0 0A            beq GETLNEND
000134r 2  C9 08            cmp #ASCII_BACKSPACE         ; handle backspace
000136r 2  D0 E6            bne GETLOOP
000138r 2  88               dey
000139r 2  88               dey
00013Ar 2  B1 D2            lda (TIB), y      ; make sure prev char not overwritten
00013Cr 2  80 E0            bra GETLOOP
00013Er 2               GETLNEND:                ; clear all if y eq \0
00013Er 2  20 rr rr         PRINT_CRLF
000141r 2               GETLNSKIPCRLF:          ; SKIP to here if don't want CRLF
000141r 2  A9 20            lda #ASCII_SPACE
000143r 2  5A               phy
000144r 2  A0 00            ldy #0
000146r 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y       ; start with space
000148r 2  7A               ply
000149r 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y        ; ends with space
00014Br 2  A9 00            lda #0            ; mark eol with 0
00014Dr 2  C8               iny
00014Er 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y
000150r 2  88               dey
000151r 2               ; start it
000151r 2  85 E2            sta CURBUF
000153r 2               
000153r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000153r 2               ; in place every token,
000153r 2               ; the counter is placed at last space before word
000153r 2               ; no rewinds
000153r 2               token:
000153r 2  A4 E2            ldy CURBUF   ; last position on INBUF
000155r 2               
000155r 2               TOKENSKIP:   ; skip spaces
000155r 2  20 rr rr         jsr try
000158r 2  F0 FB            beq TOKENSKIP
00015Ar 2  88               dey   ; keep y == <start of input word> + 1
00015Br 2  84 F8            sty NXTTOK
00015Dr 2               
00015Dr 2               TOKENSCAN:  ; scan spaces
00015Dr 2  20 rr rr         jsr try
000160r 2  D0 FB            bne TOKENSCAN
000162r 2  88               dey   ; keep y == <end of input word> + 1
000163r 2  84 E2            sty CURBUF
000165r 2               
000165r 2               TOKENDONE:  ; find size and store it;
000165r 2  98               tya
000166r 2  38               sec
000167r 2  E5 F8            sbc NXTTOK
000169r 2  A4 F8            ldy NXTTOK    ; keep it
00016Br 2  88               dey
00016Cr 2  91 D2            sta (TIB), y  ; store size for counted string
00016Er 2  84 F8            sty NXTTOK
000170r 2                   ;
000170r 2                   ;  During interpretive mode at least...do number and string capture here, before
000170r 2                   ;     looking at word list; will be pushed on stack.
000170r 2                   ;  This SHOULD have a general digit converter (any base up to 16); return C = 1 if no conversion
000170r 2                   ;   if SINGLE is defined, this will skip single digit numbers and use hardcoded ones.
000170r 2                   ;
000170r 2                   ;  Following check for #'s, check for quoted inline txt a la 'q^....^'
000170r 2                   ;
000170r 2               .ifdef numbers
000170r 2  A0 00            ldy #0
000172r 2  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
000174r 2  AA               tax                ; store size in X, pass to conversion
000175r 2  20 rr rr         jsr DIGCONVT
000178r 2  B0 02            bcs CHKFERTXT       ; if some error in conversion, skip and continue processing
00017Ar 2  80 0C            bra TOKCLR0
00017Cr 2               .endif  ; 'numbers'
00017Cr 2               CHKFERTXT:
00017Cr 2  20 rr rr         jsr TEXTGET         ; returns length +4 in X
00017Fr 2  B0 18            bcs TOKENEND
000181r 2  A0 00            ldy #0
000183r 2  A9 20            lda #ASCII_SPACE
000185r 2  4C rr rr         jmp TOKCLR
000188r 2               TOKCLR0:
000188r 2  A0 00            ldy #0
00018Ar 2  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y     ; load length again
00018Cr 2  AA               tax
00018Dr 2  E8               inx
00018Er 2  A9 20            lda #ASCII_SPACE          ; copy spaces over entire converted string
000190r 2               TOKCLR:
000190r 2  91 F8            sta (NXTTOK),y
000192r 2  C8               iny
000193r 2  CA               dex
000194r 2  D0 FA            bne TOKCLR
000196r 2               TOKNEXT:
000196r 2  4C rr rr         jmp token               ; and use 'token' to rebuild input buffer w/o converted #
000199r 2               
000199r 2               
000199r 2               TOKENEND:
000199r 2  18               clc     ; clean - setup token
00019Ar 2  60               rts
00019Br 2               ;
00019Br 2               ;--------------------UTILITIES----------------------------------------
00019Br 2               ;    A whole bunch of helper functions;
00019Br 2               ;  wcomma / comma increment WORKREG (or another reg) and NEXTHEAP ('here')
00019Br 2               ;  while copying into NEXTHEAP, uses incwx.
00019Br 2               ;
00019Br 2               ;  copyinto / copyfrom copy AND increment/decrement
00019Br 2               ;  spush/rpush - pushes something onto stacks indexed on ZP by Y (increment using addwx/incwx)
00019Br 2               ;  spull/rpull - pulls from stacks, copies into ZP indexed by Y (decrement builtin)
00019Br 2               ;
00019Br 2               ;  addwx/incwx/decwx do some of the inc/dec-rementing duties
00019Br 2               ;
00019Br 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00019Br 2               ;
00019Br 2               ;   COMMA allocates memory at top of heap for 'other stuff'
00019Br 2               ;   and makes sure heap and working reg pointers are updated.
00019Br 2               ;
00019Br 2               ; heap linked list (moves forward)
00019Br 2               ;
00019Br 2               wcomma:
00019Br 2  A0 EE            ldy #WORKREG                  ; copy addr at WORKREG, change addr fld NEXTHEAP points to
00019Dr 2               comma:
00019Dr 2  A2 E6            ldx #NEXTHEAP                 ; Y has source of address, change addr fld NEXTHEAP points to
00019Fr 2                   ; FALL THROUGH - copyinto, then rts after second incwx
00019Fr 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00019Fr 2               ; from a page zero address indexed by Y
00019Fr 2               ; into a page zero indirect address indexed by X
00019Fr 2               ;
00019Fr 2               copyinto:
00019Fr 2  B9 00 00         lda 0, y
0001A2r 2  81 00            sta (0, x)
0001A4r 2  20 rr rr         jsr incwx
0001A7r 2  B9 01 00         lda 1, y
0001AAr 2  81 00            sta (0, x)
0001ACr 2  4C rr rr         jmp incwx                        ; incwx ends with rts!
0001AFr 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001AFr 2               ;
0001AFr 2               ; generics - PUSH, PULL, incwx/addwx, copyfrom
0001AFr 2               ;
0001AFr 2               ;------------------------PUSH a cell--------------------------------
0001AFr 2               spush_2:
0001AFr 2  A0 F4            ldy #TEMP3       ; push TEMP3 on top
0001B1r 2  4C rr rr         jmp spush
0001B4r 2               spush_1:
0001B4r 2  A0 F2            ldy #TEMP2       ; push TEMP2 on top
0001B6r 2  4C rr rr         jmp spush
0001B9r 2               spush_0:             ; push TEMP1 to stack, probably top of stack
0001B9r 2  A0 F0            ldy #TEMP1
0001BBr 2                    ; FALL THROUGH
0001BBr 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001BBr 2               ; PUSH a cell
0001BBr 2               ; from a page zero address indexed by Y
0001BBr 2               ; into a page zero indirect address indexed by X
0001BBr 2               spush:
0001BBr 2  A2 E8            ldx #DSPTR
0001BDr 2  A5 E8            lda DSPTR
0001BFr 2  C9 rr            cmp #<DS                ; ditto
0001C1r 2  F0 20            beq ptrerr_s              ; ditto
0001C3r 2  4C rr rr         jmp push
0001C6r 2               rpush:
0001C6r 2  A2 EA            ldx #RTPTR
0001C8r 2  A5 EA            lda RTPTR               ; ditto
0001CAr 2  C9 rr            cmp #<RT                ; ditto
0001CCr 2  F0 11            beq ptrerr_r
0001CEr 2               
0001CEr 2                   ; FALL THROUGH
0001CEr 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0001CEr 2               ; classic stack backwards
0001CEr 2               push:
0001CEr 2  20 rr rr         jsr decwx
0001D1r 2  B9 01 00         lda 1, y
0001D4r 2  81 00            sta (0, x)
0001D6r 2  20 rr rr         jsr decwx
0001D9r 2  B9 00 00         lda 0, y
0001DCr 2  81 00            sta (0, x)
0001DEr 2  60               rts
0001DFr 2               ;
0001DFr 2               ;                      pointer error (DS or RT)
0001DFr 2               ptrerr_r:                      ; pop jsr off stack, throw error
0001DFr 2  A9 01            lda #ERR_RPTR
0001E1r 2  80 02            bra ptrerr_cont
0001E3r 2               ptrerr_s:
0001E3r 2  A9 02            lda #ERR_SPTR
0001E5r 2               ptrerr_cont:
0001E5r 2  85 D7            sta ERRFLAG
0001E7r 2  68               pla
0001E8r 2  68               pla
0001E9r 2  4C rr rr         jmp errrtn
0001ECr 2               ;
0001ECr 2               ;---------------- PULL a cell, with convenience for TEMP 1/2/3 -----
0001ECr 2               ;
0001ECr 2               spull_2:
0001ECr 2  A0 F4            ldy #TEMP3              ; pull TEMP3 from top
0001EEr 2  4C rr rr         jmp spull
0001F1r 2               
0001F1r 2               spull_1:                    ; pull TEMP2 from top
0001F1r 2  A0 F2            ldy #TEMP2
0001F3r 2  4C rr rr         jmp spull
0001F6r 2               
0001F6r 2               spull_0:
0001F6r 2  A0 F0            ldy #TEMP1             ; pull TEMP1 from top of DS
0001F8r 2               ;
0001F8r 2               ;  FALL THROUGH
0001F8r 2               ;
0001F8r 2               ; PULL a cell
0001F8r 2               ; from a page zero indirect address indexed by X
0001F8r 2               ; into a page zero address indexed by y
0001F8r 2               ;
0001F8r 2               spull:
0001F8r 2  A2 E8            ldx #DSPTR
0001FAr 2  A5 E8            lda DSPTR         ; pointer bounds checking
0001FCr 2  C9 7E            cmp #DSEND        ; ditto
0001FEr 2  F0 E3            beq ptrerr_s      ; ditto
000200r 2  4C rr rr         jmp pull          ; pull includes rts from incwx so....
000203r 2               rpull:                ;
000203r 2  A2 EA            ldx #RTPTR
000205r 2  A5 EA            lda RTPTR        ; pointer bounds checking
000207r 2  C9 FE            cmp #RTEND        ; ditto
000209r 2  F0 D4            beq ptrerr_r        ; ditto
00020Br 2               ;
00020Br 2               ;  FALL THROUGH
00020Br 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00020Br 2               ;
00020Br 2               ; from a page zero indirect address indexed by X
00020Br 2               ; into a page zero address indexed by y
00020Br 2               pull:
00020Br 2               copyfrom:
00020Br 2  A1 00            lda (0, x)
00020Dr 2  99 00 00         sta 0, y
000210r 2  20 rr rr         jsr incwx      ; NOTE:  not a jmp, returns here.
000213r 2  A1 00            lda (0, x)
000215r 2  99 01 00         sta 1, y
000218r 2                ;
000218r 2                ;  FALL THROUGH
000218r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000218r 2               ; increment a word in page zero. offset by X
000218r 2               ;
000218r 2               ;   Usage: ldx #<pointer name> and then jsr incwx/addwx
000218r 2               ;
000218r 2               ;   THESE functions are SOLELY intended to increment/decrement
000218r 2               ;   zeropage pointers, and nothing else.  Offsets indexed by
000218r 2               ;   X are INTO the zeropage space, not relative to a specific
000218r 2               ;   pointer location.
000218r 2               ;
000218r 2               incwx:
000218r 2  A9 01            lda #01
00021Ar 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00021Ar 2               ; add a byte in A to a word in page zero. offset by X
00021Ar 2               addwx:
00021Ar 2  18               clc
00021Br 2  75 00            adc 0, x
00021Dr 2  95 00            sta 0, x
00021Fr 2  90 03            bcc addwx_end
000221r 2  F6 01            inc 1, x
000223r 2  18               clc      ; keep carry clean.
000224r 2                            ; our convention is that functions SET the carry for positive results,
000224r 2                            ; clear carry for negative or neutral ones.
000224r 2               addwx_end:
000224r 2  60               rts
000225r 2               
000225r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000225r 2               ;          decwx, heap moves
000225r 2               ; decrement a word in page zero. offset by X
000225r 2               ;
000225r 2               ;   Usage: ldx #<pointer name> and then jsr decwx
000225r 2               ;
000225r 2               ;   THESE functions are SOLELY intended to increment/decrement
000225r 2               ;   zeropage pointers, and nothing else.  Offsets indexed by
000225r 2               ;   X are INTO the zeropage space, not relative to a specific
000225r 2               ;   pointer location.
000225r 2               ;
000225r 2               decwx:
000225r 2  B5 00            lda 0, x
000227r 2  D0 02            bne decwx_end
000229r 2  D6 01            dec 1, x
00022Br 2               decwx_end:
00022Br 2  D6 00            dec 0, x
00022Dr 2  60               rts
00022Er 2               ;
00022Er 2               ;
00022Er 2               ;
00022Er 2               ENGINEEND:
00022Er 2               ;                           END OF CORE ENGINE

00022Er 3               ;  error messaging

00022Er 3               wrterror:
00022Er 3  A9 rr            lda #>err_jumptable
000230r 3  85 D9            sta ERRPTR+1
000232r 3  A5 D7            lda ERRFLAG
000234r 3  F0 28            beq ERREND
000236r 3  0A               asl a
000237r 3  18               clc
000238r 3  69 rr            adc #<err_jumptable
00023Ar 3  85 D8            sta ERRPTR
00023Cr 3  90 02            bcc ERRSKIP
00023Er 3  E6 D9            inc ERRPTR+1
000240r 3               ERRSKIP:
000240r 3  20 rr rr A0      WERR ERRPTR
000244r 3  00 B1 D8 85  
000248r 3  C8 C8 B1 D8  
00025Er 3               ERREND:
00025Er 3  A9 00            lda #0
000260r 3  85 D7            sta ERRFLAG
000262r 3  85 D8            sta ERRPTR
000264r 3  85 D9            sta ERRPTR+1
000266r 3  60               rts
000267r 3               ;
000267r 3               ;
000267r 3               err_jumptable:
000267r 3  xx xx            .res 2
000269r 3  rr rr            ERR_entry RPTR_ERR              ; RT stack full/empty  - error $01
00026Br 3  rr rr            ERR_entry SPTR_ERR              ; DS stack full/empty  - error $02
00026Dr 3  rr rr            ERR_entry DIV_ERR               ; divide by zero - error $03
00026Fr 3  rr rr            ERR_entry OOM_ERR               ; out of memory  - error $04
000271r 3  rr rr            ERR_entry UKW_ERR               ; no existing word - error $05
000273r 3  rr rr            ERR_entry SEC_ERR               ; writing to dangerous RAM areas - error $06
000275r 3  rr rr            ERR_entry SYS_ERR               ; error on return from SYSCALL - error $07
000277r 3               LASTERR = 7
000277r 3               ;
000277r 3               ;  error messages
000277r 3               RPTR_ERR:
000277r 3  20 21 52 54      .byte " !RT PTR ERROR!"
00027Br 3  20 50 54 52  
00027Fr 3  20 45 52 52  
000286r 3  00               .byte 0
000287r 3               SPTR_ERR:
000287r 3  20 21 44 53      .byte " !DS PTR ERROR!"
00028Br 3  20 50 54 52  
00028Fr 3  20 45 52 52  
000296r 3  00               .byte 0
000297r 3               DIV_ERR:

00029Br 3  56 20 5A 45  
00029Fr 3  52 4F 21     
0002A2r 3  00               .byte 0
0002A3r 3               OOM_ERR:

0002A7r 3  57 20 4D 45  
0002ABr 3  4D 21        
0002ADr 3  00               .byte 0
0002AEr 3               UKW_ERR:
0002AEr 3  20 21 55 4E      .byte " !UNK WORD!"
0002B2r 3  4B 20 57 4F  
0002B6r 3  52 44 21     
0002B9r 3  00               .byte 0
0002BAr 3               SEC_ERR:
0002BAr 3  20 21 53 45      .byte " !SECURITY!"
0002BEr 3  43 55 52 49  
0002C2r 3  54 59 21

0002CEr 3  52 21        
0002D0r 3  00               .byte 0

0002D1r 3               ;-------------------------------------------------------------
0002D1r 3               ;
0002D1r 3               ;           CODE called by engine AND heap
0002D1r 3               ;
0002D1r 3               ;------- used by 'exit'
0002D1r 3               ;------------------------NOTE:  this is ALL of 'exit' but the screaming.
0002D1r 3               ;----------------- For modularity purposes this is the only sensible place for it.
0002D1r 3               ;
0002D1r 3               EXIT:
0002D1r 3               unnest:                             ; EXIT - done with previous word, on to next whether compiled or primitive
0002D1r 3               
0002D1r 3  A0 EC            ldy #INSTPTR
0002D3r 3  20 rr rr         jsr rpull                       ; IP = [RTPTR], RTPTR += 2
0002D6r 3               
0002D6r 3               next:                               ;    go on to next word; IP is pointing at next entry in data field of word
0002D6r 3               ; WORKREG = (INSTPTR) ; INSTPTR += 2
0002D6r 3  A2 EC            ldx #INSTPTR
0002D8r 3  A0 EE            ldy #WORKREG
0002DAr 3  20 rr rr         jsr copyfrom                    ; W = [IP], IP += 2  ( and either ENTER or EXECUTE )
0002DDr 3               
0002DDr 3               pick:                               ;        'THE SWITCHER' - COMPILED OR PRIMITIVE?
0002DDr 3                                                   ;
0002DDr 3               .ifdef DEBUG                        ;  DEBUG
0002DDr 3  A5 D6            lda DFLAG
0002DFr 3  D0 2F            bne PRINTWSKIP                  ; print W and [W] etc if DEBUG
0002E1r 3  5A A0 00 B9      WSEQ_raw WDISP
0002E5r 3  rr rr F0 06  
0002E9r 3  C8 20 rr rr  
0002F0r 3  A5 EF            lda WORKREG+1
0002F2r 3  20 rr rr         PRINT_BYTE
0002F5r 3  A5 EE            lda WORKREG
0002F7r 3  20 rr rr         PRINT_BYTE
0002FAr 3  5A A0 00 B9      WSEQ_raw WATDISP
0002FEr 3  rr rr F0 06  
000302r 3  C8 20 rr rr  
000309r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
00030Br 3  B1 EE            lda (WORKREG),y
00030Dr 3  20 rr rr         PRINT_BYTE
000310r 3               PRINTWSKIP:
000310r 3               .endif
000310r 3               ; .. use old-fashioned way AND new tricks
000310r 3               ;
000310r 3  A5 EF            lda WORKREG + 1                 ; compare pages (MSBs)
000312r 3  C9 rr            cmp #>ends + 1                  ;  !! Compiled must be higher in memory than hardcoded !! (see below)
000314r 3  30 16            bmi jump                        ; jump over nest if native code
000316r 3  A0 01            ldy #1                          ; ldy #0 if check extra byte
000318r 3  B1 EE            lda (WORKREG),y
00031Ar 3  F0 10            beq jump                        ; yep, jump to native execution
00031Cr 3               
00031Cr 3               nest:                               ; ENTER in classic Forth lingo   ( COMPILED )
00031Cr 3  A0 EC            ldy #INSTPTR
00031Er 3  20 rr rr         jsr rpush                       ; RTPTR -=2, [RTPTR] = [IP]
000321r 3  A5 EE            lda WORKREG                     ; W = IP
000323r 3  85 EC            sta INSTPTR
000325r 3  A5 EF            lda WORKREG + 1
000327r 3  85 ED            sta INSTPTR + 1
000329r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next                        ; next
00032Cr 3               
00032Cr 3               jump:                               ; EXECUTE                      ( PRIMITIVE )
00032Cr 3  6C EE 00         jmp (WORKREG)                   ; start running code at next word
00032Fr 3                                                   ;  JUMP [W]
00032Fr 3               ;
00032Fr 3               ;------- used by 'autoload'
00032Fr 3               ALOADTIB:
00032Fr 3  A0 00            ldy #0

00033Er 3               ALOADSKIP:
00033Er 3  20 rr rr         jsr ALOADCHKDONE
000341r 3  C8               iny

000343r 3

000343r 3  C8               iny

000350r 3  85 FE            sta TEMP7

000358r 3               ;
000358r 3               ;-------- malloc and mlen
000358r 3               
000358r 3               MALLOC:
000358r 3                   ;  TEMP1 and TEMP2 should have bytes / record type if 'jsr MALLOC'
000358r 3                   ;  uses TEMP3, y, x, a
000358r 3  A5 D0            lda MEMLAST
00035Ar 3  38               sec
00035Br 3  E9 03            sbc #3
00035Dr 3  85 D0            sta MEMLAST
00035Fr 3  B0 02            bcs MALSK00
000361r 3  C6 D1            dec MEMLAST+1
000363r 3               MALSK00:
000363r 3  38               sec
000364r 3  E5 F0            sbc TEMP1
000366r 3  85 D0            sta MEMLAST
000368r 3  A5 D1            lda MEMLAST+1
00036Ar 3  E5 F1            sbc TEMP1+1
00036Cr 3  85 D1            sta MEMLAST+1
00036Er 3                                       ;MEMLAST updated to start of new record
00036Er 3  A5 D0            lda MEMLAST
000370r 3  85 F4            sta TEMP3
000372r 3  A5 D1            lda MEMLAST+1
000374r 3  85 F5            sta TEMP3+1         ; use TEMP3 to walk through clearing of memory
000376r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
000378r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2            ; write type first
00037Ar 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
00037Cr 3  C8               iny
00037Dr 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1            ; LSB length
00037Fr 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
000381r 3  C8               iny
000382r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1          ; MSB length
000384r 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
000386r 3  A2 F4            ldx #TEMP3
000388r 3  A9 03            lda #3
00038Ar 3  20 rr rr         jsr addwx            ; increment TEMP3 by 3
00038Dr 3               MALLOOP:

00038Fr 3  A0 00            ldy #0
000391r 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
000393r 3  C6 F0            dec TEMP1
000395r 3  D0 0C            bne MALSK02
000397r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
000399r 3  F0 10            beq MALCONT
00039Br 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
00039Dr 3  C9 FF            cmp #$FF
00039Fr 3  D0 02            bne MALSK02
0003A1r 3  C6 F1            dec TEMP1+1
0003A3r 3               MALSK02:

0003A5r 3  D0 02            bne MALSK01
0003A7r 3  E6 F5            inc TEMP3+1
0003A9r 3               MALSK01:

0003ABr 3               MALCONT:                   ; now store MEMLAST at MEMPTR
0003ABr 3  A0 00            ldy #0
0003ADr 3  A5 D0            lda MEMLAST
0003AFr 3  91 CE            sta (MEMPTR),y
0003B1r 3  C8               iny

0003B6r 3  A5 CF            lda MEMPTR+1
0003B8r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
0003BAr 3  A5 CE            lda MEMPTR
0003BCr 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1             ; copy to TEMP1 before incrementing
0003BEr 3  38               sec                   ; MEMPTR + 2

0003C5r 3  C6 CF            dec MEMPTR+1
0003C7r 3               MALLOCEND:

0003D1r 3  85 F5             sta TEMP3+1

0003D7r 3  85 F0             sta TEMP1
