0003DFr 3               ;
0003DFr 3               ;-------------------------------------------------------------
0003DFr 3               ;                MATH routines
0003DFr 3               ;         with MULT16 / DIV16, signs handled by calling word.
0003DFr 3               ;         we just do the math here.
0003DFr 3               ;
0003DFr 3               ;
0003DFr 3               MULT16:                             ; 16 x 16 multiply; TEMP1 and TEMP2 are #'s, TEMP1 will be result
0003DFr 3  64 F4            stz TEMP3                       ; with TEMP3 as high bytes
0003E1r 3  64 F5            stz TEMP3+1
0003E3r 3  A2 11            ldx #17
0003E5r 3  18               clc
0003E6r 3               MULTLOOP:
0003E6r 3  66 F5            ror TEMP3+1                     ; RIGHT.  if you need to go backwards, go backwards stupid fuck.
0003E8r 3  66 F4            ror TEMP3
0003EAr 3  66 F1            ror TEMP1+1
0003ECr 3  66 F0            ror TEMP1
0003EEr 3  90 0D            bcc MULTDECCNT
0003F0r 3  18               clc
0003F1r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
0003F3r 3  65 F4            adc TEMP3
0003F5r 3  85 F4            sta TEMP3
0003F7r 3  A5 F3            lda TEMP2+1
0003F9r 3  65 F5            adc TEMP3+1
0003FBr 3  85 F5            sta TEMP3+1
0003FDr 3               MULTDECCNT:
0003FDr 3  CA               dex
0003FEr 3  D0 E6            bne MULTLOOP
000400r 3  60               rts
000401r 3               
000401r 3               ;
000401r 3               ;
000401r 3                      ; 16 x 16 divide; TEMP1 and TEMP2 #'s - TEMP3 is 'overflow'
000401r 3                      ;  TEMP2 divisor, TEMP1 dividend, TEMP1 + 3 = result + remainder
000401r 3               DIV16:
000401r 3  64 F4            stz TEMP3
000403r 3  64 F5            stz TEMP3+1
000405r 3  A2 10            ldx #16
000407r 3               UDIVLP:
000407r 3  26 F0            rol TEMP1
000409r 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
00040Br 3  26 F4            rol TEMP3
00040Dr 3  26 F5            rol TEMP3+1
00040Fr 3               UDIVCHK:
00040Fr 3  38               sec
000410r 3  A5 F4            lda TEMP3
000412r 3  E5 F2            sbc TEMP2
000414r 3  A8               tay
000415r 3  A5 F5            lda TEMP3+1
000417r 3  E5 F3            sbc TEMP2+1
000419r 3  90 04            bcc UDIVCNT
00041Br 3  84 F4            sty TEMP3
00041Dr 3  85 F5            sta TEMP3+1
00041Fr 3               UDIVCNT:
00041Fr 3  CA               dex
000420r 3  D0 E5            bne UDIVLP
000422r 3  26 F0            rol TEMP1
000424r 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
000426r 3  60               rts
000427r 3               ;
000427r 3               ;     galois32o - LSFR psuedo-random # generator
000427r 3               ;
000427r 3               ;  -- boilerplate --
000427r 3               ; 6502 LFSR PRNG - 32-bit
000427r 3               ; Brad Smith, 2019
000427r 3               ; http://rainwarrior.ca
000427r 3               ;
000427r 3               ;
000427r 3               galois32o:
000427r 3                   ; rotate the middle bytes left
000427r 3  A4 DD            ldy RSEED+2                     ; will move to RSEED+3 at the end
000429r 3  A5 DC            lda RSEED+1
00042Br 3  85 DD            sta RSEED+2
00042Dr 3                   ; compute RSEED+1 ($C5>>1 = %1100010)
00042Dr 3  A5 DE            lda RSEED+3                     ; original high byte
00042Fr 3  4A               lsr
000430r 3  85 DC            sta RSEED+1                     ; reverse: 100011
000432r 3  4A               lsr
000433r 3  4A               lsr
000434r 3  4A               lsr
000435r 3  4A               lsr
000436r 3  45 DC            eor RSEED+1
000438r 3  4A               lsr
000439r 3  45 DC            eor RSEED+1
00043Br 3  45 DB            eor RSEED+0                     ; combine with original low byte
00043Dr 3  85 DC            sta RSEED+1
00043Fr 3                   ; compute RSEED+0 ($C5 = %11000101)
00043Fr 3  A5 DE            lda RSEED+3                     ; original high byte
000441r 3  0A               asl
000442r 3  45 DE            eor RSEED+3
000444r 3  0A               asl
000445r 3  0A               asl
000446r 3  0A               asl
000447r 3  0A               asl
000448r 3  45 DE            eor RSEED+3
00044Ar 3  0A               asl
00044Br 3  0A               asl
00044Cr 3  45 DE            eor RSEED+3
00044Er 3  84 DE            sty RSEED+3                     ; finish rotating byte 2 into 3
000450r 3  85 DB            sta RSEED+0
000452r 3  60               rts
000453r 3               ;-------------------------------------------------------------------
000453r 3               ;              get delimited text from INBUF, store in string
000453r 3               ;
000453r 3               
000453r 3               .ifndef TXT2STACK
000453r 3               ;
000453r 3               ;  uses TEMP1, TEMP2, TEMP3, TEMP5, TEMP6, X, Y
000453r 3               ;
000453r 3               TEXTGET:
000453r 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1+1                     ; will store length here
000455r 3  A9 04            lda #$04
000457r 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2                       ; record type is 'sz'
000459r 3  64 F3            stz TEMP2+1
00045Br 3  64 FD            stz TEMP6                       ; to save y for later copy
00045Dr 3  A0 01            ldy #1                          ; skip len of first token
00045Fr 3               TX2SKSPC:
00045Fr 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
000461r 3  C9 20            cmp #ASCII_SPACE                        ; skip leading spaces
000463r 3  D0 03            bne TX2SK00
000465r 3  C8               iny
000466r 3  80 F7            bra TX2SKSPC
000468r 3               TX2SK00:
000468r 3  C9 71            cmp #ASCII_q
00046Ar 3  D0 63            bne TX2NOGOOD
00046Cr 3  C8               iny
00046Dr 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
00046Fr 3  C9 5E            cmp #ASCII_CARET
000471r 3  D0 5C            bne TX2NOGOOD
000473r 3  C8               iny
000474r 3  84 FD            sty TEMP6                       ; temp6 stores pos of first char
000476r 3               TX2SCAN:
000476r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y                  ; find delimiting '^'
000478r 3  C8               iny
000479r 3  C9 5E            cmp #ASCII_CARET
00047Br 3  F0 0A            beq TX2FOUND
00047Dr 3  98               tya
00047Er 3  18               clc
00047Fr 3  65 F8            adc NXTTOK
000481r 3  C9 64            cmp #MAXSTR
000483r 3  B0 4A            bcs TX2NOGOOD
000485r 3  80 EF            bra TX2SCAN
000487r 3               TX2FOUND:
000487r 3  88               dey
000488r 3  84 FC            sty TEMP5                       ; temp5 pos+1 last letter
00048Ar 3  98               tya
00048Br 3  38               sec
00048Cr 3  E5 FD            sbc TEMP6
00048Er 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1                       ; and this should be the length
000490r 3  E6 F0            inc TEMP1                       ; and add one for zero at end
000492r 3               
000492r 3  20 rr rr         jsr MALLOC                      ; TEMP1 now has address on mem stack
000495r 3               
000495r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
000497r 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1),y
000499r 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
00049Br 3  C8               iny
00049Cr 3  B1 F0            lda (TEMP1),y
00049Er 3  85 F3            sta TEMP2+1                     ; address in memory area
0004A0r 3               
0004A0r 3                   ; now for math.  NXTTOK ptr needs to be ref'd with same y
0004A0r 3                   ; as TEMP2, so we need to match them up...and we are hitting
0004A0r 3                   ; the record at +3, so....TEMP6 is start
0004A0r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
0004A2r 3  38               sec
0004A3r 3  E5 FD            sbc TEMP6
0004A5r 3  B0 02            bcs TX2SK01
0004A7r 3  C6 F3            dec TEMP2+1
0004A9r 3               TX2SK01:
0004A9r 3  18               clc
0004AAr 3  69 03            adc #3
0004ACr 3  90 02            bcc TX2SK02
0004AEr 3  E6 F3            inc TEMP2+1
0004B0r 3               TX2SK02:
0004B0r 3  A4 FD            ldy TEMP6
0004B2r 3               TX2CPYLOOP:
0004B2r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
0004B4r 3  91 F2            sta (TEMP2),y
0004B6r 3  C8               iny
0004B7r 3  C4 FC            cpy TEMP5
0004B9r 3  D0 F7            bne TX2CPYLOOP
0004BBr 3               TX2SK99:
0004BBr 3  A9 00            lda #0
0004BDr 3  91 F2            sta (TEMP2),y                   ; put zero on end
0004BFr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0                     ; push address from mem stack on DS
0004C2r 3  A5 FC            lda TEMP5
0004C4r 3  38               sec
0004C5r 3  E5 FD            sbc TEMP6
0004C7r 3  18               clc
0004C8r 3  69 04            adc #4
0004CAr 3  AA               tax
0004CBr 3  18               clc
0004CCr 3  4C rr rr         jmp TX2END
0004CFr 3               TX2NOGOOD:
0004CFr 3  38               sec
0004D0r 3               TX2END:
0004D0r 3  60               rts
0004D1r 3               ;
0004D1r 3               ;  end of TXG2  (new text capture)
0004D1r 3               ;
0004D1r 3               
0004D1r 3               .else  ;  TXT2STACK
0004D1r 3               
0004D1r 3               TEXTGET:
0004D1r 3                   stz TEMP6
0004D1r 3                   stz TEMP1
0004D1r 3                   stz TEMP1+1
0004D1r 3                   ldy #1                          ; skip len
0004D1r 3               TXTSKIPSPC:
0004D1r 3                   lda (NXTTOK),y
0004D1r 3                   cmp #ASCII_SPACE                        ; skip leading spaces
0004D1r 3                   bne TXTSPCS
0004D1r 3                   iny
0004D1r 3                   bra TXTSKIPSPC
0004D1r 3               TXTSPCS:
0004D1r 3                   cmp #ASCII_q
0004D1r 3                   bne TEXTNOGOOD
0004D1r 3                   iny
0004D1r 3                   lda (NXTTOK),y
0004D1r 3                   cmp #ASCII_CARET
0004D1r 3                   bne TEXTNOGOOD
0004D1r 3                   sta TEMP3                       ; remember....
0004D1r 3                   iny
0004D1r 3               TXTSCAN:
0004D1r 3                   lda (NXTTOK),y                  ; find delimiting '^'
0004D1r 3                   iny
0004D1r 3                   cmp #ASCII_CARET
0004D1r 3                   beq TXTFOUND
0004D1r 3                   tya
0004D1r 3                   clc
0004D1r 3                   adc NXTTOK
0004D1r 3                   cmp #MAXSTR
0004D1r 3                   bcs TEXTNOGOOD
0004D1r 3                   bra TXTSCAN
0004D1r 3               TXTFOUND:
0004D1r 3                   ldx #0
0004D1r 3                   dey
0004D1r 3                   dey                             ; now points at last letter
0004D1r 3                   tya
0004D1r 3                   sec
0004D1r 3                   sbc TEMP3                       ; and this should be the length
0004D1r 3                   stx TEMP3
0004D1r 3                   and #1
0004D1r 3                   beq TEXTGLOOP
0004D1r 3                   inc TEMP3
0004D1r 3                   inx
0004D1r 3               TEXTGLOOP:
0004D1r 3                   lda (NXTTOK),y
0004D1r 3                   cmp #ASCII_CARET                        ; when we hit the other end again...
0004D1r 3                   beq TEXTOK
0004D1r 3                   sta TEMP6
0004D1r 3               
0004D1r 3               .ifdef DEBUG
0004D1r 3                   jsr DUMPREG                     ; DEBUG
0004D1r 3               .endif
0004D1r 3               
0004D1r 3                   txa
0004D1r 3                   and #1
0004D1r 3                   bne TEXTODD
0004D1r 3                   lda TEMP6
0004D1r 3                   sta TEMP1
0004D1r 3                   stz TEMP1+1
0004D1r 3                   bra TEXTSKIP2
0004D1r 3               TEXTODD:
0004D1r 3                   lda TEMP6
0004D1r 3                   sta TEMP1+1
0004D1r 3                   phx
0004D1r 3                   phy
0004D1r 3                   jsr spush_0
0004D1r 3                   ply
0004D1r 3                   plx
0004D1r 3               TEXTSKIP2:
0004D1r 3                   inx
0004D1r 3                   dey
0004D1r 3               .ifdef DEBUG
0004D1r 3                   jsr DUMPREG                     ; DEBUG
0004D1r 3               .endif
0004D1r 3                   bra TEXTGLOOP
0004D1r 3               TEXTOK:
0004D1r 3                   phy
0004D1r 3                   phx
0004D1r 3                   txa
0004D1r 3                   and #1
0004D1r 3                   beq TEXTCONT
0004D1r 3                   jsr spush_0
0004D1r 3               TEXTCONT:
0004D1r 3                   plx
0004D1r 3                   txa
0004D1r 3                   sec
0004D1r 3                   sbc TEMP3
0004D1r 3                   sta TEMP3
0004D1r 3                   stz TEMP3+1
0004D1r 3                   jsr spush_2                     ; push length on top
0004D1r 3                   ply
0004D1r 3                   clc
0004D1r 3                   bra TEXTGEND
0004D1r 3               TEXTNOGOOD:
0004D1r 3                   sec
0004D1r 3               TEXTGEND:
0004D1r 3                   rts
0004D1r 3               
0004D1r 3               .endif  ; ---TXT2STACK
0004D1r 3               
0004D1r 3               ;-----------------------   NUMBER CONVERSIONS
0004D1r 3               ;
0004D1r 3               DEC2ASCII:       ;  X is # - return as two digits in TEMP3, TEMP3+1
0004D1r 3  A9 30            lda #ASCII_0
0004D3r 3  85 F5            sta TEMP3+1
0004D5r 3  8A               txa
0004D6r 3  85 F4            sta TEMP3
0004D8r 3               D2ASCLOOP:
0004D8r 3  38               sec
0004D9r 3  E9 0A            sbc #ASCII_LF
0004DBr 3  90 04            bcc D2ASCNEXT
0004DDr 3  E6 F5            inc TEMP3+1
0004DFr 3  80 F7            bra D2ASCLOOP
0004E1r 3               D2ASCNEXT:
0004E1r 3  18               clc
0004E2r 3  69 3A            adc #$3A
0004E4r 3  85 F4            sta TEMP3
0004E6r 3  60               rts
0004E7r 3               
0004E7r 3               .ifdef numbers
0004E7r 3               ;------------------------
0004E7r 3               ;      CONVERT DIGITS, PUSH on DS
0004E7r 3               
0004E7r 3               DIGCONVT:    ;  Y is index into NXTPTR, X is length
0004E7r 3  64 F0            stz TEMP1
0004E9r 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1+1
0004EBr 3  64 F2            stz TEMP2                       ; no necc for bin or hex, but
0004EDr 3  64 F3            stz TEMP2+1                     ; since is convenient....
0004EFr 3  64 FD            stz TEMP6
0004F1r 3  A0 01            ldy #1                          ; skip over length
0004F3r 3  A9 0A            lda #10
0004F5r 3  85 DA            sta DIGBASE
0004F7r 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
0004F9r 3  C9 2D            cmp #ASCII_MINUS
0004FBr 3  F0 0A            beq DIGCONV_MINUS
0004FDr 3  C9 24            cmp #ASCII_DOLLAR
0004FFr 3  F0 12            beq DIGHEX1
000501r 3  C9 25            cmp #ASCII_PERCENT
000503r 3  F0 07            beq DIGBIN1
000505r 3  80 12            bra DIG_SNG
000507r 3               DIGCONV_MINUS:
000507r 3  E6 FD            inc TEMP6
000509r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIG_XY
00050Cr 3               DIGBIN1:
00050Cr 3  A9 02            lda #2
00050Er 3  85 DA            sta DIGBASE
000510r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIG_XY
000513r 3               DIGHEX1:
000513r 3  A9 10            lda #16
000515r 3  85 DA            sta DIGBASE
000517r 3               DIG_XY:
000517r 3  C8               iny
000518r 3  CA               dex
000519r 3               DIG_SNG:
000519r 3                 .ifdef SINGLE
000519r 3  E0 01            cpx #1                          ; skip single digit, handle hard-wired or...
00051Br 3  D0 03            bne DIGCONV_LOOP                ; only one digit left, we can handle that elsewhere
00051Dr 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIGCONV_ERR                 ; not really an error, just return and let 'find' to it
000520r 3                 .endif
000520r 3               DIGCONV_LOOP:
000520r 3  20 rr rr         jsr GETDIG
000523r 3  90 03            bcc DIGCONT0
000525r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIGCONV_ERR
000528r 3               DIGCONT0:
000528r 3  48               pha                             ; else save it to add in a bit
000529r 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1                       ; do first shift
00052Br 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
00052Dr 3  A5 DA            lda DIGBASE                     ; check base that was set above
00052Fr 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
000531r 3  F0 06            beq DIGDEC                      ; it's DEC, go there
000533r 3  C9 02            cmp #2
000535r 3  F0 2F            beq DIGBIN                      ; it's BIN, go there
000537r 3  80 1D            bra DIGHEX                      ; Go to hex if others not true
000539r 3               DIGDEC:
000539r 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1                       ; 2nd shift; upper nybble doesn't end up right
00053Br 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
00053Dr 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1                       ; load results of two shifts
00053Fr 3  18               clc
000540r 3  65 F2            adc TEMP2                       ; add in previous total from last loop
000542r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
000544r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1                     ; and same with second digit, and the carry
000546r 3  65 F3            adc TEMP2+1
000548r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
00054Ar 3               DIGDEC2:
00054Ar 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1                       ; final shift
00054Cr 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
00054Er 3  68               pla                             ; bring back read digit
00054Fr 3  C9 0A            cmp #10                         ; make sure it's not hex, mostly
000551r 3  90 18            bcc DIGCONT                     ; jump to continue
000553r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIGCONV_ERR                 ; else throw error
000556r 3               DIGHEX:
000556r 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1
000558r 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
00055Ar 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1
00055Cr 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1
00055Er 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1
000560r 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1+1                     ; shifted three more times
000562r 3  68               pla
000563r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DIGCONT
000566r 3               DIGBIN:                             ; already did the single shift
000566r 3  68               pla
000567r 3  C9 02            cmp #2
000569r 3  B0 3E            bcs DIGCONV_ERR                 ; fall through to the additon of new digit
00056Br 3               DIGCONT:
00056Br 3  18               clc                             ; add digit finally
00056Cr 3  65 F0            adc TEMP1
00056Er 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
000570r 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2                       ; copy to intermediate result in case another dec digit
000572r 3  90 02            bcc DIGCONT2
000574r 3  E6 F1            inc TEMP1+1                     ; and inc 2nd byte if necc.
000576r 3               DIGCONT2:
000576r 3  A5 DA            lda DIGBASE
000578r 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
00057Ar 3  D0 08            bne DIGCONT3
00057Cr 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
00057Er 3  85 F3            sta TEMP2+1                     ; save it for next round, regardless
000580r 3  C9 80            cmp #$80                        ; check to see if > $8000
000582r 3  B0 25            bcs DIGCONV_ERR
000584r 3               DIGCONT3:
000584r 3  C8               iny
000585r 3  CA               dex
000586r 3  D0 98            bne DIGCONV_LOOP
000588r 3  A5 DA            lda DIGBASE
00058Ar 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
00058Cr 3  D0 16            bne DIGCONT4
00058Er 3               DECFINISH:
00058Er 3  A5 FD            lda TEMP6                       ; check for minus
000590r 3  F0 12            beq DIGCONT4
000592r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000594r 3  49 FF            eor #$FF
000596r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
000598r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
00059Ar 3  49 FF            eor #$FF
00059Cr 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
00059Er 3  E6 F0            inc TEMP1
0005A0r 3  D0 02            bne DIGCONT4
0005A2r 3  E6 F1            inc TEMP1+1
0005A4r 3               DIGCONT4:
0005A4r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0                     ; push TEMP1 on to stack
0005A7r 3  18               clc
0005A8r 3  60               rts
0005A9r 3               DIGCONV_ERR:
0005A9r 3  38               sec
0005AAr 3  60               rts
0005ABr 3               
0005ABr 3               ;--------------------------
0005ABr 3               GETDIG:                             ; y is index to next char in NXTTOK
0005ABr 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK),y
0005ADr 3  38               sec
0005AEr 3  E9 30            sbc #ASCII_0
0005B0r 3  90 10            bcc GETDIG_ERR
0005B2r 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
0005B4r 3  90 0A            bcc GETDIG_RTN
0005B6r 3  E9 07            sbc #7
0005B8r 3  C9 0A            cmp #10
0005BAr 3  90 06            bcc GETDIG_ERR
0005BCr 3  C9 10            cmp #16
0005BEr 3  B0 02            bcs GETDIG_ERR
0005C0r 3               GETDIG_RTN:                         ; pass carry clear for good digit
0005C0r 3  18               clc
0005C1r 3  60               rts
0005C2r 3               GETDIG_ERR:                         ; pass carry set for no digit
0005C2r 3  38               sec
0005C3r 3  60               rts
0005C4r 3               ;  end of new number conv
0005C4r 3               ;
0005C4r 3  27 10        H2NUM: .byte $27,$10
0005C6r 3  03 E8         .byte $03,$E8
0005C8r 3  00 64         .byte $00,$64
0005CAr 3  00 0A         .byte $00,$0A
0005CCr 3               HEX2DEC:                            ; low/high in A,Y - use X, TEMP1, TEMP3, TEMP4, TEMP6
0005CCr 3  84 F5            sty TEMP3+1
0005CEr 3  85 F4            sta TEMP3
0005D0r 3  A2 00            ldx #0
0005D2r 3               H2DDIV10:
0005D2r 3  BD rr rr         lda H2NUM,x
0005D5r 3  85 F7            sta TEMP4+1
0005D7r 3  E8               inx
0005D8r 3  BD rr rr         lda H2NUM,x
0005DBr 3  85 F6            sta TEMP4
0005DDr 3  E8               inx
0005DEr 3  64 FD            stz TEMP6
0005E0r 3               H2DLOOP:
0005E0r 3  A5 F5            lda TEMP3+1

0005E4r 3  90 19            bcc  H2DSK1
0005E6r 3  D0 06            bne  H2DSK0
0005E8r 3  A5 F4            lda TEMP3
0005EAr 3  C5 F6            cmp TEMP4
0005ECr 3  90 11            bcc  H2DSK1
0005EEr 3               H2DSK0:
0005EEr 3  A5 F4            lda TEMP3
0005F0r 3  38               sec
0005F1r 3  E5 F6            sbc TEMP4
0005F3r 3  85 F4            sta TEMP3
0005F5r 3  A5 F5            lda TEMP3+1
0005F7r 3  E5 F7            sbc TEMP4+1
0005F9r 3  85 F5            sta TEMP3+1
0005FBr 3  E6 FD            inc TEMP6
0005FDr 3  80 E1            bra H2DLOOP
0005FFr 3               H2DSK1:
0005FFr 3  A5 FD            lda TEMP6
000601r 3  18               clc
000602r 3  69 30            adc #ASCII_0
000604r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
000606r 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1+1
000608r 3  DA               phx
000609r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0                      ; remember!  A/X both destroyed with push and pull!
00060Cr 3  FA               plx
00060Dr 3  E0 08            cpx #8
00060Fr 3  F0 03            beq H2DFIN
000611r 3  4C rr rr         jmp H2DDIV10

000619r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1

000620r 3  60               rts

0007F3r 3               ;
0007F3r 3               ;    load mainoff (TEMP1 $F0), ramstart (TEMP3 $F4), and endsoff (TEMP2 $F2)
0007F3r 3               ;     with current ROM offsets (main = start, ends = finish)
0007F3r 3               ;     and ramstart with destination corresponding to main.
0007F3r 3               ;     Set 'supprint' <> 0 (TEMP5 $FC) to suppress printing of progress bar.
0007F3r 3               ;
0007F3r 3               MEMCPY:
0007F3r 3  A0 00            ldy  #0
0007F5r 3               COPYLOOP:
0007F5r 3  B1 F0            lda (mainoff),y
0007F7r 3  91 F4            sta (ramstart),y
0007F9r 3  A5 F0            lda mainoff
0007FBr 3  C5 F2            cmp endsoff
0007FDr 3  D0 06            bne SKIP1
0007FFr 3  A5 F1            lda mainoff+1
000801r 3  C5 F3            cmp endsoff+1
000803r 3  F0 17            beq CPRTS
000805r 3               SKIP1:
000805r 3  A5 FC            lda supprint
000807r 3  D0 05            bne SKIP01
000809r 3  A9 2E 20 rr      PRINT_CHAR #ASCII_PERIOD
00080Dr 3  rr           
00080Er 3               SKIP01:
00080Er 3  E6 F0            inc mainoff
000810r 3  D0 02            bne SKIP2
000812r 3  E6 F1            inc mainoff + 1
000814r 3               SKIP2:
000814r 3  E6 F4            inc ramstart
000816r 3  D0 02            bne SKIP3
000818r 3  E6 F5            inc ramstart+1
00081Ar 3               SKIP3:
00081Ar 3  80 D9            bra COPYLOOP
00081Cr 3               CPRTS:
00081Cr 3  60               rts
00081Dr 3               ; ---------------- end of upper.s
00081Dr 3               ;
00081Dr 3               
00081Dr 2               UPPER_END:
00081Dr 2               ;
00081Dr 2               ;
00081Dr 2               YSOUND_START:

00081Dr 2               ;   .include "sound_os.s"
00081Dr 2               ;.endif
00081Dr 2               YSOUND_END:

000222r 3  A0 03            ldy #3
000224r 3               SZLOOP:
000224r 3  B1 F2            lda (TEMP2),y
000226r 3  F0 06            beq SZEND

00022Br 3  C8               iny
00022Cr 3  80 F6            bra SZLOOP
00022Er 3               SZEND:
00022Er 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
000231r 3               ;
000231r 3               ; ( addr n -- w0 w1 ... w(n-1) )  push n words from memory to stack
000231r 3               ;
000231r 3  rr rr 07 64  def_word "dsgetn@", "dsgetn", 0
000235r 3  73 67 65 74  
000239r 3  6E 40        
00023Br 3  A0 F6            ldy #TEMP4

000240r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1  ; get ptr addr
000243r 3  20 rr rr         jsr MEMLEN   ; returns TEMP3 w/maddr, length in TEMP1
000246r 3  A5 F6            lda TEMP4
000248r 3  0A               asl a
000249r 3  85 F6            sta TEMP4
00024Br 3  C5 F0            cmp TEMP1
00024Dr 3  B0 02            bcs DSGETNSK
00024Fr 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
000251r 3               DSGETNSK:
000251r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000253r 3  C9 78            cmp #$78
000255r 3  90 03            bcc DSGETNSK2
000257r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DSGETERR
00025Ar 3               DSGETNSK2:
00025Ar 3  18               clc
00025Br 3  69 03            adc #3
00025Dr 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
00025Fr 3  A0 03            ldy #3
000261r 3  4C rr rr         jmp DSGLOOP
000264r 3               ;
000264r 3               ; ( addr -- w w ... w )  push words from memory to stack
000264r 3               ;
000264r 3  rr rr 06 64  def_word "dsget@", "dsget", 0

00026Cr 3  40           
00026Dr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1    ; get addr on memstack
000270r 3  20 rr rr         jsr MEMLEN     ; maddr in TEMP3, length in TEMP1
000273r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1

000279r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
00027Br 3  D0 1F            bne DSGETERR    ; too much data, can't push this on
00027Dr 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
00027Fr 3  18               clc
000280r 3  69 03            adc #3
000282r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1

000288r 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2

00028Dr 3  85 F3            sta TEMP2+1
00028Fr 3  C8               iny

0002A0r 3  4C rr rr         jmp errrtn
0002A3r 3               ;
0002A3r 3               ; ( w w w..len addr -- addr)    stores len BYTES (words * 2) from stack
0002A3r 3               ;
0002A3r 3  rr rr 06 64  def_word "dwstk!", "dwstkstore", 0

0002B2r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_1      ; and push ptr addr back on stack
0002B5r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
0002B8r 3

0002B8r 3  A0 00            ldy #0            ; count up
0002BAr 3               DWFLOOP:

0002BCr 3  38               sec
0002BDr 3  E9 7E            sbc #DSEND
0002BFr 3  B0 13            bcs DW_FWDEND
0002C1r 3  5A               phy
0002C2r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0
0002C5r 3  7A               ply
0002C6r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
0002C8r 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
0002CAr 3  C8               iny
0002CBr 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
0002CDr 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
0002CFr 3  C8               iny
0002D0r 3  C4 FC            cpy TEMP5
0002D2r 3  D0 E6            bne DWFLOOP
0002D4r 3               DW_FWDEND:
0002D4r 3  60               rts
0002D5r 3               ;
0002D5r 3               ;
0002D5r 3               ; ( 0c 0c 0c...len addr -- addr)    stores len chars from stack
0002D5r 3               ;
0002D5r 3  rr rr 06 64  def_word "dcstk!", "dcstkstore", 0

0002E7r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
0002EAr 3

0002EAr 3  A0 00            ldy #0            ; count up
0002ECr 3               HSRLOOP:

0002EEr 3  38               sec
0002EFr 3  E9 7E            sbc #DSEND
0002F1r 3  B0 0E            bcs HS_FWDEND
0002F3r 3  5A               phy
0002F4r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0
0002F7r 3  7A               ply
0002F8r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
0002FAr 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
0002FCr 3  C8               iny
0002FDr 3  C4 FC            cpy TEMP5
0002FFr 3  D0 EB            bne HSRLOOP
000301r 3               HS_FWDEND:
000301r 3  60               rts
000302r 3               ;
000302r 3               ; ( 0c 0c 0c...len addr -- addr)  stores len chars from stack, reverse order
000302r 3               ;
000302r 3  rr rr 07 72  def_word "rdcstk!", "rdstkstore", 0
000306r 3  64 63 73 74  
00030Ar 3  6B 21        
00030Cr 3  20 rr rr         jsr HSSETUP
00030Fr 3  20 rr rr         jsr HS_STREV
000312r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_1      ; and push ptr addr back on stack
000315r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
000318r 3               
000318r 3               HSSETUP:
000318r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1     ; get addr from malloc run -> TEMP2
00031Br 3  20 rr rr         jsr MEMLEN      ; length in TEMP1, maddr TEMP3
00031Er 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000320r 3  85 FC            sta TEMP5
000322r 3  A5 F4            lda TEMP3
000324r 3  18               clc
000325r 3  69 03            adc #3          ; calc offset to storage
000327r 3  85 F4            sta TEMP3
000329r 3  90 02            bcc HSSETUPEND
00032Br 3  E6 F5            inc TEMP3+1
00032Dr 3               HSSETUPEND:
00032Dr 3  60               rts
00032Er 3               
00032Er 3               HS_STREV:
00032Er 3  A4 FC            ldy TEMP5            ; count down from length
000330r 3  A9 00            lda #0
000332r 3  88               dey
000333r 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
000335r 3               HSFLOOP:
000335r 3  A5 E8            lda DSPTR
000337r 3  38               sec
000338r 3  E9 7E            sbc #DSEND
00033Ar 3  B0 0C            bcs HS_REVEND
00033Cr 3  5A               phy
00033Dr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0
000340r 3  7A               ply
000341r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000343r 3  88               dey
000344r 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
000346r 3  D0 ED            bne HSFLOOP
000348r 3               HS_REVEND:
000348r 3  60               rts
000349r 3               ;
000349r 3               ;   replace leading zeros with spcs when creating decimal ascii string
000349r 3               ;
000349r 3               DECSFINISH:
000349r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
00034Br 3               DECSCLRLOOP:             ; replace $00 or leading $30 with spaces
00034Br 3  B1 F4            lda (TEMP3),y
00034Dr 3  F0 06            beq  DECSTSK02
00034Fr 3  C9 30            cmp #ASCII_0
000351r 3  F0 02            beq  DECSTSK02
000353r 3  80 07            bra  DECSTDONE
000355r 3               DECSTSK02:
000355r 3  A9 20            lda #ASCII_SPACE
000357r 3  91 F4            sta (TEMP3),y
000359r 3  C8               iny
00035Ar 3  80 EF            bra DECSCLRLOOP
00035Cr 3               DECSTDONE:
00035Cr 3  60               rts
00035Dr 3               ;
00035Dr 3               ;
00035Dr 3               ;
00035Dr 3  rr rr 05 64  def_word "decs!", "decstore", 0
000361r 3  65 63 73 21

00036Br 3  20 rr rr         jsr DECSFINISH
00036Er 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_1      ; and push ptr addr back on stack
000371r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
000374r 3               ;
000374r 3               ;
000374r 3               extensions:
000374r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000374r 3               ; ( w n -- w >> n ) -- shift right
000374r 3  rr rr 02 3E  def_word ">>", "shr", 0
000378r 3  3E           
000379r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1
00037Cr 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
00037Er 3  F0 0E            beq SRZERO
000380r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0
000383r 3               SRLOOP:
000383r 3  46 F1            lsr TEMP1 + 1
000385r 3  66 F0            ror TEMP1
000387r 3  C6 F2            dec TEMP2
000389r 3  D0 F8            bne SRLOOP
00038Br 3  4C rr rr         jmp this         ; 'this' includes jsr spush_0 and next
00038Er 3               SRZERO:
00038Er 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
000391r 3               
000391r 3               ; ( w n -- w << n ) -- shift left
000391r 3  rr rr 02 3C  def_word "<<", "shl", 0
000395r 3  3C           
000396r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1
000399r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
00039Br 3  F0 0E            beq SLZERO
00039Dr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0
0003A0r 3               SLLOOP:
0003A0r 3  06 F0            asl TEMP1
0003A2r 3  26 F1            rol TEMP1 + 1
0003A4r 3  C6 F2            dec TEMP2
0003A6r 3  D0 F8            bne SLLOOP
0003A8r 3  4C rr rr         jmp this         ; 'this' includes jsr spush_0 and next
0003ABr 3               SLZERO:
0003ABr 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
0003AEr 3               
0003AEr 3               ;--------------- bit test/set/clear ----------------------------------
0003AEr 3               ; ( n b -- t? )
0003AEr 3  rr rr 04 74  def_word "tbit", "tbit", 0            ; nondestructive test bit
0003B2r 3  62 69 74     
0003B5r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1     ; which bit
0003B8r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
0003BAr 3  29 0F            and #$0F        ; only want 0-16
0003BCr 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
0003BEr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0
0003C1r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0     ; backup!
0003C4r 3  20 rr rr         jsr BITWIND
0003C7r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1

0003CBr 3  F0 08            beq TBCLR      ; test bit 0
0003CDr 3  A9 FF            lda #$FF
0003CFr 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
0003D1r 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1

0003D7r 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1+1
0003D9r 3               TBITEND:
0003D9r 3  4C rr rr         jmp this
0003DCr 3               
0003DCr 3               ; ( n b -- ns )
0003DCr 3  rr rr 04 73  def_word "sbit", "sbit", 0             ; set bit
0003E0r 3  62 69 74

0003E6r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2

0003EAr 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
0003ECr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0

0003F2r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1

0003F6r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1

0003FEr 3               
0003FEr 3               ; ( n b -- nc )
0003FEr 3  rr rr 04 63  def_word "cbit", "cbit", 0
000402r 3  62 69 74     
000405r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1     ; which bit
000408r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
00040Ar 3  29 0F            and #$0F        ; only want 0-16
00040Cr 3  85 F2            sta TEMP2
00040Er 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0
000411r 3  20 rr rr         jsr BITWIND
000414r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1

000418r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
00041Ar 3  20 rr rr         jsr BITUNWIND
00041Dr 3  4C rr rr         jmp this
000420r 3               
000420r 3               BITWIND:
000420r 3  64 F4            stz TEMP3
000422r 3  64 F5            stz TEMP3+1

000428r 3               BITWLOOP:
000428r 3  46 F1            lsr TEMP1+1

000434r 3

000443r 3               BITUWSKIP:
000443r 3  60               rts

000444r 3               ; start of dictionary
000444r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000444r 3               core_dict:
000444r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000444r 3               ; ( -- u ) ; tos + 1 unchanged
000444r 3  rr rr 03 6B  def_word "key", "key", 0
000448r 3  65 79        
00044Ar 3               KEYRDLP:

00044Dr 3  90 FB            bcc KEYRDLP
00044Fr 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1

000454r 3

000454r 3               ; ( u -- ) ; tos + 1 unchanged
000454r 3  rr rr 04 65  def_word "emit", "emit", 0

00045Er 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000460r 3  20 rr rr         PRINT_CHAR
000463r 3  4C rr rr         jmp next
000466r 3               
000466r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000466r 3               ; ( w1 w2 -- NOT(w1 AND w2) )
000466r 3  rr rr 04 6E  def_word "nand", "nand", 0
00046Ar 3  61 6E 64     
00046Dr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1             ; load TEMP2, TEMP1 from stack
000470r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0
000473r 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
000475r 3  25 F0            and TEMP1
000477r 3  49 FF            eor #$FF            ; toggles FIRST byte okay, but...
000479r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
00047Br 3  A5 F3            lda TEMP2 + 1
00047Dr 3  25 F1            and TEMP1 + 1
00047Fr 3  49 FF            eor #$FF           ; and then second.
000481r 3                                      ; sta TEMP1+1 at 'keeps', then jsr spush_0 and 'next'
000481r 3  4C rr rr         jmp keeps
000484r 3               
000484r 3               ;---------------------PLUS and MINUS----------------------------------
000484r 3               ; ( w1 w2 -- w1+w2 )
000484r 3  rr rr 01 2B  def_word "+", "plus", 0
000488r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1        ; load TEMP2 from stack
00048Br 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0        ; then TEMP1
00048Er 3  18               clc
00048Fr 3  A5 F2            lda TEMP2
000491r 3  65 F0            adc TEMP1
000493r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
000495r 3  A5 F3            lda TEMP2+1
000497r 3  65 F1            adc TEMP1+1
000499r 3                                   ; 'keeps' = sta TEMP1+1; jsr spush_0; jmp next
000499r 3  4C rr rr         jmp keeps
00049Cr 3               
00049Cr 3               ; ( w1 w2 -- w1-w2 )
00049Cr 3  rr rr 01 2D  def_word "-", "minus", 0
0004A0r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1        ; get TEMP 2 from stack
0004A3r 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0        ; then TEMP 1
0004A6r 3  38               sec
0004A7r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
0004A9r 3  E5 F2            sbc TEMP2
0004ABr 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
0004ADr 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
0004AFr 3  E5 F3            sbc TEMP2+1
0004B1r 3                                  ; 'keeps' = sta TEMP1+1; jsr spush_0; jmp next
0004B1r 3  18               clc            ; not sure why, but just in case?
0004B2r 3  4C rr rr         jmp keeps
0004B5r 3               
0004B5r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0004B5r 3               ; ( 0 -- $0000) | ( n -- $FFFF)  normalize boolean - change TOS to $0000 if false, $FFFF if TRUE
0004B5r 3  rr rr 04 62  def_word "bool", "normbool", 0
0004B9r 3  6F 6F 6C     
0004BCr 3  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0

0004C3r 3  F0 19            beq PUSHFALSE
0004C5r 3  4C rr rr         jmp PUSHTRUE
0004C8r 3               ;
0004C8r 3               ; ( -- $FFFF )
0004C8r 3  rr rr 04 54  def_word "TRUE", "istrue", 0
0004CCr 3  52 55 45     
0004CFr 3               PUSHTRUE:
0004CFr 3  A9 FF            lda #$FF
0004D1r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
0004D3r 3  4C rr rr         jmp keeps
0004D6r 3               ;
0004D6r 3               ; ( -- $0000 )
0004D6r 3  rr rr 05 46  def_word "FALSE", "isfalse", 0
0004DAr 3  41 4C 53 45  
0004DEr 3               PUSHFALSE:
0004DEr 3  64 F0            stz TEMP1
0004E0r 3  64 F1            stz TEMP1+1
0004E2r 3  4C rr rr         jmp this
0004E5r 3               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
0004E5r 3               ; ( -- state ) pushes addr of status word on stack

0004ECr 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1
0004EEr 3  A9 00            lda #>STATUS
0004F0r 3  4C rr rr         jmp keeps   ; pushes addr of status word on stack?  keeps includes sta TEMP+1 etc.
0004F3r 3

000501r 3  A9 01            lda #1

00050Cr 2               ;
00050Cr 2               ;
00050Cr 2               ;------------ CRITICAL CORE PRIMITIVES (AGSB and PGS) ----------------
00050Cr 2               ;
00050Cr 2               ;   COMPILE (:), FINISH (;), FETCH (@), STORE (!)
00050Cr 2               ;      including:  keeps, this, next, finish
00050Cr 2               ;       ...and other stuff.
00050Cr 2               ;
00050Cr 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00050Cr 2               ; ( a -- ) execute a jump to a reference at top of data stack
00050Cr 2  rr rr 04 65  def_word "exec", "exec", 0
000510r 2  78 65 63     
000513r 2  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0
000516r 2  6C F0 00         jmp (TEMP1)        ; assumes an address on top of DS
000519r 2               
000519r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000519r 2               ; ( -- ) execute a jump to a reference at IP
000519r 2  rr rr 02 3A  def_word ":$", "docode", 0
00051Dr 2  24           
00051Er 2  6C EC 00         jmp (INSTPTR)                 ;  DOCODE (thus the ':')
000521r 2               
000521r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000521r 2               ; ( -- ) execute a jump to next
000521r 2  rr rr 02 3B  def_word ";$", "donext", 0        ;  'next' (thus the ';')
000525r 2  24           
000526r 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
000529r 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
000529r 2               ;
000529r 2               ; ( w a -- ) ; [a] = w    (w is word, a is an address)
000529r 2  rr rr 01 21  def_word "!", "store", 0
00052Dr 2               storew:
00052Dr 2  20 rr rr         jsr spull_1             ; get address, store in TEMP2
000530r 2  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0             ; get data, store in TEMP1
000533r 2  A2 F2            ldx #TEMP2              ;  [a]
000535r 2  A0 F0            ldy #TEMP1              ;   w
000537r 2  20 rr rr         jsr copyinto            ; copy TEMP2 stuff to addr in TEMP1 (opposite of @)..
00053Ar 2  4C rr rr         jmp next                ;            ...(see below)
00053Dr 2               ;
00053Dr 2               ;--------------------------------------------FETCH--------------------
00053Dr 2               ; ( a -- w ) ; w = [a]
00053Dr 2  rr rr 01 40  def_word "@", "fetch", 0      ; replace addr of data on top of DS, with data pointed to
000541r 2               fetchw:
000541r 2  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0             ; get addr from DS
000544r 2  A2 F0            ldx #TEMP1
000546r 2  A0 F2            ldy #TEMP2
000548r 2  20 rr rr         jsr copyfrom            ; copies data from [TEMP1] => TEMP2
00054Br 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00054Br 2               ;            NEXT entry point for many AGSB primitives
00054Br 2               ;---------------------------------------------------------------------
00054Br 2               copys:                      ; copy from cell at y (zp) to TEMP1
00054Br 2  B9 00 00         lda 0, y
00054Er 2  85 F0            sta TEMP1
000550r 2  B9 01 00         lda 1, y
000553r 2               keeps:                      ; saves bytes since have to get here anyway
000553r 2  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
000555r 2               this:                       ; same as above
000555r 2  20 rr rr         jsr spush_0             ; then push back on stack
000558r 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
00055Br 2               ;
00055Br 2               ;-----------------------IMMEDIATE, '[', ']', ','-----------------------
00055Br 2  rr rr 01 49  def_word "I", "Imm", 0
00055Fr 2               wimm:                        ; jmp here if proccessing a compiled
00055Fr 2  A5 E5            lda LASTHEAP+1           ;   word that needs to run 'immediate'.
000561r 2  85 CD            sta TEMP9+1
000563r 2  A5 E4            lda LASTHEAP             ; get addr of 'last' compiled word, copy to TEMP4, add 2
000565r 2  18               clc
000566r 2  69 02            adc #2                   ; ..to find where length byte is...
000568r 2  85 CC            sta TEMP9
00056Ar 2  90 02            bcc IMMSKIP
00056Cr 2  E6 CD            inc TEMP9+1
00056Er 2               IMMSKIP:
00056Er 2  A0 00            ldy #0
000570r 2  B1 CC            lda (TEMP9),y
000572r 2  09 80            ora #$80                 ; ...set bit 7 and store
000574r 2  91 CC            sta (TEMP9),y
000576r 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
000579r 2               ;
000579r 2  rr rr 81 5B  def_word "[", "leftbrack", FLAG_IMM       ; switch to 'interpret'
00057Dr 2  64 E0            stz STATUS
00057Fr 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
000582r 2               ;
000582r 2  rr rr 01 5D  def_word "]", "rtbrack", 0               ; switch back to 'compile'
000586r 2  A9 01            lda #1
000588r 2  85 E0            sta STATUS
00058Ar 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
00058Dr 2               ;
00058Dr 2               ;
00058Dr 2                                                        ; NOTE: 'lit' or similar will have pushed a value
00058Dr 2                                                        ; or address onto stack; 'comma' stores it inline in word
00058Dr 2                                                        ;
00058Dr 2  rr rr 01 2C  def_word ",", "xcomma", 0                ; pull data from top of stack, store at 'here', adjust
000591r 2  20 rr rr         jsr spull_0                          ; 'here' to point at next cell
000594r 2  A0 00            ldy #0
000596r 2  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
000598r 2  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP),y                     ; POP DS TO TEMP1, [here] = TEMP1
00059Ar 2  C8               iny
00059Br 2  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
00059Dr 2  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP),y
00059Fr 2  A2 E6            ldx #NEXTHEAP                        ; here += 2
0005A1r 2  A9 02            lda #2
0005A3r 2  20 rr rr         jsr addwx
0005A6r 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
0005A9r 2               
0005A9r 2               ;--------------------------------------------SEMIS-----------------
0005A9r 2  rr rr 81 3B  def_word ";", "semis",  FLAG_IMM
0005ADr 2  A5 FA            lda BACKHEAP
0005AFr 2  85 E4            sta LASTHEAP                ; bring back BACKHEAP to LASTHEAP
0005B1r 2  A5 FB            lda BACKHEAP + 1
0005B3r 2  85 E5            sta LASTHEAP + 1
0005B5r 2               
0005B5r 2  64 E0            stz STATUS                  ; set status to 'interpret' (presumably from 'compile')
0005B7r 2               
0005B7r 2               finish:                         ; compiled words must end with exit
0005B7r 2  A9 rr            lda #<exit
0005B9r 2  85 EE            sta WORKREG
0005BBr 2  A9 rr            lda #>exit
0005BDr 2  85 EF            sta WORKREG + 1
0005BFr 2  20 rr rr         jsr wcomma                  ; change NEXTHEAP to point to addr of 'exit',
0005C2r 2                                               ; and make sure is last entry in code table for word...
0005C2r 2                                               ; as all good Forth compiled words should do.
0005C2r 2  4C rr rr         jmp next
0005C5r 2               ;
0005C5r 2               ;
0005C5r 2               ;------------------------------------------COMPILE------------------
0005C5r 2  rr rr 01 3A  def_word ":", "colon", 0
0005C9r 2  A5 E6            lda NEXTHEAP
0005CBr 2  85 FA            sta BACKHEAP                ; backup NEXTHEAP to BACKHEAP
0005CDr 2  A5 E7            lda NEXTHEAP + 1
0005CFr 2  85 FB            sta BACKHEAP + 1
0005D1r 2               
0005D1r 2  A9 01            lda #1                      ; set status to 'compile'
0005D3r 2  85 E0            sta STATUS
0005D5r 2               
0005D5r 2               COMPHEADER:
0005D5r 2               ; copy LASTHEAP into (NEXTHEAP)
0005D5r 2  A0 E4            ldy #LASTHEAP
0005D7r 2  20 rr rr         jsr comma                    ; change NEXTHEAP to point to LASTHEAP  ('here' <= 'last')
0005DAr 2  20 rr rr         jsr token                    ; get first token, the name of new word
0005DDr 2  A0 00            ldy #0                       ; copy it to heap: length and name
0005DFr 2                                                ; code field comes with later proc
0005DFr 2               COMPLOOP:
0005DFr 2  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK), y
0005E1r 2  C9 20            cmp #ASCII_SPACE
0005E3r 2  F0 0B            beq COMPEND
0005E5r 2  C0 00            cpy #0                       ; if this is the length field...
0005E7r 2  D0 02            bne COMCOPY
0005E9r 2  09 40            ora #$40                     ; set bit six for 'compiled' word
0005EBr 2               COMCOPY:
0005EBr 2  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP), y
0005EDr 2  C8               iny
0005EEr 2  D0 EF            bne COMPLOOP
0005F0r 2               COMPEND:
0005F0r 2  98               tya                          ; and update NEXTHEAP  :  'here' incremented by length
0005F1r 2  A2 E6            ldx #(NEXTHEAP)
0005F3r 2  20 rr rr         jsr addwx                   ; 'here' now at CFA
0005F6r 2               
0005F6r 2               ;~~~~~~~~ all done....
0005F6r 2  4C rr rr         jmp next                     ; and then see below; compiled word will continue until ';'
0005F9r 2               ;
0005F9r 2               ;---------------------ADD IN EXTRA HARDCODED LOGIC / NUMERALS / ETC--

0005F9r 2                  .include "hywords.s"

000703r 3               
000703r 3               VCHEAD:
000703r 3               ; copy LASTHEAP into (NEXTHEAP)
000703r 3  A0 E4            ldy #LASTHEAP
000705r 3  20 rr rr         jsr comma                    ; change NEXTHEAP to point to LASTHEAP  ('here' <= 'last')
000708r 3  20 rr rr         jsr token                    ; get first token, the name of new word
00070Br 3  A0 00            ldy #0                       ; copy it to heap: length and name
00070Dr 3                                                ; code field comes with later proc
00070Dr 3               VCLOOP:
00070Dr 3  B1 F8            lda (NXTTOK), y
00070Fr 3  C9 20            cmp #ASCII_SPACE                    ; copy in length and name
000711r 3  F0 05            beq VCMID
000713r 3               VCCOPY:

000715r 3  C8               iny
000716r 3  D0 F5            bne VCLOOP
000718r 3               VCMID:
000718r 3  98               tya                          ; and update NEXTHEAP  :  'here' incremented by length
000719r 3  A2 E6            ldx #(NEXTHEAP)
00071Br 3  20 rr rr         jsr addwx                   ; 'here' now at CFA
00071Er 3               
00071Er 3               
00071Er 3  A2 14            ldx #(VCEND0-VCCODE)        ; calc offset
000720r 3  A0 00            ldy #0
000722r 3               VCLOOP2:
000722r 3  B9 rr rr         lda VCCODE, y
000725r 3  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP),y
000727r 3  C8               iny
000728r 3  CA               dex
000729r 3  D0 F7            bne VCLOOP2
00072Br 3  5A               phy                        ; save for later
00072Cr 3  A5 E7            lda NEXTHEAP+1
00072Er 3  85 F1            sta TEMP1+1
000730r 3  A5 E6            lda NEXTHEAP
000732r 3  18               clc
000733r 3  69 0F            adc #15                     ; calc addresses for indirect data
000735r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1                   ; read.
000737r 3  90 02            bcc VCSKIP00
000739r 3  E6 F1            inc TEMP1+1
00073Br 3               VCSKIP00:                      ; update addresses for lda's
00073Br 3  A0 03            ldy #3                     ; <VCDATA
00073Dr 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
00073Fr 3  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP),y
000741r 3  A0 07            ldy #7                     ; >VCDATA
000743r 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1
000745r 3  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP),y
000747r 3  A9 00            lda #0
000749r 3  A0 0F            ldy #15
00074Br 3  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP),y          ; store zero in data word
00074Dr 3  C8               iny

000750r 3               
000750r 3  68               pla                        ; to update

000756r 3               VCFINISH:

00078Fr 3               ; copy LASTHEAP into (NEXTHEAP)
00078Fr 3  A0 E4            ldy #LASTHEAP
000791r 3  20 rr rr         jsr comma                    ; change NEXTHEAP to point to LASTHEAP  ('here' <= 'last')

000799r 3                                                ; code field comes with later proc

0007AAr 3                                               ; copy in code from CCCODE ('docon')
0007AAr 3  A2 16            ldx #(CCEND0-CCCODE)        ; calc offset
0007ACr 3  A0 00            ldy #0

0007B1r 3  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP),y

0007C1r 3  85 F0            sta TEMP1                   ; read.
0007C3r 3  90 02            bcc CCSKIP00
0007C5r 3  E6 F1            inc TEMP1+1
0007C7r 3               CCSKIP00:
0007C7r 3  A0 03            ldy #3                     ; offset to lda CCDATA
0007C9r 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
0007CBr 3  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP),y
0007CDr 3  C8               iny
0007CEr 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1

0007D6r 3  E6 F1            inc TEMP1+1
0007D8r 3               CCSKIP01:

0007DAr 3  A5 F0            lda TEMP1
0007DCr 3  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP),y
0007DEr 3  C8               iny
0007DFr 3  A5 F1            lda TEMP1+1

0007E5r 3  A0 11            ldy #17

0007EBr 3  C8               iny
0007ECr 3  91 E6            sta (NEXTHEAP),y
0007EEr 3                            ; pull back offset from above
0007EEr 3  68               pla                        ; update NEXTHEAP
0007EFr 3  A2 E6            ldx #NEXTHEAP
0007F1r 3  20 rr rr         jsr addwx
0007F4r 3               CCFINISH:
0007F4r 3  A5 FA            lda BACKHEAP

000804r 3               ;
000804r 3               ;   cons copies this in and 'customizes it' by
000804r 3               ;   plugging in corrected address for CCDATA
000804r 3               ;
000804r 3               ;  'DOCONS'
000804r 3               CCCODE:
000804r 3  89 00            bit #00

000817r 3               CCEND:

000996r 3               ; ( w1 w2 -- w1 AND w2 )
000996r 3  rr rr 03 61  def_word "and", "xand", 0   ; had to use 'xand' cuz 'and' used for something important...
00099Ar 3  6E 64

000EC9r 3               ;

000EE4r 3               d16skip0:

0013A3r 2               .include "bload.s"
0013A3r 3               ;
0013A3r 3               ;  BLOAD test code
0013A3r 3               ;
0013A3r 3               ;  ALL binary libraries loadable with 'bload' MUST be Forth words,
0013A3r 3               ;   (not jsr subroutines), fully relocatable.  That means NO jumps
0013A3r 3               ;   or branches to labels!  Calculate all jumps and branches accordingly
0013A3r 3               ;   and use numercal offsets.  jsr's and jmp's to core primitive labels
0013A3r 3               ;   (next, keeps, this, etc) or ROM locations will work fine.
0013A3r 3               ;
0013A3r 3               ;  Format below must be rigidly followed, with length of name followed
0013A3r 3               ;   by that many bytes for the name.  'bit #0' ($89 $00) must be
0013A3r 3               ;   the first instruction in any native code word loaded past
0013A3r 3               ;   'exit'.  Termination must be $00 $00 for each word, with "END"
0013A3r 3               ;   to designate the end of a 'library'.
0013A3r 3               ;
0013A3r 3               test00:
0013A3r 3  06           .byte 6
0013A4r 3  42 4C 74 65  .byte "BLtest"
0013A8r 3  73 74        
0013AAr 3  22                SKIPNEXT
0013ABr 3  00                .byte 0
0013ACr 3  20 rr rr          PRINT_CRLF
0013AFr 3  A9 54 20 rr       PRINT_CHAR #'T', #'E', #'S', #'T'
0013B3r 3  rr A9 45 20  
0013B7r 3  rr rr A9 53  
0013C3r 3  20 rr rr          PRINT_CRLF
0013C6r 3  4C rr rr          jmp next
0013C9r 3  00 00        .byte 0, 0
0013CBr 3  07           .byte 7
0013CCr 3  42 4C 74 65  .byte "BLtest2"
0013D0r 3  73 74 32     
0013D3r 3  22                SKIPNEXT
0013D4r 3  00                .byte 0
0013D5r 3  20 rr rr          PRINT_CRLF
0013D8r 3  A9 54 20 rr       PRINT_CHAR #'T', #'E', #'S', #'T', #'Y'
0013DCr 3  rr A9 45 20  
0013E0r 3  rr rr A9 53  
0013F1r 3  20 rr rr          PRINT_CRLF
0013F4r 3  4C rr rr          jmp next
0013F7r 3  00 00        .byte 0, 0
0013F9r 3  45 4E 44     .byte "END"
