0013FCr 1               .include "wozmon.s"
0013FCr 2               ;
0013FCr 2               ;   Taken mostly from Steve Wozniak's Apple 1 Monitor for the 6502, or WOZMON
0013FCr 2               ;
0013FCr 2               .debuginfo
0013FCr 2               .segment "BUFFERS"
000500r 2               IN:
000500r 2  xx xx xx xx                  .res            $100
000504r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000508r 2  xx xx xx xx  
000600r 2               
000600r 2               .segment "WOZMON"
000000r 2               ; WOZMON Entrypoint
000000r 2               MON_START:
000000r 2  D8                           cld                             ; Clear decimal arithmetic mode.
000001r 2  58                           cli                             ; Enable interrupts
000002r 2  64 rr                        stz             ZP_D_STATE      ; Byte mode, not DISAM mode
000004r 2  80 0B                        bra             @is_start
000006r 2               
000006r 2               @not_cr:
000006r 2  C9 08                        cmp             #ASCII_BACKSPACE
000008r 2  F0 0F                        beq             @is_backspace
00000Ar 2  C9 1B                        cmp             #ASCII_ESC
00000Cr 2  F0 03                        beq             @is_start
00000Er 2  C8                           iny                             ; Advance text index.
00000Fr 2  10 0B                        bpl             @get_next_char  ; Auto ESC if line longer than 127.
000011r 2               
000011r 2               @is_start:
000011r 2  20 rr rr                     PRINT_CRLF
000014r 2               
000014r 2               @get_line:
000014r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             WRITE_PROMPT
000017r 2  A0 01                        ldy             #1              ; Initialize text index.
000019r 2               
000019r 2               @is_backspace:
000019r 2  88                           dey                             ; Back up text index.
00001Ar 2  30 F8                        bmi             @get_line       ; Beyond start of line, reinitialize.
00001Cr 2               
00001Cr 2               @get_next_char:
00001Cr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             READ_CHAR
00001Fr 2  90 FB                        bcc             @get_next_char
000021r 2  99 rr rr                     sta             IN,y            ; Add to text buffer.
000024r 2  C9 0D                        cmp             #ASCII_CR
000026r 2  D0 DE                        bne             @not_cr
000028r 2  A0 FF                        ldy             #$FF            ; Reset text index.  Will iny shortly...
00002Ar 2  A9 00                        lda             #$00            ; For ZP_WM_XAM mode.
00002Cr 2  AA                           tax                             ; .X=0.
00002Dr 2               
00002Dr 2               @set_block:
00002Dr 2  0A                           asl
00002Er 2               
00002Er 2               @set_store:
00002Er 2  0A                           asl                             ; Leaves $7B if setting STOR mode.
00002Fr 2               
00002Fr 2               @set_mode:
00002Fr 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_MODE      ; $00 = ZP_WM_XAM, $74 = STOR, $B8 = BLOK ZP_WM_XAM.
000031r 2               
000031r 2               @skip_delim:
000031r 2  C8                           iny                             ; Advance text index.
000032r 2               
000032r 2               @next_item:
000032r 2  B9 rr rr                     lda             IN,y            ; Get character.
000035r 2  C9 0D                        cmp             #ASCII_CR       ; CR?
000037r 2  F0 DB                        beq             @get_line       ; Yes, done with this line.
000039r 2  C9 2E                        cmp             #ASCII_PERIOD
00003Br 2  90 F4                        bcc             @skip_delim     ; Skip delimiter.
00003Dr 2  F0 EE                        beq             @set_block      ; Set BLOCK ZP_WM_XAM mode.
00003Fr 2  C9 3A                        cmp             #ASCII_COLON
000041r 2  F0 EB                        beq             @set_store      ; Yes, set STOR mode.
000043r 2  C9 4D                        cmp             #ASCII_M
000045r 2  10 0A                        bpl             @check_rtow
000047r 2  C9 4B                        cmp             #ASCII_K
000049r 2  30 24                        bmi             @not_tuvw
00004Br 2  49 4B                        eor             #ASCII_K        ; 'K' resets DASTATE to 0
00004Dr 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_D_STATE
00004Fr 2  80 E0                        bra             @skip_delim
000051r 2               
000051r 2               @check_rtow:
000051r 2  C9 52                        cmp             #ASCII_R
000053r 2  F0 54                        beq             @run_prog
000055r 2  90 18                        bcc             @not_tuvw
000057r 2  C9 58                        cmp             #ASCII_X        ; R, S, T, U, V, or W
000059r 2  B0 14                        bcs             @not_tuvw
00005Br 2  C9 53                        cmp             #ASCII_S
00005Dr 2  D0 02                        bne             @not_spawn
00005Fr 2                               ;lda             ZP_WM_XAM
00005Fr 2                               ;ldy             ZP_WM_XAM + 1
00005Fr 2                               ;jsr             SPAWN_TASK
00005Fr 2  80 9F                        bra             MON_START
000061r 2               
000061r 2               @not_spawn:
000061r 2  18                           clc
000062r 2  69 9C                        adc             #($F0 - ASCII_T); T=FFF0, U=FFF1, V=FFF2, W=FFF3
000064r 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_HVP       ;
000066r 2  A9 FF                        lda             #$FF            ;
000068r 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_HVP + 1   ;
00006Ar 2  C8                           iny                             ; skip the mnemonic
00006Br 2  80 2C                        bra             @not_hex_or_escape
00006Dr 2               
00006Dr 2               @bra_is_start:
00006Dr 2  80 A2                        bra             @is_start       ; unrecognized
00006Fr 2               
00006Fr 2               @not_tuvw:
00006Fr 2  84 rr                        sty             ZP_Y_SAVE       ; Save Y for comparison
000071r 2  86 rr                        stx             ZP_WM_HVP       ; $00 -> Low byte of HPV
000073r 2  86 rr                        stx             ZP_WM_HVP + 1   ; ...and High byte.
000075r 2               
000075r 2               @next_hex:
000075r 2  B9 rr rr                     lda             IN,y            ; Get character for hex test.
000078r 2  49 30                        eor             #ASCII_0        ; Map digits to $0-9.
00007Ar 2  C9 0A                        cmp             #$0A            ; Digit?
00007Cr 2  90 06                        bcc             @is_digit       ; Yes.
00007Er 2  69 88                        adc             #$88            ; Map letter "A"-"F" to $FA-FF.
000080r 2  C9 FA                        cmp             #$FA            ; Hex letter?
000082r 2  90 11                        bcc             @not_hex        ; No, character not hex.
000084r 2               
000084r 2               @is_digit:
000084r 2  0A                           asl                             ; LSD to MSD of A.
000085r 2  0A                           asl
000086r 2  0A                           asl
000087r 2  0A                           asl
000088r 2  A2 04                        ldx             #4              ; Shift count.
00008Ar 2               
00008Ar 2               @hex_shift:
00008Ar 2  0A                           asl                             ; Hex digit left, MSB to carry.
00008Br 2  26 rr                        rol             ZP_WM_HVP       ; Rotate into LSD.
00008Dr 2  26 rr                        rol             ZP_WM_HVP + 1   ; Rotate into MSD's.
00008Fr 2  CA                           dex                             ; Done 4 shifts?
000090r 2  D0 F8                        bne             @hex_shift      ; No, loop.
000092r 2  C8                           iny                             ; Advance text index.
000093r 2  D0 E0                        bne             @next_hex       ; Always taken. Check next character for hex.
000095r 2               
000095r 2               @not_hex:
000095r 2  C4 rr                        cpy             ZP_Y_SAVE       ; Check if HPV empty (no hex digits).
000097r 2  F0 D4                        beq             @bra_is_start   ; Yes, back to start.
000099r 2               
000099r 2               @not_hex_or_escape:
000099r 2  24 rr                        bit             ZP_WM_MODE      ; Test ZP_WM_MODE byte.
00009Br 2  50 15                        bvc             @not_store      ; B6=0 is STOR, 1 is XAM and BLOCK XAM.
00009Dr 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_HVP       ; LSD's of hex data.
00009Fr 2  92 rr                        sta             (ZP_WM_ST)      ; Store to current 'store index'.
0000A1r 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_WM_ST        ; Increment store index.
0000A3r 2  D0 8D                        bne             @next_item      ; Get next item (no carry).
0000A5r 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_WM_ST + 1    ; Add carry to 'store index' high order.
0000A7r 2               
0000A7r 2               @to_next_item:
0000A7r 2  80 89                        bra             @next_item      ; Get next command item.
0000A9r 2               
0000A9r 2               @run_prog:
0000A9r 2  A9 rr 48 A9                  _M_JSRR         ZP_WM_XAM, MON_START
0000ADr 2  rr 48 6C rr  
0000B1r 2  rr           
0000B2r 2               
0000B2r 2               @not_store:
0000B2r 2  30 27                        bmi             @examine_next   ; B7 = 0 for XAM, 1 for BLOCK XAM.
0000B4r 2  A2 02                        ldx             #2              ; Byte count.
0000B6r 2               
0000B6r 2               @set_addr:
0000B6r 2  B5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_HVP - 1,x ; Copy hex data to
0000B8r 2  95 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_ST - 1,x  ;   'store index'.
0000BAr 2  95 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_XAM - 1,x ; And to 'ZP_WM_XAM index'.
0000BCr 2  CA                           dex                             ; Next of 2 bytes.
0000BDr 2  D0 F7                        bne             @set_addr       ; Loop unless X = 0.
0000BFr 2               
0000BFr 2               @print_next_addr:
0000BFr 2  20 rr rr                     PRINT_CRLF
0000C2r 2  A5 rr 20 rr                  PRINT_BYTE      ZP_WM_XAM + 1, ZP_WM_XAM   ; Print 'examine index'
0000C6r 2  rr A5 rr 20  
0000CAr 2  rr rr        
0000CCr 2  A9 3A 20 rr                  PRINT_CHAR      #ASCII_COLON    ; ...+ COLON
0000D0r 2  rr           
0000D1r 2               
0000D1r 2               @print_data:
0000D1r 2  18                           clc
0000D2r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_XAM
0000D4r 2  5A                           phy
0000D5r 2  A4 rr                        ldy             ZP_WM_XAM + 1
0000D7r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr             DISASM_AY       ; DISASM
0000DAr 2  7A                           ply
0000DBr 2               
0000DBr 2               @examine_next:
0000DBr 2  64 rr                        stz             ZP_WM_MODE      ; 0 -> ZP_WM_MODE (XAM mode).
0000DDr 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_XAM
0000DFr 2  C5 rr                        cmp             ZP_WM_HVP       ; Compare 'examine index' to hex data.
0000E1r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_XAM + 1
0000E3r 2  E5 rr                        sbc             ZP_WM_HVP + 1
0000E5r 2  B0 C0                        bcs             @to_next_item   ; Not less, so no more data to output.
0000E7r 2  38                           sec
0000E8r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_D_EXBYTES
0000EAr 2  65 rr                        adc             ZP_WM_XAM
0000ECr 2  85 rr                        sta             ZP_WM_XAM
0000EEr 2  90 02                        bcc             :+
0000F0r 2  E6 rr                        inc             ZP_WM_XAM + 1
0000F2r 2               :
0000F2r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_D_STATE      ; if disassembling, always print the address
0000F4r 2  D0 C9                        bne             @print_next_addr
0000F6r 2  A5 rr                        lda             ZP_WM_XAM       ; Check low-order 'examine index' byte
0000F8r 2  29 07                        and             #7              ; For MOD 8 = 0
0000FAr 2  F0 C3                        beq             @print_next_addr
0000FCr 2  80 D3                        bra             @print_data
0000FEr 2               
0000FEr 1               .include "mmu.s"
0000FEr 2               .debuginfo
0000FEr 2               
0000FEr 2               .segment "MMU"
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .struct     Address
000000r 2                           l           .byte
000000r 2                           h           .byte
000000r 2               .endstruct
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .struct     PageAddress
000000r 2                           page        .byte
000000r 2                           addr        .tag Address
000000r 2               .endstruct
000000r 2               
000000r 2               ; status byte =>
000000r 2               ; Bit 0: Allocated (1=Allocated, 0=Free)        Is the memory free or allocated
000000r 2               ; Bit 1: Paged (1=Paged, 0=Task)                Points to Paged RAM
000000r 2               ; Bit 2: Shared (1=Shared, 0=Task)              Points to Shared RAM when Shared == 1 && Paged == 1
000000r 2               ; Bit 3: Block (1=sz in Pages, 0=sz in Bytes)
000000r 2               ; Bit 4-6: RESERVED
000000r 2               ; Bit 7: IsValid (1=Yes, 0=NO)              Is this structure in use?
000000r 2               
000000r 2               AI_ALLOCATED = $01
000000r 2               AI_PAGED     = $02
000000r 2               AI_SHARED    = $04
000000r 2               AI_BLOCK     = $08
000000r 2               AI_VALID     = $80
000000r 2               
000000r 2               AI_DEFAULT   = AI_VALID
000000r 2               ALLOC_TASK   = AI_VALID | AI_ALLOCATED
000000r 2               ALLOC_PAGED  = ALLOC_TASK | AI_PAGED
000000r 2               ALLOC_SHARED = ALLOC_PAGED | AI_SHARED
000000r 2               
000000r 2               MSG_IN_BUFFER_BASE  = $8000
000000r 2               MSG_OUT_BUFFER_BASE = $9000
000000r 2               
000000r 2               MSG_BUFFER_BANK = $F0
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .struct     BlockAllocInfo
000000r 2                           status      .byte
000000r 2                           pages       .word                               ; 256-byte pages in this block
000000r 2                           next        .tag Address
000000r 2                           addr        .tag PageAddress
000000r 2               .endstruct
000000r 2               
000000r 2               PI_VALID = $01
000000r 2               
000000r 2               .struct     BlockInfo
000000r 2                           status      .byte
000000r 2               .endstruct
000000r 2               
000000r 2               MMU_INIT:
000000r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         TASK_RAM_INIT
000003r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         SHARED_RAM_INIT
000006r 2               
000006r 2               TASK_RAM_INIT:
000006r 2  AD F0 FF                 lda         T_REGISTER
000009r 2  C9 00                    cmp         #SYSTEM_TASK_NUM
00000Br 2  F0 03                    beq         :+
00000Dr 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         ERROR_NOT_SYSTEM_TASK
000010r 2               
000010r 2               :
000010r 2  DA                       phx
000011r 2  A2 0F                    ldx         #MAX_TASK_NUMBER
000013r 2               
000013r 2               @task_loop:
000013r 2  8E F0 FF                 stx         T_REGISTER
000016r 2               
000016r 2               ; Initialize the Task RAM for the current task
000016r 2                           ; start at $0400-$7FFF
000016r 2               
000016r 2               ; Initiazize the Paged RAM space for the current task
000016r 2               
000016r 2               ; Initialize Message buffer pointers
000016r 2  A0 0F                    ldy         #MAX_TASK_NUMBER
000018r 2               
000018r 2               :
000018r 2                           ;lda         ZP_MSG_INP,Y
000018r 2                           ;sta         ZP_MSG_OUTP,Y
000018r 2  88                       dey
000019r 2  10 FD                    bpl         :-
00001Br 2  CA                       dex
00001Cr 2  10 F5                    bpl         @task_loop
00001Er 2  80 05                    bra         :+
000020r 2  A9 00                    lda         #SYSTEM_TASK_NUM
000022r 2  8D F0 FF                 sta         T_REGISTER
000025r 2               
000025r 2               :
000025r 2  FA                       plx
000026r 2  60                       rts
000027r 2               
000027r 2               ; .X = To Task#, .A = Byte to write
000027r 2               MSG_SEND_BYTE:
000027r 2  78                       sei
000028r 2  5A                       phy                                             ; Save Y, RAM_BANK and U
000029r 2  A4 rr                    ldy         RAM_BANK_REG
00002Br 2  5A                       phy
00002Cr 2  AC F1 FF                 ldy         U_REGISTER
00002Fr 2  5A                       phy
000030r 2  48                       pha
000031r 2  AD F0 FF                 lda         T_REGISTER
000034r 2  0A 0A 0A 0A              SL_N        4
000038r 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_M_TEMP
00003Ar 2  05 rr                    ora         ZP_M_TEMP
00003Cr 2  AA                       tax
00003Dr 2                           ;lda
00003Dr 2  68                       pla
00003Er 2  8D F1 FF                 sta         U_REGISTER
000041r 2  68                       pla
000042r 2  85 rr                    sta         RAM_BANK_REG
000044r 2  7A                       ply
000045r 2  58                       cli
000046r 2  60                       rts
000047r 2               
000047r 2               .macro _M_ERROR_RETURN  errno
000047r 2                           lda         #errno
000047r 2                           sec
000047r 2                           rts
000047r 2               .endmacro
000047r 2               
000047r 2               ERROR_NOT_SYSTEM_TASK:
000047r 2  A9 01 38 60              _M_ERROR_RETURN     ERR_NOT_SYSTEM_TASK
00004Br 2               
00004Br 2               ERROR_OUT_OF_MEMORY:
00004Br 2  A9 02 38 60              _M_ERROR_RETURN     ERR_OUT_OF_MEMORY
00004Fr 2               
00004Fr 2               ; IN: .A.Y = Bytes to allocate
00004Fr 2               ; OUT (success): .A.Y = Address of allocation, C = 0
00004Fr 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERROR, C = 1
00004Fr 2               TASK_ALLOC:
00004Fr 2  C0 00                    cpy         #0                                  ; Is this a byte alloc or block alloc
000051r 2  F0 07                    beq         TASK_BYTE_ALLOC
000053r 2  C9 00                    cmp         #0                                  ; Is this a whole-block allocation?
000055r 2  F0 02                    beq         TASK_BLOCK_ALLOC
000057r 2  C8                       iny                                             ; Allocate one more page to cover the extra bytes
000058r 2  98                       tya                                             ; ...and then fall through to do a block allocation
000059r 2               
000059r 2               ; IN: .A = Pages to allocate for current task
000059r 2               ; OUT (success): .A.Y = Address of the allocation structure, C = 0
000059r 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERROR, C = 1
000059r 2               TASK_BLOCK_ALLOC:
000059r 2  60                       rts
00005Ar 2               
00005Ar 2               ; IN: .A = Bytes to allocate for current task
00005Ar 2               ; OUT (success): .A.Y = Address of the allocation structure, C = 0
00005Ar 2               ; OUT (failure): .A = ERROR, C = 1
00005Ar 2               TASK_BYTE_ALLOC:
00005Ar 2  60                       rts
00005Br 2               
00005Br 2               TASK_FREE:
00005Br 2  60                       rts
00005Cr 2               
00005Cr 2               ; IN: .A.Y = Bytes to allocate, T must be 0
00005Cr 2               ; OUT (success): .A.Y = Address of shared allocation structure, C = 0
00005Cr 2               ; OUT (out of memory): .A = ERR_OUT_OF_MEMORY, C = 1
00005Cr 2               ;           If T <> 0, .A = ERR_NOT_SYSTEM_TASK, C = 1
00005Cr 2               SHARED_ALLOC:
00005Cr 2  DA                       phx
00005Dr 2  AE F0 FF                 ldx         T_REGISTER
000060r 2  E0 00                    cpx         #SYSTEM_TASK_NUM
000062r 2  F0 04                    beq         :+
000064r 2  FA                       plx
000065r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         ERROR_NOT_SYSTEM_TASK
000068r 2               
000068r 2               :
000068r 2                                                                           ; Do the allocation
000068r 2               
000068r 2  FA                       plx
000069r 2  60                       rts
00006Ar 2               
00006Ar 2               ; IN: .A.Y = Address of PageAddress struct that points to location in shared memory to free
00006Ar 2               SHARED_FREE:
00006Ar 2  60                       rts
00006Br 2               
00006Br 2               SHARED_RAM_INIT:
00006Br 2  60                       rts
00006Cr 2               
00006Cr 2               ; IN: .X = Byte to write, .A.Y = Address of PageAddress struct that points to read location in shared memory
00006Cr 2               SHARED_WRITE:
00006Cr 2  78                       sei
00006Dr 2  DA                       phx                                             ; Push the byte to write
00006Er 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SHARED_RW_PAGE_SETUP                ; Clobbers .X.  After, .X = old RAM_BANK, .Y = old U
000071r 2  68                       pla                                             ; Get it back
000072r 2  92 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_SP1)                          ; Do the write
000074r 2  86 rr                    stx         RAM_BANK_REG                        ; Restore prior RAM_BANK
000076r 2  8C F1 FF                 sty         U_REGISTER                          ; Restore prior Shared RAM Macro-page
000079r 2  58                       cli
00007Ar 2  60                       rts
00007Br 2               
00007Br 2               ; IN: .A.Y = Address of PageAddress struct that points to shared memory location to write to
00007Br 2               ; OUT: .A = Byte read at address
00007Br 2               SHARED_READ:
00007Br 2  78                       sei
00007Cr 2  DA                       phx                                             ; Don't clobber .X
00007Dr 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         SHARED_RW_PAGE_SETUP                ; Clobbers .X.  After, .X = old RAM_BANK, .Y = old U
000080r 2  B2 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_SP1)                          ; Do the read
000082r 2  86 rr                    stx         RAM_BANK_REG                        ; Restore prior RAM_BANK
000084r 2  8C F1 FF                 sty         U_REGISTER                          ; Restore prior Shared RAM Macro-page
000087r 2  FA                       plx
000088r 2  58                       cli
000089r 2  60                       rts
00008Ar 2               
00008Ar 2               SHARED_RW_PAGE_SETUP:
00008Ar 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_SP1
00008Cr 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_M_SP1 + 1
00008Er 2  A4 01                    ldy         PageAddress::addr + Address::l
000090r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_SP1),Y                        ; Get the LOB of the address
000092r 2  AA                       tax
000093r 2  C8                       iny                                             ; Now the HOB
000094r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_SP1),Y
000096r 2  A8                       tay
000097r 2  B2 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_SP1)                          ; Load the page
000099r 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_M_SP1
00009Br 2  84 rr                    sty         ZP_M_SP1 + 1
00009Dr 2  A8                       tay                                             ; Save the page for use later
00009Er 2  09 F0                    ora         #$F0                                ; Shared Bank transform
0000A0r 2  A6 rr                    ldx         RAM_BANK_REG                        ; Save bank for restore
0000A2r 2  85 rr                    sta         RAM_BANK_REG                        ; Update the RAM_BANK to new Shared bank
0000A4r 2  98                       tya                                             ; Get the original page back
0000A5r 2  4A                       lsr                                             ; Shift right 4-bits
0000A6r 2  4A                       lsr
0000A7r 2  4A                       lsr
0000A8r 2  4A                       lsr
0000A9r 2  AC F1 FF                 ldy         U_REGISTER                          ; Save macro-page for restore
0000ACr 2  8D F1 FF                 sta         U_REGISTER                          ; Update macro-page[0..3] from PageAddress::page[4..7]
0000AFr 2  60                       rts
0000B0r 2               
0000B0r 2               ; Memory Copy
0000B0r 2               ; ZP_TEMP_VEC: From, ZP_TEMP_VEC2: To, .A.Y: Size
0000B0r 2               MEM_COPY:
0000B0r 2  DA                       phx
0000B1r 2  AA                       tax
0000B2r 2  D0 03                    bne         :+
0000B4r 2  98                       tya
0000B5r 2  F0 3F                    beq         @done                               ; copy zero bytes?  Done!
0000B7r 2               :
0000B7r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF
0000BAr 2  A5 rr 20 rr              PRINT_BYTE  ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
0000BEr 2  rr           
0000BFr 2  A5 rr 20 rr              PRINT_BYTE  ZP_TEMP_VEC
0000C3r 2  rr           
0000C4r 2  A9 2D 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #ASCII_DASH, #ASCII_GT
0000C8r 2  rr A9 3E 20  
0000CCr 2  rr rr        
0000CEr 2  A5 rr 20 rr              PRINT_BYTE  ZP_TEMP_VEC2 + 1
0000D2r 2  rr           
0000D3r 2  A5 rr 20 rr              PRINT_BYTE  ZP_TEMP_VEC2
0000D7r 2  rr           
0000D8r 2               
0000D8r 2               @loop:
0000D8r 2  B2 rr                    lda         (ZP_TEMP_VEC)
0000DAr 2  92 rr                    sta         (ZP_TEMP_VEC2)
0000DCr 2  A9 2E 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #ASCII_PERIOD
0000E0r 2  rr           
0000E1r 2  CA                       dex
0000E2r 2  D0 04                    bne         :+
0000E4r 2  98                       tya
0000E5r 2  F0 0F                    beq         @done
0000E7r 2  88                       dey
0000E8r 2               :

0000EAr 2  D0 02                    bne         :+
0000ECr 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
0000EEr 2               :

0000F0r 2  D0 02                    bne         :+

0000F6r 2  FA                       plx
0000F7r 2  60                       rts
0000F8r 2               
0000F8r 2               ; TESTS
0000F8r 2               MEM_TEST:

0000FBr 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF
0000FEr 2                           ;stz         T_REGISTER
0000FEr 2               
0000FEr 2               @task_num_loop:
0000FEr 2  64 rr                    stz         RAM_BANK_REG
000100r 2  A9 04                    lda         #$04
000102r 2  A2 80                    ldx         #$80                                ; exclude the task serial buffers @ $0200 && $0300
000104r 2  20 rr rr                 jsr         TEST_PAGE_RANGE
000107r 2  A2 A0                    ldx         #$A0                                ; end of banked RAM
000109r 2

00010Dr 2  rr

00015Cr 2  rr

000167r 2  A9 FF 20 rr              PRINT_BYTE  #$FF
00016Br 2  rr

000170r 2  rr           
000171r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_TEMP_VEC
000173r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_TEMP
000175r 2               
000175r 2               @loop_init:
000175r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0
000177r 2               
000177r 2               @loop:
000177r 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_TEMP_VEC),Y                     ; save what is in memory (non-descructive)
000179r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_SV
00017Br 2  A9 AA                    lda         #$AA
00017Dr 2               @test_it:
00017Dr 2  49 FF                    eor         #$FF
00017Fr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_TEMP_VEC),Y
000181r 2  D1 rr                    cmp         (ZP_TEMP_VEC),Y
000183r 2  F0 04                    beq         @next
000185r 2  A9 21                    lda         #ASCII_BANG
000187r 2  80 0D                    bra         @write                              ; always, no need to restore value since it isn't storing properly anyway
000189r 2               
000189r 2               @next:
000189r 2  C9 AA                    cmp         #$AA
00018Br 2  D0 F0                    bne         @test_it
00018Dr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_SV                             ; restore saved value
00018Fr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_TEMP_VEC),Y
000191r 2  C8                       iny
000192r 2  D0 E3                    bne         @loop
000194r 2  A9 2E                    lda         #ASCII_PERIOD
000196r 2               
000196r 2               @write:
000196r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CHAR
000199r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_TEMP
00019Br 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
00019Dr 2  E4 rr                    cpx         ZP_TEMP_VEC + 1
00019Fr 2  D0 D4                    bne         @loop_init
0001A1r 2  4C rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF_JMP
0001A4r 2               
0001A4r 2               ; test memory pages, start page in .A, end page in .X
0001A4r 2               DEEP_PAGE_TEST_RANGE_AX:
0001A4r 2  86 rr                    stx         ZP_M_SP2_H
0001A6r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_SP1_H
0001A8r 2  80 06                    bra         DEEP_PAGE_TEST_RANGE
0001AAr 2               
0001AAr 2               ; test a single page in .A
0001AAr 2               DEEP_PAGE_TEST_A:
0001AAr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_SP1_H
0001ACr 2               
0001ACr 2               ; test a single page in ZP_M_SP1_H
0001ACr 2               DEEP_PAGE_TEST:
0001ACr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_SP1_H
0001AEr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_SP2_H
0001B0r 2               
0001B0r 2               ; test a range of pages, start in ZP_M_SP1_H, end in ZP_M_SP2_H
0001B0r 2               DEEP_PAGE_TEST_RANGE:
0001B0r 2  DA 5A                    PUSH_XY
0001B2r 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_M_SP1_L
0001B4r 2               @next_page:
0001B4r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_SP1_H
0001B6r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_BYTE
0001B9r 2  A9 00 20 rr              PRINT_BYTE  #0
0001BDr 2  rr           
0001BEr 2  A9 3A 20 rr              PRINT_CHAR  #ASCII_COLON
0001C2r 2  rr           
0001C3r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF
0001C6r 2  A0 00                    ldy         #0
0001C8r 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0
0001CAr 2               @next_cell:
0001CAr 2  64 rr                    stz         ZP_M_TEMP
0001CCr 2  B1 rr                    lda         (ZP_M_SP1),Y
0001CEr 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_SV                             ; save the old value
0001D0r 2  A9 01                    lda         #1
0001D2r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_TEMP2
0001D4r 2               @loop_start:
0001D4r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_TEMP2
0001D6r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_SP1),Y                     ; store it
0001D8r 2  D1 rr                    cmp         (ZP_M_SP1),Y                     ; test it
0001DAr 2  D0 08                    bne         :+
0001DCr 2  49 FF                    eor         #$FF                                ; test the inverse
0001DEr 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_SP1),Y                     ; store it
0001E0r 2  D1 rr                    cmp         (ZP_M_SP1),Y                     ; test it
0001E2r 2  F0 06                    beq         @shift
0001E4r 2               :
0001E4r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_TEMP2
0001E6r 2  05 rr                    ora         ZP_M_TEMP                           ; add bit to set of bad bits found
0001E8r 2  85 rr                    sta         ZP_M_TEMP
0001EAr 2               @shift:
0001EAr 2  06 rr                    asl         ZP_M_TEMP2                          ; Walk the bit forward
0001ECr 2  D0 E6                    bne         @loop_start
0001EEr 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_TEMP                           ; get the set of bad bits we found
0001F0r 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_BYTE
0001F3r 2  E8                       inx
0001F4r 2  E0 10                    cpx         #$10
0001F6r 2  F0 07                    beq         :+
0001F8r 2  A9 20 20 rr              PRINT_SPACE
0001FCr 2  rr           
0001FDr 2  80 05                    bra         :++
0001FFr 2               :
0001FFr 2  20 rr rr                 PRINT_CRLF
000202r 2  A2 00                    ldx         #0
000204r 2               :
000204r 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_SV
000206r 2  91 rr                    sta         (ZP_M_SP1),Y                     ; restore the old value
000208r 2  C8                       iny
000209r 2  D0 BF                    bne         @next_cell
00020Br 2  A5 rr                    lda         ZP_M_SP1_H
00020Dr 2  C5 rr                    cmp         ZP_M_SP2_H
00020Fr 2  F0 05                    beq         @done
000211r 2  E6 rr                    inc         ZP_M_SP1_H
000213r 2  4C rr rr                 jmp         @next_page
000216r 2               @done:
000216r 2  7A FA                    PULL_YX
000218r 2  60                       rts
000219r 2

00009Br 2                               ; Set basic FM patch (simple sine-like sound)

0000A2r 2

0000D7r 2

0000D9r 2  A9 00                        lda         #$00    ; Max Volume
0000DBr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
0000DEr 2               
0000DEr 2  A2 61                        ldx         #$61    ; Total Level (volume) for operator 1, Channel 1
0000E0r 2  A9 00                        lda         #$00    ; Max Volume
0000E2r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
0000E5r 2               
0000E5r 2  A9 rr                        lda         #<SCOM_NOTES_0
0000E7r 2  85 A0                        sta         YMN0L
0000E9r 2  A9 rr                        lda         #>SCOM_NOTES_0

0000EDr 2               
0000EDr 2  A9 rr                        lda         #<SCOM_NOTES_1

0000F1r 2  A9 rr                        lda         #>SCOM_NOTES_1
0000F3r 2  85 A3                        sta         YMN1H
0000F5r 2               
0000F5r 2  A0 00                        ldy         #0          ; counter for channel 0
0000F7r 2               @play_loop:
0000F7r 2                               ; 0. set outer counter to 0
0000F7r 2                               ; 1. key off channel 0
0000F7r 2                               ; 1a.  key off on channel 1
0000F7r 2                               ; 2. set note to next note in channel 0 list
0000F7r 2                               ; 3. key on on channel 0
0000F7r 2                               ; 4. one delay
0000F7r 2                               ; 5. notes for rest of sequence on channel 1
0000F7r 2                               ; 5a.  reset counter to 0
0000F7r 2                               ; 5b.  key off on channel 1
0000F7r 2                               ; 5c.  set note on channel 1
0000F7r 2                               ; 5d.  key on on channel 1
0000F7r 2                               ; 5e.  one delay
0000F7r 2                               ; 5f.  increment counter
0000F7r 2                               ; 5g.  loop until 7 notes have been played on channel 1
0000F7r 2                               ; 6.  increment outer counter to play next start note on channel 0
0000F7r 2                               ; 7.  loop back to 1 until outer count reaches 8 (8 sequences have played)
0000F7r 2                               ; Key Off (silence previous note)
0000F7r 2               
0000F7r 2  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
0000F9r 2  A9 00                        lda         #$00    ; CH0 off
0000FBr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
0000FEr 2               
0000FEr 2  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
000100r 2  A9 01                        lda         #$01    ; CH1 off
000102r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000105r 2               
000105r 2                               ; Set frequency (KC)
000105r 2  A2 28                        ldx         #$28    ; KC register for CH0
000107r 2  B1 A0                        lda         (YMN0L),y
000109r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
00010Cr 2               
00010Cr 2                               ; Key On
00010Cr 2  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
00010Er 2  A9 78                        lda         #$78    ; CH0 on
000110r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000113r 2               
000113r 2                               ; Delay 8 64th notes in length
000113r 2  A9 08                        lda         #8
000115r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_DELAY_64
000118r 2               
000118r 2  5A                           phy
000119r 2  A0 00                        ldy         #0
00011Br 2  80 07                        bra         @skip_ch1_off   ; already off at the start of the loop
00011Dr 2               
00011Dr 2               @next_ch1:
00011Dr 2  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
00011Fr 2  A9 01                        lda         #$01    ; CH1 off
000121r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000124r 2               
000124r 2               @skip_ch1_off:
000124r 2                               ; Set frequency (KC)
000124r 2  A2 29                        ldx         #$29    ; KC register for CH1
000126r 2  B1 A2                        lda         (YMN1L),y
000128r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
00012Br 2               
00012Br 2                               ; Key On
00012Br 2  A2 08                        ldx         #$08    ; Key On/Off register
00012Dr 2  A9 79                        lda         #$79    ; CH1 on
00012Fr 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000132r 2               
000132r 2                               ; Delay 8 64th notes in length
000132r 2  A9 08                        lda         #8
000134r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_DELAY_64
000137r 2               
000137r 2  C8                           iny
000138r 2  C0 07                        cpy         #7
00013Ar 2  D0 E1                        bne         @next_ch1
00013Cr 2               
00013Cr 2                               ; Next note on CH0
00013Cr 2  7A                           ply                 ; pull CH0 count
00013Dr 2  C8                           iny
00013Er 2  C0 08                        cpy         #8      ; 8 bars
000140r 2  D0 B5                        bne         @play_loop
000142r 2               
000142r 2                               ; Stop sound
000142r 2  A2 08                        ldx         #$08
000144r 2  A9 00                        lda         #$00
000146r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr         YM_WRITE
000149r 2               
000149r 2  A2 08                        ldx         #$08
00014Br 2  A9 01                        lda         #$01
00014Dr 2  4C rr rr                     jmp         YM_WRITE
000150r 2               
000150r 2               YM_TIMEOUT = 64
000150r 2               
000150r 2               ; Write value in A to YM-2151 register in X
000150r 2               YM_WRITE:
000150r 2  78                           sei
000151r 2  5A                           phy
000152r 2  A0 40                        ldy         #YM_TIMEOUT
000154r 2               
000154r 2               @ym_wait1:
000154r 2  88                           dey
000155r 2  30 18                        bmi         @timeout
000157r 2  2C rr rr                     bit         YM_DATA
00015Ar 2  30 F8                        bmi         @ym_wait1
00015Cr 2  8E rr rr                     stx         YM_REG
00015Fr 2  A0 40                        ldy         #YM_TIMEOUT
000161r 2               
000161r 2               @ym_wait2:
000161r 2  88                           dey
000162r 2  30 0B                        bmi         @timeout
000164r 2  2C rr rr                     bit         YM_DATA
000167r 2  30 F8                        bmi         @ym_wait2
000169r 2  8D rr rr                     sta         YM_DATA
00016Cr 2  18                           clc
00016Dr 2  80 01                        bra         @cleanup
00016Fr 2               
00016Fr 2               @timeout:
00016Fr 2  38                           sec
000170r 2               
000170r 2               @cleanup:
000170r 2  7A                           ply
000171r 2  58                           cli
000172r 2  60                           rts
000173r 2               
000173r 2               YM_LOADPATCH:
000173r 2                               ; Make re-entrant safe by protecting tmp and pointer variables from interrupt
000173r 2  08                           php
000174r 2  78                           sei
000175r 2               
000175r 2                               ; and #$07 ; mask channel to range 0..7
000175r 2  86 B0                        stx AZP0L
000177r 2  84 B1                        sty AZP0H
000179r 2  18                           clc
00017Ar 2  69 20                        adc #$20 ; first byte of patch goes to YM:$20+channel
00017Cr 2  AA                           tax
00017Dr 2               
00017Dr 2  B2 B0                        lda (AZP0L)
00017Fr 2  29 3F                        and #$3F
000181r 2               
000181r 2                               ;sta ymtmp1
000181r 2                               ;lda ymshadow,x
000181r 2                               ;and #$C0 ; L+R bits for YM channel
000181r 2                               ;ora ymtmp1 ; Add the patch byte without L+R
000181r 2  09 C0                        ora #$C0;
000183r 2               
000183r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr YM_WRITE
000186r 2  B0 16                        bcs @fail
000188r 2  A0 00                        ldy #0
00018Ar 2  8A                           txa         ; YM_WRITE preserves X (YM register)
00018Br 2                               ; Now skip over $28 and $30 by adding $10 to the register address.
00018Br 2                               ; C guaranteed clear by successful ym_write
00018Br 2  69 10                        adc #$10
00018Dr 2  AA                           tax         ; set up for loop
00018Er 2               @next:
00018Er 2  8A                           txa
00018Fr 2                               ; C guaranteed clear by successful YM_WRITE
00018Fr 2  69 08                        adc #$08
000191r 2  B0 0E                        bcs @success
000193r 2  C8                           iny
000194r 2  AA                           tax
000195r 2  B1 B0                        lda (AZP0L),y
000197r 2  5A                           phy         ; YM_WRITE clobbers .Y
000198r 2  20 rr rr                     jsr YM_WRITE
00019Br 2  7A                           ply
00019Cr 2  90 F0                        bcc @next
00019Er 2               @fail:
00019Er 2  28                           plp         ; restore interrupt flag
00019Fr 2  38                           sec
0001A0r 2  60                           rts         ; return C set as failed patch write.
0001A1r 2               @success:
0001A1r 2  28                           plp         ; restore interrupt flag
0001A2r 2  18                           clc
0001A3r 2  60                           rts
0001A4r 2               
0001A4r 2               ; YM_DELAY_64 - Delay subroutine for musical note lengths
0001A4r 2               ; A: number of 64th-note delays to wait (a 64th note @120 BPM = ~ 111,861 clock cycles @ 3.57955 MHz)
0001A4r 2               YM_DELAY_64:
0001A4r 2  DA 5A                        PUSH_XY
0001A6r 2  85 B2                        sta         YMTMP1
0001A8r 2               
0001A8r 2               @far_outer:
0001A8r 2  A9 01                        lda         #1          ; 2 cycles
0001AAr 2  A2 4B                        ldx         #75
0001ACr 2  A0 8E                        ldy         #142
0001AEr 2  80 04                        bra         @inner
0001B0r 2               
0001B0r 2               @outer:
0001B0r 2  A2 00                        ldx         #0          ; 2 cycles
0001B2r 2               
0001B2r 2               @mid:
0001B2r 2  A0 00                        ldy         #0          ; 2 cycles
0001B4r 2               
0001B4r 2               @inner:
0001B4r 2  88                           dey                     ; 2 cycles
0001B5r 2  D0 FD                        bne         @inner      ; 3 cycles when branching, 2 when not
0001B7r 2  CA                           dex                     ; 2 cycles
0001B8r 2  D0 F8                        bne         @mid        ; 3 cycles when branching, 2 when not
0001BAr 2               
0001BAr 2                               ; Fine-tuning at end of inner loop
0001BAr 2  3A                           dec                     ; 2 cycles
0001BBr 2  D0 F3                        bne         @outer      ; 3 cycles when branching, 2 when not
0001BDr 2  C6 B2                        dec         YMTMP1      ; 3 cycles
0001BFr 2  D0 E7                        bne         @far_outer  ; 3 cycles when branching, 2 when not
0001C1r 2  7A FA                        PULL_YX
0001C3r 2  60                           rts                     ; 6 cycles
0001C4r 2
